<script>
// TELEGRAM ICHKI BRAUZERIDAN CHIQISH: bot havolani oddiy URL qilib yuboradi, Telegram
// mobil'da uni O'Z ichki brauzerida ochadi (Bot API'da buni taqiqlash parametri YO'Q).
// Sahifa Telegram webview'ida ochilganini aniqlasa, o'zini TIZIM brauzeriga ko'chiradi:
// Android -> intent:// (standart VIEW action), iOS -> x-safari-https:// (Safari'ni ochadi).
// Muvaffaqiyatsiz bo'lsa hech narsa buzilmaydi — sahifa ichki brauzerda ishlashda davom etadi.
function _tgExtUrl(){
  var ua=navigator.userAgent||'';
  var tg=!!(window.TelegramWebview||window.TelegramWebviewProxy)||/Telegram/i.test(ua);
  if(!tg)return null;
  var rest=location.host+location.pathname+location.search+location.hash;
  if(/android/i.test(ua))return 'intent://'+rest+'#Intent;scheme=https;action=android.intent.action.VIEW;end';
  if(/iphone|ipad|ipod/i.test(ua))return 'x-safari-https://'+rest;
  return null;   // Telegram Desktop havolani baribir tashqi brauzerda ochadi
}
(function(){try{
  if(sessionStorage.getItem('_tgOut'))return;   // bitta urinish (qaytish loopi bo'lmasin)
  var u=_tgExtUrl();
  if(u){sessionStorage.setItem('_tgOut','1');location.href=u;}
}catch(e){}})();
</script>

<div id="splash">
  <div class="sp-box">
    <img id="spLogo" alt="">

<script>
/* Splash logosi DARROV qo'yiladi (API javobini kutmasdan): /{brend}/logo — brendda logo
   bo'lmasa 404 qaytadi va DetalQR ikonkasiga tushamiz. Shu sabab ekran hech qachon
   bo'sh turmaydi. Skrinshot rejimida (?shot=1) splash umuman ko'rsatilmaydi. */
(function(){
  try{
    if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('shot')){
      var sp=document.getElementById('splash'); if(sp)sp.remove(); return;
    }
    var seg=location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    /* BREND SAHIFASI (/slug — model yo'q, skaner ekrani): zagruzka oynasi KERAK EMAS
       (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-26). Model sahifasi (/slug/m/artikul) da qoladi. */
    if(seg[1]!=='m'){
      var sp2=document.getElementById('splash'); if(sp2)sp2.remove(); return;
    }
    var slug=decodeURIComponent(seg[0]||'');
    var img=document.getElementById('spLogo');
    img.onerror=function(){ img.onerror=null; img.src='/icon-512.png'; };
    img.src = slug ? ('/'+encodeURIComponent(slug)+'/logo') : '/icon-512.png';
    /* Splash EKRANDA ko'rina boshlagan payt (navigatsiya emas, BIRINCHI KADR) — undan
       kamida 3 s o'tmaguncha splash yopilmaydi (pastdagi splashHide). */
    window.__spT0 = performance.now();
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){ requestAnimationFrame(function(){
      window.__spT0 = performance.now(); }); });
  }catch(e){}
})();
</script>

<script>
function loadScript(srcs, cb, fail){
  if(!srcs.length){ if(fail)fail(); return; }
  const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=srcs[0];
  s.onload=cb; s.onerror=()=>loadScript(srcs.slice(1),cb,fail);
  document.head.appendChild(s);
}
loadScript([
  'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js',
  'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js',
  'https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js'
], main, ()=>{document.getElementById('err').style.display='flex';
   document.getElementById('err').textContent='3D kutubxona yuklanmadi. Internetni tekshiring.';
   try{splashHide();}catch(e){}});

const APP_VERSION='v0.3 · build 57';

function esc(s){return (s==null?'':''+s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
// UTOLSHENIYE (облицовка пласти / склейка) — 2026-09-03, foydalanuvchi qarori:
// detal BITTA bo'lib ko'rinadi (kromka ham bir butun, o'rtada chok yo'q), ma'lumot
// oynasi to'lib ketmasin — faqat kichik BELGI turadi, tarkibi esa spetsifikatsiyada.
function utolParts(p){
  const ls=(p&&p.plastics)||[]; if(!ls.length)return null;
  const b=Math.abs(p.base_thick||0)||null;
  const a=[]; if(b)a.push(b);
  for(const l of ls){ const t=Math.abs((l&&l.thick)||0); if(t)a.push(t); }
  return a.length>1?a:null;
}
function utolBadge(p){
  const a=utolParts(p); if(!a)return '';
  return ' <span class="utol" title="'+esc(t('utolTip'))+': '+a.join(' + ')+' mm">\u2261</span>';
}

function t(k){return (T2[LANG]&&T2[LANG][k])||T2.uz[k]||k;}
function tp(k,o){let s=t(k);for(const p in (o||{}))s=s.split('{'+p+'}').join(o[p]);return s;}

// svg+matn tugmasidagi oxirgi matn tugunini almashtiradi (svg saqlanadi)
function setLbl(id,txt){
  const e=document.getElementById(id);if(!e)return;
  for(let n=e.lastChild;n;n=n.previousSibling){
    if(n.nodeType===3&&n.textContent.trim()){n.textContent=' '+txt;return;}
  }
  e.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '+txt));
}

// 2026-08-31 (foydalanuvchi): AVTOMATIK aylanish o'rniga ANIQ tanlov — ro'yxat ochiladi,
// foydalanuvchi o'zi bosgan til DARHOL saqlanadi (endi brend standartidan USTUN turadi).
function openLangPicker(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#langbox button[data-l]').forEach(b=>
    b.classList.toggle('on', b.getAttribute('data-l')===LANG));
  document.getElementById('langwrap').style.display='flex';
}
function closeLangPicker(){ document.getElementById('langwrap').style.display='none'; }
function pickLang(code){
  if(!LANGS.includes(code)) return;
  LANG=code; LANG_EXPLICIT=true;
  try{ localStorage.setItem('lang',LANG); }catch(e){}   // Safari private rejimida ham yiqilmasin
  location.reload();   // butun UI yangi tilda qayta chiziladi
}

const seg=location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
// 👁 MIJOZ REJIMI: /k/<token> sahifasida server `window.__CLIENT` ni joylaydi.
// Bunda yo'lda brend slug'i ham, artikul ham YO'Q — ma'lumot alohida (qirqilgan)
// API'dan olinadi va sahifa faqat KO'RISH uchun ishlaydi.
const CLIENT=(typeof window!=='undefined'&&window.__CLIENT)?window.__CLIENT:null;
const SLUG=CLIENT?'':decodeURIComponent(seg[0]||'');     // /{brend}/  yoki  /{brend}/m/{artikul}[/{des}]
// KROMKA (torec) yangi ko'rinishi (har tomon o'z rangi + 3D polosa) — HOZIRCHA faqat Sirojiddin
// brendida sinaladi; boshqa brendlarда eski ko'rinish (2D palitra, 3D polosasiz) qoladi.
// KROMKA ko'rinishi (torec = panelning yon yuzasi, kromka teksturasi/rangi o'sha
// yuzada) — 2026-08-06 dan HAMMA BRENDGA yoqilgan (avval sinov uchun bitta brendda
// edi). Skript orqali yuklangan modelda kromka Bazisdagi rasm/rang bilan chiziladi;
// ma'lumot bo'lmasa (bot orqali .b3d) torec panel materialida qoladi.
const KROMKA_NEW = true;
// /{slug}/m/{art}  → seg=[slug,'m',art];   /{slug}/  → seg=[slug]
let ARTICLE=CLIENT?'client':((seg[1]==='m'||seg[1]==='p')?decodeURIComponent(seg[2]||''):'');
const FIRST_TARGET=seg[3]?decodeURIComponent(seg[3]):null;
// SKRINSHOT rejimi (?shot=1): serverda (headless brauzer) modelning toza rasmini olish uchun —
// UI yashiriladi, razmer/furnitura belgilari chizilmaydi, tayyor bo'lganda window.__shotReady=true.
const SHOT=new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('shot');

const COLORS={};
// Dekor nomiga qarab rang (LDSP/ЛДСП uchun keng ro'yxat). Tartib muhim — avval mos kelgani.
const MAT_KEYWORDS=[
  [['бел','white','снеж','альпийск'],0xe9ebed],
  [['чёрн','черн','black','графит','антрацит'],0x30343a],
  [['венге'],0x3a2e29],
  [['кашт','капп','capp'],0x6f5645],
  [['орех','walnut'],0x7c5c3c],
  [['дуб','сонома','oak','крафт'],0xc6a675],
  [['ясень','ash'],0xccba99],
  [['бук','beech'],0xd6b588],
  [['клен','клён','maple'],0xdfc89f],
  [['вишн','cherry','ольха'],0x8c4a3a],
  [['серы','сер.','grey','gray','бетон','камень'],0x9aa0a6],
  [['дым'],0x888d94],
  [['золот','gold','песоч'],0xc9b27a],
  [['хдф','hdf','апельсин','крем','беж','ваниль','слон'],0xe6dcc6],
];
function keywordColor(name){
  const ln=(name||'').toLowerCase();
  for(const k of MAT_KEYWORDS){if(k[0].some(s=>ln.includes(s)))return k[1];}
  return null;
}
function hsl2hex(h,s,l){   // h,s,l: 0..1
  const f=n=>{const k=(n+h*12)%12;const a=s*Math.min(l,1-l);return l-a*Math.max(-1,Math.min(k-3,9-k,1));};
  const to=x=>Math.round(x*255);
  return (to(f(0))<<16)|(to(f(8))<<8)|to(f(4));
}
function hashHue(name){let h=0;for(const ch of (name||''))h=(h*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;return (h%360)/360;}
function tweak(hex,n){     // bir xil turkumdagi materiallarni yorqinlik bilan ajratish
  const r=(hex>>16)&255,g=(hex>>8)&255,b=hex&255;
  const f=(n%2)? 1+0.13*Math.ceil(n/2) : 1-0.11*Math.ceil(n/2);
  const c=x=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(x*f)));
  return (c(r)<<16)|(c(g)<<8)|c(b);
}
// Modeldagi har bir noyob materialga ALOHIDA rang biriktiramiz (bir-biridan ajralsin).
function assignMaterialColors(panels){
  const names=[...new Set(panels.map(p=>(p.mat||'')))];
  const kwSeen={};
  for(const name of names){
    if(COLORS[name]!==undefined)continue;
    const kw=keywordColor(name);
    if(kw!==null){
      const n=(kwSeen[kw]||0);kwSeen[kw]=n+1;
      COLORS[name]=n===0?kw:tweak(kw,n);   // bir xil dekor (masalan 2 xil oq) yorqinlik bilan farqlanadi
    }else{
      COLORS[name]=hsl2hex(hashHue(name),0.30,0.64);   // dekor nomidan barqaror, farqli rang
    }
  }
}
function matColor(name){
  if(COLORS[name]===undefined){
    const kw=keywordColor(name);
    COLORS[name]=(kw!==null)?kw:hsl2hex(hashHue(name),0.30,0.64);
  }
  return COLORS[name];
}
// Kromka (torec) rangi: HAR TOMON o'z material rangi bilan (avval palitradan/dominantdan edi).
//   b.col   -> skript/extract yuborgan HAQIQIY rang; b.same (Узидан) -> panel materiali rangi;
//   b.mat   -> kromka material NOMIdan barqaror rang (matColor). Yo'q -> panel rangi.
function _colCss(c){ return (typeof c==='number') ? '#'+('000000'+((c>>>0)&0xffffff).toString(16)).slice(-6) : String(c); }
function buttColorCss(p,b){
  // 1) skript/extract REAL kromka rangini yuborgan bo'lsa — AYNAN o'shani ishlatamiz
  if(b&&b.col){ const s=String(b.col); return s.charAt(0)==='#'?s:('#'+s); }
  // 2) kromka teksturasi/rangi YUKLANMAGAN -> DETAL (panel) materialining rangidan
  //    (kromka material NOMIdan taxminiy rang OLINMAYDI — noto'g'ri chiqishi mumkin)
  if(p&&p.col){ const s=String(p.col); return s.charAt(0)==='#'?s:('#'+s); }   // real-tex panel palitra rangi
  return _colCss(matColor((p&&p.mat)||''));                                     // aks holda panel material rangi
}
// KROMKA = PANELNING YON YUZASI (2026-08-05). Detalning 6 yuzasidan 2 tasi asosiy yuza,
// qolgan 4 tasi (torec) — kromkaga tegishli. Kromka endi alohida "polosa" bo'lib emas,
// AYNAN o'sha yon yuzalar bo'yalib ko'rsatiladi:
//   • kromka urilmagan tomon  -> panel materiali (ko'zga tashlanmaydi, ajralib turmaydi);
//   • kromka urilgan tomon    -> kromkaning HAQIQIY rangi/teksturasi (skript yuborgan bo'lsa).
// Ma'lumot yo'q bo'lsa (bot orqali .b3d yuklangan model) — hamma yuza panel materialida,
// kromka nomi faqat 2D chizmada ko'rsatiladi.
// b.col/b.tex = skript yuborgan real rang/tekstura; p.edge.col = eski skript (dominant kromka).
// TINT — Bazisdagi HAQIQIY material rangi, lekin tekstura RASMI kelmagan holat uchun:
// katalog/protsedural naqsh shu rangga bo'yaladi. Rangni to'g'ridan ishlatsak tekstura
// qorayib ketadi (map × color), shuning uchun yorqinligini 1 ga normallaymiz — naqsh
// saqlanib, TUSI Bazisdagidek bo'ladi.
// sRGB chiqishда HEX rang (Bazis qiymati) aynan o'zi ko'rinishi uchun lineynыy fazoga
// o'giriladi. Oq (0xffffff — tekstura ustidagi rang) o'zgarmaydi, shuning uchun xavfsiz.
function _sRGBFix(m){
  if(!m||!m.color||THREE.sRGBEncoding===undefined)return m;
  if(m.color.convertSRGBToLinear)m.color.convertSRGBToLinear();
  return m;
}
// Bazis material/kromkaga RANG TANLANMAGANDA 0x00FF00 (neon yashil) yoki 0xFF00FF
// qaytaradi — bu HAQIQIY rang emas, «bo'sh» belgisi. Jonli bazada 142 panel va 4 115
// kromka shu qiymat bilan yotibdi (80 model) va ular kislotali yashil bo'lib chizilardi.
// Bunday qiymatni RANG YO'Q deb qabul qilamiz — material NOMIdan rang olinadi.
function _emptyCol(c){
  const s=String(c==null?'':c).toLowerCase().replace('#','').trim();
  return s==='00ff00'||s==='ff00ff';
}
function tintHex(c){
  if(_emptyCol(c)) return null;
  // Tint — tekstura ustiga KO'PAYTIRILADIGAN rang (Bazis material rangi).
  // AVVAL: eng yorqin kanal 255 ga "cho'zilardi" (k=255/mx). Bu nisbatni saqlagani bilan
  // TO'QLIKNI butunlay yo'q qilardi va natijalar noto'g'ri chiqardi (jonli o'lchov):
  //   #333300 antratsit LMDF -> #ffff00 SOF SARIQ (50 panel)
  //   #000d00 napravlyayushchiy -> #00ff00 NEON YASHIL (18 panel)
  //   #151515 "Стекло черный"  -> #ffffff OQ (12 panel)
  //   #7e7e7e / #808080 / #aeaeae kulranglar -> hammasi OQ (400+ panel)
  //   #000000 -> 0 qaytardi, JS'da falsy => `||0xffffff` bilan OQqa aylanardi (29 panel)
  // ENDI: rang O'Z holicha qoladi; faqat JUDA to'q tint biroz yoritiladi (aks holda
  // tekstura naqshi qorayib ko'rinmay qoladi). Deyarli qora tint esa BETARAF to'q
  // kulrangga o'tadi — bunday qiymatlarda rang tusi (hue) beqaror bo'ladi.
  if(!c) return null;
  const h=String(c).replace('#','');
  let r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16), g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16), b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);
  if(isNaN(r)||isNaN(g)||isNaN(b)) return null;
  const mx=Math.max(r,g,b);
  if(mx<24) return 0x2a2a2a;                 // deyarli qora -> betaraf to'q kulrang
  const MIN=110;                             // eng yorqin kanal uchun quyi chegara
  if(mx<MIN){ const k=MIN/mx;
    r=Math.round(r*k); g=Math.round(g*k); b=Math.round(b*k); }
  return (Math.min(255,r)<<16)|(Math.min(255,g)<<8)|Math.min(255,b);
}
// «Kromka panelning O'ZIDAN» — ya'ni kromka lentasi panel bilan AYNAN bir xil dekor.
// Ustalar buni juda har xil yozadi (o'zbekcha/ruscha, lotin/kirill, imlo xatolari bilan).
const _EDGE_SAME=/узидан|уз[иы]дан|uzidan|o.?zidan|ozidan|uxzini|o.?zi(ga|ni|dan)?\b|в\s*цвет|подбор|под\s*цвет|в\s*тон|мос\b|same/i;
// KROMKA RANGI material NOMIdan (skript rang/tekstura yubormagan hollar uchun).
// MUAMMO (2026-08-31, davr-mebel): jonli bazadagi 562 166 kromkadan 430 902 tasida
// (77%, 2 956 model) na `col`, na `tex` bor — chunki .b3d/bot yo'li ularni yubormaydi.
// Avval bunday kromka BUTUNLAY e'tiborsiz qolardi (`edgeKeyOfButt` null qaytarardi ->
// `splitSidesByEdge` umuman guruh yasamasdi) va torec panel materialida chizilardi.
// Natijada masalan OCH KASHEMIR panelning VENGE (to'q) kromkasi ko'rinmay ketardi.
// Endi nomdan rang chiqarishga urinamiz. QAT'IY qoida — RANG O'YLAB TOPILMAYDI:
//   • nomda «узидан / o'zidan / в цвет» bo'lsa  -> panel materiali (to'g'ri: dekor bir xil);
//   • nom lug'atda tanilsa (венге, шимо, белый, forest…) -> O'SHA rang;
//   • tanilmasa (masalan «Кора») -> yana panel materiali (tasodifiy pastel rang QO'YILMAYDI).
function _buttAutoCol(p,b){
  const nm=String((b&&(b.mat||b.sign))||'').trim();
  if(!nm||_EDGE_SAME.test(nm))return null;
  let f=null;
  try{ f=_matFinish(nm); }catch(e){ return null; }
  if(!f||f.unk)return null;
  const hex=f.base||f.mcol||(f.kind==='mirror'?'#d3dade':(f.kind==='glass'?'#bcd2e2':null));
  return hex||null;
}
function edgeKeyOfButt(p,b){
  if(!b)return null;
  if(b.tex)return 't'+String(b.tex)+(b.tint&&!_emptyCol(b.tint)?('~'+b.tint):'');
  if(b.col&&!_emptyCol(b.col))return 'c'+String(b.col);
  if(p&&p.edge&&p.edge.col&&!_emptyCol(p.edge.col)&&!p.edge.same)return 'c'+String(p.edge.col);
  const auto=_buttAutoCol(p,b);
  if(auto)return 'c'+auto;
  return null;   // real ma'lumot yo'q -> panel materiali (kromka ajralib turmaydi)
}
// nuqtadan kesmagacha masofa (panel lokal mm)
function _segDist(px,py,a,b){
  const dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],L2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
  let t=L2?(((px-a[0])*dx+(py-a[1])*dy)/L2):0;
  t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
  return Math.hypot(px-(a[0]+t*dx),py-(a[1]+t*dy));
}
// nuqtadan KROMKA qirrasigacha masofa. Qirra YOY bo'lsa (oval detal) extractor
// b.pts — yoyning haqiqiy nuqtalarini beradi; faqat vatar (b.seg) bilan o'lchansa
// chuqur yoyda masofa katta chiqib, kromka tanilmay qolardi (oval detalda kromka
// rangi ko'rinmasdi).
function _buttDist(px,py,b){
  const pts=b.pts;
  if(pts&&pts.length>1){
    let d=Infinity;
    for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const q=_segDist(px,py,pts[i],pts[i+1]);if(q<d)d=q;}
    return d;
  }
  return _segDist(px,py,b.seg[0],b.seg[1]);
}
// Yon yuza uchburchaklarini kromkasi bo'yicha guruhlaydi (ExtrudeGeometry indekssiz —
// uchburchaklarni qayta tartiblab material guruhlarini quramiz). Qaytaradi: kalitlar ro'yxati
// (material indeksi = 2 + o'rni) yoki null (rangli kromka yo'q — hech narsa o'zgarmaydi).
function splitSidesByEdge(geo,p){
  const bts=(p.butts||[]).filter(b=>b&&b.seg&&b.seg.length===2&&edgeKeyOfButt(p,b));
  if(!bts.length||geo.index)return null;
  const gs=geo.groups||[];const sideG=gs.find(x=>x.materialIndex===1);
  if(!sideG||!sideG.count)return null;
  const attrs=['position','normal','uv'].map(n=>geo.attributes[n]).filter(Boolean);
  const pos=geo.attributes.position;
  let bmax=0;for(const bv of (p.bevels||[]))bmax=Math.max(bmax,+bv.a||0,+bv.b||0);
  const tol=4+bmax;                       // zapilli yuza markazi konturdan ichkarida bo'ladi
  const start=sideG.start,nTri=Math.floor(sideG.count/3);
  const keys=[],keyOfTri=new Array(nTri);
  for(let t=0;t<nTri;t++){
    const base=start+t*3;
    let mx=0,my=0;
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){mx+=pos.getX(base+k);my+=pos.getY(base+k);}
    mx/=3;my/=3;
    let best=null,bd=tol;
    for(const b of bts){const d=_buttDist(mx,my,b);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=b;}}
    const key=best?edgeKeyOfButt(p,best):null;   // teshik devorlari va kromkasiz tomonlar -> null
    keyOfTri[t]=key;
    if(key&&keys.indexOf(key)<0)keys.push(key);
  }
  if(!keys.length)return null;
  // tartib: avval kromkasiz (panel materiali), keyin har kromka o'z bloki bilan
  const order=[];
  for(let t=0;t<nTri;t++)if(!keyOfTri[t])order.push(t);
  const counts=[order.length];
  for(const key of keys){let c=0;for(let t=0;t<nTri;t++)if(keyOfTri[t]===key){order.push(t);c++;}counts.push(c);}
  for(const a of attrs){                  // uchburchaklarni yangi tartibda qayta yozamiz
    const it=a.itemSize,src=a.array.slice(start*it,(start+nTri*3)*it);
    for(let i=0;i<order.length;i++){
      const from=order[i]*3*it,to=(start+i*3)*it;
      for(let k=0;k<3*it;k++)a.array[to+k]=src[from+k];
    }
    a.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  geo.groups=gs.filter(x=>x!==sideG);
  let off=start;
  for(let i=0;i<counts.length;i++){
    if(counts[i])geo.addGroup(off,counts[i]*3,i===0?1:(1+i));   // 1 = panel materiali, 2.. = kromkalar
    off+=counts[i]*3;
  }
  return keys;
}
// 2D chizmada juda OCH kromkani biroz to'qlashtiramiz (och panel fonida ko'rinib tursin)
function buttColor2D(p,b){
  const css=buttColorCss(p,b), h=css.replace('#','');
  const r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)||0, g2=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)||0, bl=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)||0;
  if((0.299*r+0.587*g2+0.114*bl)/255>0.72){ const f=0.6;
    return '#'+[r,g2,bl].map(v=>('0'+Math.round(v*f).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join(''); }
  return css;
}

let scene,camera,renderer,raycaster,model,meshes=[],selected=null,DATA=null,clock;
let cx=0,cy=0,cz=0,dist=1500,rotY=0.7,rotX=0.45,modelR=2000;
let _camFly=null;   // 📷 ikki bosqichli parvoz: uzoqlashish -> skanerlangan detalga yaqinlashish
let velY=0,velX=0,dragging=false;   // aylanish inersiyasi
let drillOpen=false, drillFilter={};
let drillSelHole=null, _drillPanel=null;   // 2D'da tanlangan teshik (chetlargacha razmer uchun)
let homeView=null;   // model markazi + masofa (markazga qaytarish uchun)
let dimGroup=null, dimsVisible=false, dimSpan=1000;   // 'Размер' gabarit o'lchov chiziqlari (lazy)
let furn3D=false, furnMarkerGroup=null;   // furnitura/krepyojni 3D modelda ko'rsatish (lazy)
let furnInstances=[];   // har bir furnitura NUSXAsi uchun {im,idx,base,gid,fit,visible} — blok bo'yicha
let furnMeshList=[];    // furnitura InstancedMesh'lari (raycast/tanlash uchun)
let selectedFurn=null, _furnHi=null, _furnHiMat=null;   // tanlangan furnitura + porloq (highlight) mesh
// yashirish/izolyatsiya qilishda (InstancedMesh'da nusxa alohida 'visible'ga ega emas, shu sabab
// matritsani nolga tenglashtirib "yashiramiz").
let _furnZeroMat=null;
function furnZeroMat(){ return _furnZeroMat||(_furnZeroMat=new THREE.Matrix4().makeScale(0,0,0)); }
function makeFurnInstance(im,idx,base,gid,line,fit){
  let vis=true, off=null;   // off = explode siljishi (yoki null)
  function apply(){
    let m=base;
    if(off){ m=base.clone(); m.elements[12]+=off.x; m.elements[13]+=off.y; m.elements[14]+=off.z; }
    im.setMatrixAt(idx, vis?m:furnZeroMat());
    im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    if(line){ line.visible=vis; if(vis){ line.matrix.copy(m); line.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate=true; } }
  }
  return {
    im, idx, base, gid, line, fit:fit||null, userData:{gid},
    get visible(){ return vis; },
    set visible(v){ v=!!v; if(v===vis)return; vis=v; apply(); },
    setExplode(o){ off=o; apply(); }   // explode siljishini qo'llaydi (o=null -> asl joyi)
  };
}
let _furnEdgeCount=0;   // yaratilgan furnitura kontur-chiziqlari (FURN_EDGE_MAX chegara)
let ANIMS=[], animMeshes=[], animInstances=[], animActive=new Map(), _animPrevT=0, animRoot=-1, animAllRoots=[];   // animatsiya (Map<root,state>)
let hiddenSet=new Set(), blockSel=null, blockMode=false;   // skrit qilish + blok tanlash
// RENTGEN REJIM — model ochilganda YOQIQ (foydalanuvchi so'rovi 2026-08-16). Yoqiq bo'lsa
// detal/blok tanlanganda qolganlari xiralashadi va tanlangani yarim-shaffof bo'lib ichidagi
// teshik/furnitura ko'rinadi. O'chirilsa — detal ODDIY ko'rinishda tanlanadi.
let xrayOn=true;
// TO'SILGAN DETALNI KO'RSATISH: tanlangan BITTA detal ustiga juda yengil (10%) sariq
// qatlam qo'yiladi — depthTest=false, ya'ni oldidagi panellar uni to'sa olmaydi.
// BLOK tanlanganda bu qatlam QO'YILMAYDI (83 ta detal sariq bo'lib "bo'rtib" ketmasin) —
// u yerda faqat to'q-sariq kontur ishlaydi. (Foydalanuvchi tanlovi 2026-08-16.)
let _selGhost=null;
function clearSelGhost(){
  if(_selGhost){ if(_selGhost.parent)_selGhost.parent.remove(_selGhost);
                 if(_selGhost.material)_selGhost.material.dispose(); _selGhost=null; }
}
function addSelGhost(m){
  if(!m||!m.geometry)return;
  const mat=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff8a2a,transparent:true,opacity:0.10,
    depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
  _selGhost=new THREE.Mesh(m.geometry,mat);
  _selGhost.renderOrder=998; _selGhost.userData._ghost=true;
  m.add(_selGhost);
}
let rulerOn=false, rulerPts=[], rulerGroup=null, rulerDetails=[];   // lineyka: 2 DETAL orasini o'lchaydi
let loupeVisible=false, loupeX=0, loupeY=0;
let rulerOuter=false;      // false = ICHKI (yaqin yuzalar orasi), true = TASHQI (eng uzoq yuzalar)                // lineyka lupasi (kattalashtiruvchi doira)
let _screenshotPending=false;                              // skrinshot so'rovi — render() ichida shu kadrdan olinadi
let explodeOn=false, explodeMax=0;                          // yoyib ko'rsatish (exploded view)
let vcRenderer=null, vcScene=null, vcCamera=null, vcCube=null, vcRay=null;   // ViewCube
const TEX_MM=1000;         // standart fallback: 1 tekstura rasmi 1000mm (Bazis PARAM_12/13 default)
const _texCache={};        // id -> THREE.Texture (panellar bo'lishadi)
const _texMatName={};      // id -> material nomi (tekstura yuklanmasa protsedural naqsh SHUNDAN yasaladi)
function getTex(id,matName){
  if(matName&&!_texMatName[id])_texMatName[id]=matName;
  if(!_texCache[id]){
    const t=new THREE.TextureLoader().load('/tex/'+id+'.webp',undefined,undefined,()=>_texFail(id));
    t.wrapS=t.wrapT=THREE.RepeatWrapping;
    if(THREE.sRGBEncoding!==undefined)t.encoding=THREE.sRGBEncoding;
    // qiya burchakdan qaraganda ham tekstura tiniq qolsin (yog'och tolasi yuvilib ketmasin)
    if(renderer)t.anisotropy=Math.min(8,renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy());
    _texCache[id]=t;
  }
  return _texCache[id];
}
// TEKSTURA FAYLI YUKLANMADI (404 / buzuq rasm / tarmoq uzildi).
// AVVAL bunday panel BUTUNLAY KO'RINMAY qolardi: material `transparent:true` bilan quriladi,
// yuklanmagan `map` esa shaderда alfa=0 beradi — foydalanuvchi buni "material shaffof bo'lib
// qolgan" deb ko'rardi (davr-mebel/258213, katalogда bo'lgan-u pack'ga tushmagan tekstura).
// Endi material NOMIdan protsedural naqsh yasab, aynan SHU teksturaning rasmi qilib qo'yamiz:
// bitta joyda tuzatilgani uchun shu teksturani ishlatgan HAMMA panel darhol tiklanadi.
function _texFail(id){
  const t=_texCache[id]; if(!t||t.__failed)return; t.__failed=true;
  const nm=_texMatName[id]||'';
  let cv=null;
  try{ const f=genMatTex(nm); if(f&&f.map&&f.map.image)cv=f.map.image; }catch(e){}
  if(!cv){                       // protsedural naqsh yo'q (metall/oyna/ko'zgu) — tekis rang
    try{
      cv=document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width=cv.height=8;
      const g=cv.getContext('2d');
      g.fillStyle='#'+('000000'+((matColor(nm)>>>0)&0xffffff).toString(16)).slice(-6);
      g.fillRect(0,0,8,8);
    }catch(e){ return; }
  }
  t.image=cv; t.needsUpdate=true;
  console.warn('[DetalQR] tekstura yuklanmadi:',id,'— material nomidan naqsh qo\'yildi:',nm);
  try{ render(); }catch(e){}
}
// geometriya UV'sini real o'lchamga moslab "pishiradi" (bitta tekstura repeat=1 bilan ishlaydi)
// TOREC (yon yuza) UV — kromka ko'rinishi uchun. Panel yuzasining UV'si position.xy dan
// olinadi; yon yuzada esa x,y qalinlik bo'ylab O'ZGARMAYDI -> tekstura "cho'zilib" ketadi
// (naqsh ko'rinmaydi, tekis dog' bo'lib qoladi). Shuning uchun torecda UV boshqacha:
//   u = qirra bo'ylab masofa (mm),  v = qalinlik bo'ylab (mm).
// Shunda yog'och tolasi kromka bo'ylab yotadi — Bazisdagi kabi.
// kvaternion (x,y,z,w) bilan vektorni aylantirish
function _rotQ(q,v){
  if(!q)return v;
  const qx=q[0],qy=q[1],qz=q[2],qw=q[3],vx=v[0],vy=v[1],vz=v[2];
  const ix= qw*vx + qy*vz - qz*vy,
        iy= qw*vy + qz*vx - qx*vz,
        iz= qw*vz + qx*vy - qy*vx,
        iw=-qx*vx - qy*vy - qz*vz;
  return [ix*qw + iw*(-qx) + iy*(-qz) - iz*(-qy),
          iy*qw + iw*(-qy) + iz*(-qx) - ix*(-qz),
          iz*qw + iw*(-qz) + ix*(-qy) - iy*(-qx)];
}
function bakeSideUV(geo,sx,sy,cx,cy,quat,swap){
  const g=(geo.groups||[]).find(x=>x.materialIndex===1);   // 1 = yon yuzalar guruhi
  if(g)bakeSideUVGroup(geo,g,sx,sy,cx,cy,quat,swap);
}
// panel konturining markazi (tashqi normalni aniqlash uchun)
function polyCenter(p){
  if(!p||!p.poly||!p.poly.length)return null;
  let x=0,y=0;
  for(const q of p.poly){x+=q[0];y+=q[1];}
  return [x/p.poly.length, y/p.poly.length];
}
// Kromkaning O'Z sozlamasi bilan (Bazisdagi «Шаг X/Y»): u = qirra bo'ylab / Шаг X,
// v = qalinlik bo'ylab / Шаг Y. Bazisdagi «Угол» bu yerda QAYTA qo'llanmaydi — u
// Bazisning panel koordinatasiga tegishli, kromka qadamlari (masalan 160×16, bunda
// 16 = detal qalinligi) allaqachon shu ko'rinishga hisoblangan.
// `swap` (2026-09-03, muammo xabari #82, PlotWood): «Кромка отображает текстуру
// поперек. Должен быть алгоритм который распознает торцы и располагает текстуру
// ВДОЛЬ торца.» — haq. Kromka lentasi rulondan chiqadi, tolasi HAR DOIM lenta
// uzunligi bo'ylab. Bizda esa rasmning tola o'qi (ts.g) qalinlik (16 mm) tomonga
// tushib qolardi: 2800 mm qadamli dekorning 16 mm'lik bo'lagi = tolalar torets
// bo'ylab EMAS, KO'NDALANG va juda zich. `swap` bo'lsa u/v almashadi: rasm tolasi
// torets UZUNLIGI bo'ylab yotadi.
function bakeSideUVGroup(geo,g,sx,sy,cx,cy,quat,swap){
  const uv=geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.uv, pos=geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.position;
  if(!uv||!pos||!g||!g.count)return;
  const nTri=Math.floor(g.count/3);
  for(let t=0;t<nTri;t++){
    const b=g.start+t*3;
    let dx=1,dy=0,best=-1,mx=0,my=0;                        // eng uzun xy-qirra + markaz
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const j=(i+1)%3;
      const ax=pos.getX(b+i),ay=pos.getY(b+i),bx=pos.getX(b+j),by=pos.getY(b+j);
      mx+=ax/3; my+=ay/3;
      const d=Math.hypot(bx-ax,by-ay);
      if(d>best){best=d;dx=bx-ax;dy=by-ay;}
    }
    if(best>1e-6){dx/=best;dy/=best;}else{dx=1;dy=0;}
    // YO'NALISH: kromka yozuvi/naqshi hamma tomonda BIR XIL yotishi kerak (aks holda
    // ba'zi tomonlarda KO'ZGUDA chiqadi). Buni DUNYO koordinatasida hisoblaymiz — panel
    // fazoda ixtiyoriy aylantirilgan bo'lishi mumkin. Tashqaridan qaralganda "o'ng"
    // tomon: U = UP × N (UP = dunyo tepasi, N = yuzaning tashqi normali).
    if(cx!==undefined){
      // TASHQI NORMAL: geometriyaning o'z normalidan (aniq). Kontur markazidan hisoblash
      // murakkab (o'yiqli/ichi bo'sh) konturda XATO yo'nalish berardi — shu sabab yozuv
      // ba'zi tomonlarда ko'zguda chiqardi.
      let nx, ny;
      const nrm=geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.normal;
      if(nrm){ nx=nrm.getX(b); ny=nrm.getY(b); }
      if(nx===undefined || (Math.abs(nx)<1e-6 && Math.abs(ny)<1e-6)){ nx=mx-cx; ny=my-cy; }
      const nl=Math.hypot(nx,ny);
      if(nl>1e-6){
        nx/=nl; ny/=nl;
        const nW=_rotQ(quat,[nx,ny,0]), dW=_rotQ(quat,[dx,dy,0]);
        // UP=(0,1,0) bo'lgani uchun UP × N = (nW[2], 0, -nW[0])
        const tx=nW[2], tz=-nW[0];
        const s=dW[0]*tx + dW[2]*tz;
        if(Math.abs(s) > 1e-6){
          if(s < 0){dx=-dx;dy=-dy;}                 // 1) yuzasi yonga qaragan qirra:
                                                     //    tashqaridan qaralganda o'ngga
        }else if(Math.abs(dW[1]) > 1e-6){
          if(dW[1] < 0){dx=-dx;dy=-dy;}             // 2) TIK qirra: pastdan yuqoriga
        }else{
          // 3) yuzasi TEPAGA/PASTGA qaragan qirra (panel ustki/ostki toreci) — yo'nalishni
          // birinchi ikki qoida aniqlamaydi. Bunda uchburchaklar TASODIFIY tomonga tushib,
          // BITTA yuzaning ikki uchburchagi qarama-qarshi bo'lib qolardi (yozuv chalkashardi).
          // Shu sabab qat'iy tartib: +X, u ham nol bo'lsa +Z tomonga.
          if(dW[0] < -1e-6 || (Math.abs(dW[0]) <= 1e-6 && dW[2] < 0)){dx=-dx;dy=-dy;}
        }
      }
    }
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const x=pos.getX(b+i),y=pos.getY(b+i),z=pos.getZ(b+i);
      const al=(x*dx+y*dy);                 // qirra BO'YLAB masofa
      if(swap) uv.setXY(b+i, z*sx, al*sy);  // tola torets uzunligi bo'ylab
      else     uv.setXY(b+i, al*sx, z*sy);
    }
  }
  uv.needsUpdate=true;
}
// «РАСТЯНУТЬ» (Bazis Stretch) — 2026-09-03 TEKSHIRUVI:
// Bazis material xossasi `Stretch` teksturani panelga cho'zadi deb o'ylagan edik va
// 02.09 da shunday qilib ko'rildi — NATIJA NOTO'G'RI chiqdi. Haqiqiy dalil:
// `.b3d` faylining ichida BAZISNING O'Z preview rasmi saqlanadi (PNG, offset ~209).
// «Комод» (material «М3 корпус 16мм Дерево», Шаг 2070×2800, растянуть=1) preview'ida
// har panelda dekorning KICHIK bo'lagi ko'rinadi — ya'ni Bazis teksturani panelga
// cho'zmaydi, Шаг bo'yicha YOTQIZADI. Demak `Растянуть` = rasmni aynan Шаг
// to'rtburchagiga moslash (proporsiyani buzib) — biz bakeUV'da allaqachon shunday
// qilamiz (u=x/Шаг_X, v=y/Шаг_Y). Shu sabab alohida ishlov KERAK EMAS.
// GORIZONTAL (yotiq) panelmi — krishka / dno / polka? Normali dunyo Y o'qiga yaqin.
// Kerak: Bazisda `TextureOrientation=2` («вертикально») YOTIQ panelda ma'noga ega emas
// (vertikal yo'nalish panel tekisligida yo'q), shuning uchun Bazisning o'zi u yerda
// boshqa qoidaga o'tadi — pastdagi izohga qarang.
function isFlatPanel(p){
  const q=p&&p.quat;
  if(!q||q.length!==4) return false;
  try{
    const v=new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1).applyQuaternion(new THREE.Quaternion(q[0],q[1],q[2],q[3]));
    return Math.abs(v.y)>0.9;
  }catch(e){ return false; }
}
function bakeUV(geo,sx,sy,swap,ang,fromPos,mir){
  const uv=geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.uv; if(!uv)return;
  // fromPos=true -> UV panel LOKAL koordinatasidan (position.xy) hisoblanadi: shunda
  // tekstura yon yuzaga (TOREC/kromka) ham DAVOM etadi (Узидан kromka = panel bilan bir xil).
  const pos=fromPos?(geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.position):null;
  const c=ang?Math.cos(ang*Math.PI/180):1, s=ang?Math.sin(ang*Math.PI/180):0;   // Bazis "Угол"
  for(let i=0;i<uv.count;i++){
    let u,v;
    if(pos){u=pos.getX(i);v=pos.getY(i);}else{u=uv.getX(i);v=uv.getY(i);}
    if(swap){const tmp=u;u=v;v=tmp;}
    u*=sx; v*=sy;
    if(mir)u=-u;                                    // Bazis «Зеркально»
    if(ang){const nu=u*c-v*s, nv=u*s+v*c; u=nu; v=nv;}   // tekstura burchagi
    uv.setXY(i,u,v);
  }
  uv.needsUpdate=true;
}

// ============ CSG (Boolean Subtract) — Bazis "Вычитание тел" uchun ============
// Minimal BSP-CSG (Evan Wallace csg.js asosida), THREE r128 BufferGeometry bilan ishlaydi.
// Base paneldan ayirma qurollarini HAQIQIY o'yish uchun (3ds Max Boolean Subtract kabi).
const CSGop=(function(){
  const EPS=1e-5;
  function Vertex(pos,normal){this.pos=pos;this.normal=normal;}
  Vertex.prototype.clone=function(){return new Vertex(this.pos.clone(),this.normal.clone());};
  Vertex.prototype.flip=function(){this.normal.negate();};
  Vertex.prototype.interpolate=function(o,t){return new Vertex(this.pos.clone().lerp(o.pos,t),this.normal.clone().lerp(o.normal,t));};
  function Plane(normal,w){this.normal=normal;this.w=w;}
  // Newell metodi: kollinear 1-3 uchi bo'lgan (yoki bir oz notekis) poligon uchun ham
  // ISHONCHLI normal beradi — oddiy 3-nuqtali cross ba'zan null qaytarardi.
  Plane.fromVerts=function(vs){const n=new THREE.Vector3();for(let i=0;i<vs.length;i++){const a=vs[i].pos,b=vs[(i+1)%vs.length].pos;n.x+=(a.y-b.y)*(a.z+b.z);n.y+=(a.z-b.z)*(a.x+b.x);n.z+=(a.x-b.x)*(a.y+b.y);}if(n.lengthSq()<1e-16)return null;n.normalize();return new Plane(n,n.dot(vs[0].pos));};
  Plane.prototype.clone=function(){return new Plane(this.normal.clone(),this.w);};
  Plane.prototype.flip=function(){this.normal.negate();this.w=-this.w;};
  Plane.prototype.splitPolygon=function(poly,cf,cb,fr,bk){
    const CO=0,FR=1,BA=2,SP=3;let ptype=0;const types=[];
    for(const v of poly.vertices){const t=this.normal.dot(v.pos)-this.w;const ty=(t<-EPS)?BA:(t>EPS)?FR:CO;ptype|=ty;types.push(ty);}
    if(ptype===CO){(this.normal.dot(poly.plane.normal)>0?cf:cb).push(poly);}
    else if(ptype===FR){fr.push(poly);}
    else if(ptype===BA){bk.push(poly);}
    else{const f=[],b=[];const vs=poly.vertices;
      for(let i=0;i<vs.length;i++){const j=(i+1)%vs.length,ti=types[i],tj=types[j],vi=vs[i],vj=vs[j];
        if(ti!==BA)f.push(vi);
        if(ti!==FR)b.push(ti!==BA?vi.clone():vi);
        if((ti|tj)===SP){const t=(this.w-this.normal.dot(vi.pos))/this.normal.dot(vj.pos.clone().sub(vi.pos));const v=vi.interpolate(vj,t);f.push(v);b.push(v.clone());}}
      if(f.length>=3){const np=new Polygon(f);if(np.plane)fr.push(np);}
      if(b.length>=3){const np=new Polygon(b);if(np.plane)bk.push(np);}}
  };
  function Polygon(vertices){this.vertices=vertices;this.plane=Plane.fromVerts(vertices);}
  Polygon.prototype.clone=function(){return new Polygon(this.vertices.map(v=>v.clone()));};
  Polygon.prototype.flip=function(){this.vertices.reverse();for(const v of this.vertices)v.flip();if(this.plane)this.plane.flip();};
  function Node(polys){this.plane=null;this.front=null;this.back=null;this.polygons=[];if(polys)this.build(polys);}
  Node.prototype.invert=function(){for(const p of this.polygons)p.flip();if(this.plane)this.plane.flip();if(this.front)this.front.invert();if(this.back)this.back.invert();const t=this.front;this.front=this.back;this.back=t;};
  Node.prototype.clipPolygons=function(polys){if(!this.plane)return polys.slice();let fr=[],bk=[];for(const p of polys)this.plane.splitPolygon(p,fr,bk,fr,bk);if(this.front)fr=this.front.clipPolygons(fr);bk=this.back?this.back.clipPolygons(bk):[];return fr.concat(bk);};
  Node.prototype.clipTo=function(bsp){this.polygons=bsp.clipPolygons(this.polygons);if(this.front)this.front.clipTo(bsp);if(this.back)this.back.clipTo(bsp);};
  Node.prototype.allPolygons=function(){let ps=this.polygons.slice();if(this.front)ps=ps.concat(this.front.allPolygons());if(this.back)ps=ps.concat(this.back.allPolygons());return ps;};
  Node.prototype.build=function(polys){if(!polys.length)return;if(!this.plane)this.plane=polys[0].plane&&polys[0].plane.clone();if(!this.plane)return;const fr=[],bk=[];for(const p of polys){if(!p.plane)continue;this.plane.splitPolygon(p,this.polygons,this.polygons,fr,bk);}if(fr.length){if(!this.front)this.front=new Node();this.front.build(fr);}if(bk.length){if(!this.back)this.back=new Node();this.back.build(bk);}};
  function toPolys(geo){let g=geo.index?geo.toNonIndexed():geo;let np=g.attributes.normal;if(!np){g=g.clone();g.computeVertexNormals();np=g.attributes.normal;}const pp=g.attributes.position,out=[];
    for(let i=0;i<pp.count;i+=3){const vs=[];for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const idx=i+k;vs.push(new Vertex(new THREE.Vector3(pp.getX(idx),pp.getY(idx),pp.getZ(idx)),new THREE.Vector3(np.getX(idx),np.getY(idx),np.getZ(idx))));}const poly=new Polygon(vs);if(poly.plane)out.push(poly);}return out;}
  // WELD (indeksli): bir tekislikdagi qo'shni uchburchaklar umumiy vertexni bo'lishadi ->
  // ular uzluksiz rasterlanadi, seam (antialiasing chizig'i/shimmer) chiqmaydi. Turli normalда
  // (haqiqiy qirra) vertexlar birlashmaydi -> keskin burchak saqlanadi.
  function toGeo(polys){
    const pos=[],nor=[],idx=[],map=new Map();
    const key=(p,n)=>Math.round(p.x*100)+'_'+Math.round(p.y*100)+'_'+Math.round(p.z*100)+'_'+Math.round(n.x*8)+'_'+Math.round(n.y*8)+'_'+Math.round(n.z*8);
    const add=v=>{const k=key(v.pos,v.normal);let i=map.get(k);if(i===undefined){i=pos.length/3;pos.push(v.pos.x,v.pos.y,v.pos.z);nor.push(v.normal.x,v.normal.y,v.normal.z);map.set(k,i);}return i;};
    for(const poly of polys){const vs=poly.vertices;for(let i=2;i<vs.length;i++){idx.push(add(vs[0]),add(vs[i-1]),add(vs[i]));}}
    const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));
    g.setAttribute('normal',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(nor,3));
    g.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(pos.length/3*2),2));
    g.setIndex(idx);
    return g;}
  function subtract(aPolys,bPolys){const a=new Node(aPolys),b=new Node(bPolys);a.invert();a.clipTo(b);b.clipTo(a);b.invert();b.clipTo(a);b.invert();a.build(b.allPolygons());a.invert();return a.allPolygons();}
  // T-JUNCTION repair: BSP notekis bo'linganida bir uchburchak qirrasi o'rtasiga qo'shni
  // uchburchak vertexi tushib, ular zich ulanmaydi -> hairline tirqish/shimmer. Har qirra ustida
  // yotgan (boshqa polygon) vertexini o'sha qirraga QO'SHAMIZ -> tirqish yo'qoladi.
  // ⚡ 2026-09-02: ilgari har QIRRA uchun BARCHA nuqtalar aylanib chiqilardi
  // (O(qirra × nuqta)) va har solishtirishda Vector3 klonlanardi. Murakkab modelda
  // (bomens-2 «Стол для переговоров») shu funksiya YAKKA O'ZI ~10.3 s olardi — jami
  // qurilish 19 s (foydalanuvchi: «modeli qurilishi juda sekin»). Endi nuqtalar X
  // bo'yicha saralanadi, har qirraga faqat X-oralig'idagi nomzodlar binar qidiruv
  // bilan olinadi va hisob klonsiz (skalyar) bajariladi.
  function repairT(polys){
    const pts=[],seen=new Set();
    for(const poly of polys)for(const v of poly.vertices){const k=Math.round(v.pos.x*50)+'_'+Math.round(v.pos.y*50)+'_'+Math.round(v.pos.z*50);if(!seen.has(k)){seen.add(k);pts.push(v.pos);}}
    const EPS=0.08, E2=EPS*EPS;
    pts.sort((u,v)=>u.x-v.x);
    const N=pts.length, xs=new Float64Array(N);
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++)xs[i]=pts[i].x;
    const lower=v=>{let lo=0,hi=N;while(lo<hi){const m=(lo+hi)>>1;if(xs[m]<v)lo=m+1;else hi=m;}return lo;};
    for(const poly of polys){
      const vs=poly.vertices,nv=[];
      for(let i=0;i<vs.length;i++){
        const a=vs[i],b=vs[(i+1)%vs.length];nv.push(a);
        const ax=a.pos.x,ay=a.pos.y,az=a.pos.z;
        const abx=b.pos.x-ax,aby=b.pos.y-ay,abz=b.pos.z-az;
        const L2=abx*abx+aby*aby+abz*abz;if(L2<1e-6)continue;
        const mnx=Math.min(ax,b.pos.x)-EPS,mxx=Math.max(ax,b.pos.x)+EPS;
        const mny=Math.min(ay,b.pos.y)-EPS,mxy=Math.max(ay,b.pos.y)+EPS;
        const mnz=Math.min(az,b.pos.z)-EPS,mxz=Math.max(az,b.pos.z)+EPS;
        let on=null;
        for(let k=lower(mnx);k<N&&xs[k]<=mxx;k++){
          const q=pts[k];
          if(q.y<mny||q.y>mxy||q.z<mnz||q.z>mxz)continue;
          const px=q.x-ax,py=q.y-ay,pz=q.z-az;
          const t=(px*abx+py*aby+pz*abz)/L2;
          if(t<=1e-4||t>=1-1e-4)continue;
          const dx=px-abx*t,dy=py-aby*t,dz=pz-abz*t;
          if(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz<E2){ if(!on)on=[]; on.push({t,p:q}); }
        }
        if(on){ on.sort((x,y)=>x.t-y.t);
          for(const o of on)nv.push(new Vertex(o.p.clone(),a.normal.clone())); }
      }
      if(nv.length>=3)poly.vertices=nv;
    }
    return polys;
  }
  return {toPolys,toGeo,subtract,repairT};
})();

// CSG natijasidan KONTUR (crease) qirralari: qirra faqat IKKI XIL tekislik uchrashgan joyда
// chiziladi (haqiqiy burchak/o'yiq chegarasi). Bitta tekislikdagi ichki bo'linishlar (BSP
// T-junction chiziqlari) chizilmaydi — shu bilan begona chiziqlar chiqmaydi, kontur qoladi.
function csgCreaseEdges(polys){
  const vk=p=>Math.round(p.x*10)+'_'+Math.round(p.y*10)+'_'+Math.round(p.z*10);
  const nk=n=>Math.round(n.x*50)+'_'+Math.round(n.y*50)+'_'+Math.round(n.z*50);   // ~1° aniqlik
  const em=new Map();
  for(const poly of polys){
    if(!poly.plane)continue;
    const key=nk(poly.plane.normal), vs=poly.vertices;
    for(let i=0;i<vs.length;i++){
      const a=vs[i].pos,b=vs[(i+1)%vs.length].pos,ka=vk(a),kb=vk(b);
      if(ka===kb)continue;
      const ek=ka<kb?ka+'|'+kb:kb+'|'+ka;
      let e=em.get(ek);
      if(!e){e={a,b,pl:new Set()};em.set(ek,e);}
      e.pl.add(key);
    }
  }
  const pos=[];
  for(const e of em.values()) if(e.pl.size>=2) pos.push(e.a.x,e.a.y,e.a.z,e.b.x,e.b.y,e.b.z);
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));
  return g;
}

// CSG natijasida UV YO'Q: `CSGop.toGeo` uv massivini NOLLAR bilan to'ldiradi —
// tekstura bitta nuqtadan olinib, detal bir tekis «bo'yalgan» bo'lib chiqardi
// (foydalanuvchi 2026-09-02: «murakkab detallarda tekstura detalga birikishida xatolik»).
// Panel UV'si millimetrda (ExtrudeGeometry qopqoqlari ham shunday) — normalga qarab
// eng mos tekislikka yassi proyeksiya qilamiz.
function csgUV(g){
  const p=g&&g.attributes&&g.attributes.position, nr=g&&g.attributes&&g.attributes.normal;
  if(!p||!nr)return g;
  const uv=new Float32Array(p.count*2);
  for(let i=0;i<p.count;i++){
    const ax=Math.abs(nr.getX(i)), ay=Math.abs(nr.getY(i)), az=Math.abs(nr.getZ(i));
    let u,v;
    if(az>=ax&&az>=ay){ u=p.getX(i); v=p.getY(i); }        // plast (A/B)
    else if(ax>=ay){ u=p.getZ(i); v=p.getY(i); }           // yon (x normal)
    else { u=p.getX(i); v=p.getZ(i); }                     // yon (y normal)
    uv[i*2]=u; uv[i*2+1]=v;
  }
  g.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uv,2));
  return g;
}

// Base geometriyadan (LOKAL) ayirma qurollarini CSG bilan o'yadi. Base pos/quat orqali
// joylashadi; qurollar WORLD transformidan base LOKAL fazasiga o'tkazib ayiriladi.
// CSG KESHI: "Вычитание тел" (Boolean) — eng qimmat amal (bitta panelga ~80-100 ms).
// Bir xil detal ko'p marta takrorlanadi (masalan 68 ta bir xil tortma yonbosh), lekin
// qurollar DUNYO koordinatasida saqlangani uchun har biri "boshqa" bo'lib ko'rinardi.
// Kalitni PANEL LOKAL fazosida tuzamiz (qurol transformi panelga NISBATAN) — shunda
// takrorlar bir xil kalit oladi. O'lchov (mebelrum/1034): 142 amal -> 4 ta noyob (97% tejash).
// Natija KLON qilib beriladi: keyinchalik UV/kromka guruhlari har panelда alohida o'zgaradi.
const _csgCache=new Map();
function _csgKey(p,tools){
  try{
    const q=new THREE.Quaternion(p.quat[0],p.quat[1],p.quat[2],p.quat[3]).invert();
    const pos=new THREE.Vector3(p.pos[0],p.pos[1],p.pos[2]);
    const r=n=>Math.round(n*100)/100;
    const parts=[JSON.stringify(p.poly),p.thick,JSON.stringify(p.cuts||[]),JSON.stringify(p.pieces||[])];
    for(const t of tools){
      const tq=new THREE.Quaternion(t.quat[0],t.quat[1],t.quat[2],t.quat[3]).premultiply(q);
      const tp=new THREE.Vector3(t.pos[0],t.pos[1],t.pos[2]).sub(pos).applyQuaternion(q);
      parts.push(JSON.stringify(t.poly),t.thick,
                 r(tp.x)+','+r(tp.y)+','+r(tp.z),
                 r(tq.x*1e4)/1e4+','+r(tq.y*1e4)/1e4+','+r(tq.z*1e4)/1e4+','+r(tq.w*1e4)/1e4);
    }
    return parts.join('|');
  }catch(e){ return null; }
}
function applyCSGSubtract(baseGeo,p,tools){
  const _k=_csgKey(p,tools);
  if(_k&&_csgCache.has(_k)) return _csgCache.get(_k).clone();
  const _res=_applyCSGSubtractRaw(baseGeo,p,tools);
  if(_k&&_res) _csgCache.set(_k,_res.clone());
  return _res;
}
function _applyCSGSubtractRaw(baseGeo,p,tools){
  try{
    const baseM=new THREE.Matrix4().compose(new THREE.Vector3(p.pos[0],p.pos[1],p.pos[2]),
      new THREE.Quaternion(p.quat[0],p.quat[1],p.quat[2],p.quat[3]),new THREE.Vector3(1,1,1));
    const baseInv=baseM.clone().invert();
    let acc=CSGop.toPolys(baseGeo);
    for(const t of tools){
      const tp=t.poly; if(!tp||tp.length<3)continue;
      const sh=new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(tp[0][0],tp[0][1]);
      for(let i=1;i<tp.length;i++)sh.lineTo(tp[i][0],tp[i][1]); sh.closePath();
      const dep=panelDep(t);
      const tgeo=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh,{depth:dep,bevelEnabled:false});
      if(t.thick<0)tgeo.translate(0,0,-dep);
      const toolM=new THREE.Matrix4().compose(new THREE.Vector3(t.pos[0],t.pos[1],t.pos[2]),
        new THREE.Quaternion(t.quat[0],t.quat[1],t.quat[2],t.quat[3]),new THREE.Vector3(1,1,1));
      tgeo.applyMatrix4(baseInv.clone().multiply(toolM));   // base LOKAL fazasiga
      const bp=CSGop.toPolys(tgeo);
      if(bp.length) acc=CSGop.subtract(acc,bp);
      tgeo.dispose&&tgeo.dispose();
    }
    if(!acc.length) return baseGeo;
    acc=CSGop.repairT(acc);              // T-junction tirqish/shimmer tuzatish
    const out=csgUV(CSGop.toGeo(acc));   // UV — tekstura to'g'ri yotishi uchun
    out._crease=csgCreaseEdges(acc);     // kontur qirralari (coplanar bo'linish chiziqlarisiz)
    return out;
  }catch(e){ console.warn('CSG-XATO ['+(p&&p.name)+'/'+(p&&(p.mat||'').slice(0,10))+']: '+((e&&e.message)||e)); return baseGeo; }
}

// ---- PROTSEDURAVIY TEKSTURA: material NOMIdan avtomatik generatsiya ----
// Tashqi tekstura bazasi ishlatilmaydi: nomdan yog'och turi/rang/pardoz aniqlanadi,
// choksiz (tile) naqsh kanvasda chiziladi. Bir xil nom -> doim bir xil natija (seed=nom hash).
const _genCache={};
function _mhash(s){let h=2166136261;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++){h^=s.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}return h>>>0;}
function _hasW(n,k){return new RegExp('(^|[^a-zа-яё0-9])'+k,'i').test(n);}  // so'z boshi ('тик' 'пластик'ka mos kelmasin)
// Egger laminat palitrasiga yaqin ohanglar: kamroq to'yingan, kulrang-bej yog'och ranglari
const _WOODS=[
 [['венге','wenge','venge'],'#463226','#291b10'],
 [['сонома','sonoma'],'#c9b28e','#9c8563'],
 [['крафт','craft'],'#b6905d','#87663c'],
 [['гарден','garden'],'#9c8062','#6f573e'],
 [['орех','walnut','ceviz','ноче','noce','orex','oreh'],'#7d5a3c','#54381f'],
 [['ясень','ясин','yasen','ash','шимо'],'#d6cbb4','#b3a687'],
 [['махагон','mahagon','mahogany'],'#6e3524','#4a2012'],
 [['вишн','cherry','kiraz'],'#8a4530','#5e2c1c'],
 [['бук','beech'],'#c6a37e','#9c7a55'],
 [['сосна','sosna','pine'],'#d2b68e','#a98e64'],
 [['акаци','acacia'],'#a97e4d','#7d5a30'],
 [['зебрано','zebrano'],'#a8875e','#5a4128'],
 [['берез','birch'],'#d8c8a8','#b2a17e'],
 [['ольха','alder'],'#bd9068','#956e48'],
 [['тик','teak'],'#92653a','#6a4524'],
 [['палисандр','rosewood'],'#5b3a2e','#3a2119'],
 [['гикори','hickory'],'#b08a5c','#875f38'],
 [['листвен','larch'],'#c8a172','#9e7c50'],
 [['каштан','kashtan','chestnut'],'#8a5f3c','#5e3d22'],
 [['кедр','kedr','cedar'],'#a8825a','#7a5a38'],
 [['toledo','толедо'],'#9b7a55','#6f5232'],
 [['жексон','джексон','jackson'],'#c0b09a','#94836c'],
 [['измир','izmir'],'#b28a5e','#85613c'],
 [['паркет','parket'],'#c3a87e','#8f7654'],

const _COLS=[
 [['forest'],'#2e5c44'],
 [['кашемир','кашмир','кашимер','kashmir','kashemir','kashimer','kashmer','cashmere'],'#cbc3b7'],
 [['лаванда','lavanda'],'#b3a3c6'],

function _shade(hex,f){
  const n=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16),c=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(v*f)));
  return '#'+((1<<24)|(c((n>>16)&255)<<16)|(c((n>>8)&255)<<8)|c(n&255)).toString(16).slice(1);
}
function _hsl2hex(h,s,l){
  const a=s*Math.min(l,1-l),f=k=>{const x=(k+h/30)%12;return l-a*Math.max(-1,Math.min(x-3,9-x,1));},c=v=>Math.round(v*255);
  return '#'+((1<<24)|(c(f(0))<<16)|(c(f(8))<<8)|c(f(4))).toString(16).slice(1);
}
function _matFinish(name){
  // normalizatsiya: kichik harf, ё->е ("Чёрный"), apostroflar olib tashlanadi ("yog'och"->"yogoch")
  const n=(name||'').toLowerCase().replace(/ё/g,'е').replace(/[ʼ'`’ʻ]/g,'');
  const seed=_mhash(n)||1;
  const gloss=/глянц|глянец|глянс|glyan|gloss|parlak|yaltir|акрил|akril/.test(n)||_hasW(n,'уф')||_hasW(n,'uv')||_hasW(n,'hg');
  if(/зеркал|zerkal|mirror|kozgu|козгу/.test(n))return{kind:'mirror',seed};
  // OYNA/SHISHA. ISTISNO: "Akril White Glass" kabi nomlar — bu OPAK akril fasad dekori
  // ("Glass" shunchaki kolleksiya nomi). Ular 55% shaffof ko'k shisha bo'lib chizilardi.
  // Akril nomida ANIQ "прозрачн/transparent" bo'lsagina haqiqiy shaffof deb olamiz.
  if(/стекл|glass|шиша|oyna|ойна|прозрачн|prozrachn|transparent|силикон|silicon/.test(n)
     && !(/акрил|akril/.test(n) && !/прозрачн|prozrachn|transparent/.test(n)))return{kind:'glass',seed};
  if(/метал|metal|хром|xrom|никел|алюмин|аллюмин|allu|сталь|steel|латун|chrome|металик|металлик|metalik|metallik|камондор|komandor|профил|profil|труба|truba|turba|temir|нержав|направляющ|рельс|rels|гола|gola|штанг|фольга|folga|фурнитур|furnitura|frente/.test(n)
     || _hasW(n,'inox'))   // 'inox' — FAQAT alohida so'z sifatida: 'Laminox' plita
                           // brendi ichida qolib ketib, 4 856 panelni XROM METALL qilardi
    return{kind:'metal',seed,mcol:/латун|бронз|jez/.test(n)?'#c9a84c':null};
  if(/мрамор|marble|оникс|onyx|гранит|granit|марквина|markvina|marquina/.test(n)){
    const dark=/черн|black|антрацит|темн|марквина|markvina|marquina/.test(n);
    return{kind:'marble',base:dark?'#2a2c30':'#ece8e2',vein:dark?'#8f9298':'#9aa0a8',seed,gloss:true};
  }
  if(/бетон|beton|цемент|cement/.test(n))return{kind:'concrete',base:'#9aa0a3',seed};
  if(/столешниц|столещниц|столешк|stoleshn|столишниц|stolishn|кварц|kvarts|камень|kamen|санг/.test(n))
    return{kind:'concrete',base:'#b3a897',seed};                       // stol usti tosh dekorlari (щ/ш ustalar chalkashtiradi)
  if(/кафел|kafel|плитк/.test(n))return{kind:'plain',base:'#e9e7e1',seed,gloss:true};
  if(/обои|обой|oboy/.test(n))return{kind:'plain',base:'#ded6c6',seed,gloss:false};
  for(const[keys,base,dark]of _WOODS){
    if(keys.some(k=>_hasW(n,k))){
      let f=1;
      if(/темн|dark|koyu|тук|toq/.test(n))f=.74;else if(/светл|light|och/.test(n))f=1.18;
      return{kind:'wood',base:_shade(base,f),dark:_shade(dark,f),seed,gloss};
    }
  }
  // oltin/zar rang (yog'och emasligida): ruchka, dekor profil va h.k.
  if(/(^|[^a-zа-яе0-9])(золот|gold|oltin|tilla)/.test(n))return{kind:'metal',seed,mcol:'#c9a84c'};
  for(const[keys,base]of _COLS){
    if(keys.some(k=>_hasW(n,k))){
      let f=1;
      if(/темн|dark|koyu/.test(n))f=.78;else if(/светл|light/.test(n))f=1.15;
      return{kind:'plain',base:_shade(base,f),seed,gloss};
    }
  }
  // ranggi ko'rsatilmagan akril/plastik — bunday katalog yozuvlarida odatda OQ glyanets
  // (aniq rang bo'lganda yuqorida _COLS'da allaqachon ushlanadi). "пластик" — KIRILLCHA
  // variant ham (jonli bazada 611 panel) ilgari tushib qolardi.
  if(/акрил|akril|пластм|пластик|plastm|plastik/.test(n))return{kind:'plain',base:'#f0eee8',seed,gloss:true};
  // PLITA nomi aytilgan, LEKIN dekor/rang aytilmagan ("ЛДСП 16мм", "МДФ 16мм", "ДСП - 16мм",
  // "MDF 16 mm Kastamonu"...). Ilgari bular xesh-rangli TASODIFIY och pastelga tushardi —
  // aynan foydalanuvchi «tekstura shaffof bo'lib qolgan» degan ko'rinish (hisobot #20/2054).
  // Bu tekshiruv _COLS dan KEYIN turadi: nomda rang/dekor bo'lsa u DOIM ustun (masalan
  // "ЛДСП Белый" — oq bo'lib qoladi). Jonli bazada ~7 000 panel shu holatda.
  if(/лдсп|лмдф|мдф|хдф|дсп|двп|ldsp|lmdf|mdf|hdf|dsp/.test(n))
    return{kind:'plain',base:'#ded8cc',seed,gloss};
  // nomdan rang chiqmadi — nomga barqaror yumshoq pastel (unk = lug'atda tanilmagan)
  return{kind:'plain',base:_hsl2hex(seed%360,.16,(68+((seed>>9)%14))/100),seed,gloss,unk:true};
}
function _mkTexFromCanvas(cv){
  // ATAYIN sRGB dekodlanmaydi (renderer chiqishda qayta kodlamaydi — dekod ranglarni
  // "isitib" yuborardi). Yorug'lik balansi buildScene'da envMapIntensity bilan qilinadi.
  const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
  t.wrapS=t.wrapT=THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  if(renderer)t.anisotropy=Math.min(8,renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy());
  return t;
}
function _rndGen(seed){let s=seed>>>0;return()=>((s=(s*1664525+1013904223)>>>0)/4294967296);}
function _perN(rnd,K){  // davriy 1D value-noise (chok yo'q)
  const v=[...Array(K)].map(rnd);
  return t=>{const x=(((t%1)+1)%1)*K,i=Math.floor(x),f=x-i,a=v[i%K],b=v[(i+1)%K],u=f*f*(3-2*f);return a+(b-a)*u;};
}
function _genWood(base,dark,seed){
  const N=512,cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cv.height=N;
  const g=cv.getContext('2d'),rnd=_rndGen(seed);
  // 4 oktava: yirik ohang + o'rta + mayda + JUDA mayda (zich tola chiziqlari)
  const b1=_perN(rnd,6),b2=_perN(rnd,24),b3=_perN(rnd,110),b4=_perN(rnd,260),w1=_perN(rnd,4),w2=_perN(rnd,13);
  const bc=parseInt(base.slice(1),16),dc=parseInt(dark.slice(1),16);
  const br=(bc>>16)&255,bg2=(bc>>8)&255,bb=bc&255,dr=(dc>>16)&255,dg=(dc>>8)&255,db=dc&255;
  const img=g.createImageData(N,N),d=img.data;
  for(let y=0;y<N;y++){
    const ty0=y/N;
    for(let x=0;x<N;x++){
      const w=(w1(x/N)-.5)*.028+(w2(x/N)-.5)*.010;   // deyarli TO'G'RI tola (laminat kabi)
      const ty=ty0+w;
      let v=b1(ty)*.30+b2(ty)*.28+b3(ty)*.24+b4(ty)*.18;
      v=v*v*(3-2*v);
      v+=(rnd()-.5)*.05;
      v=v<0?0:v>1?1:v;
      const i=(y*N+x)*4;
      d[i]=br+(dr-br)*v;d[i+1]=bg2+(dg-bg2)*v;d[i+2]=bb+(db-bb)*v;d[i+3]=255;
    }
  }
  g.putImageData(img,0,0);
  // to'q tomirlar — davriy sinuslar (gorizontal choksiz), y bo'yicha 3 nusxa (chok yo'q)
  g.globalCompositeOperation='multiply';
  const nv=6+Math.floor(rnd()*4);
  for(let k=0;k<nv;k++){
    const y0=rnd()*N,A1=1.5+rnd()*3,A2=.5+rnd()*1.2,c1=1+Math.floor(rnd()*2),c2=3+Math.floor(rnd()*4),p1=rnd()*6.28,p2=rnd()*6.28;
    g.strokeStyle='rgba(58,36,18,'+(.07+rnd()*.10).toFixed(2)+')';
    g.lineWidth=.7+rnd()*1.4;
    for(const dy of[-N,0,N]){
      g.beginPath();
      for(let x=0;x<=N;x+=4){
        const y=y0+dy+A1*Math.sin(6.283*(x/N)*c1+p1)+A2*Math.sin(6.283*(x/N)*c2+p2);
        x?g.lineTo(x,y):g.moveTo(x,y);
      }
      g.stroke();
    }
  }
  // g'ovak shtrixlari (pore) — tola bo'ylab kalta to'q/och chiziqchalar (laminat zichligi)
  for(let k=0;k<170;k++){
    const x0=rnd()*N,y0=rnd()*N,L=5+rnd()*26,dkk=rnd()<.72;
    g.strokeStyle=dkk?('rgba(52,34,18,'+(.05+rnd()*.08).toFixed(2)+')')
                     :('rgba(255,250,240,'+(.04+rnd()*.06).toFixed(2)+')');
    g.lineWidth=.6+rnd()*.7;
    for(const dx of[-N,0,N])for(const dy of[-N,0,N]){
      g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x0+dx,y0+dy);g.lineTo(x0+dx+L,y0+dy+(rnd()-.5)*1.6);g.stroke();
    }
  }
  g.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  return cv;
}
function _genPlain(base,seed,amp){   // melamin/shagren: mayda donador shovqin
  const N=256,cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cv.height=N;
  const g=cv.getContext('2d'),rnd=_rndGen(seed);
  const bc=parseInt(base.slice(1),16),br=(bc>>16)&255,bg2=(bc>>8)&255,bb=bc&255;
  const img=g.createImageData(N,N),d=img.data;
  for(let i=0;i<N*N;i++){
    const o=(rnd()-.5)*2*amp,j=i*4;
    d[j]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,br+o));d[j+1]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,bg2+o));d[j+2]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,bb+o));d[j+3]=255;
  }
  g.putImageData(img,0,0);
  return cv;
}
function _genMarble(base,vein,seed){
  const N=512,cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cv.height=N;
  const g=cv.getContext('2d'),rnd=_rndGen(seed);
  g.fillStyle=base;g.fillRect(0,0,N,N);
  for(let k=0;k<14;k++){   // yumshoq bulutlar
    const x=rnd()*N,y=rnd()*N,r=40+rnd()*120;
    const gr=g.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);
    const c=rnd()<.5?'255,255,255':'120,126,134';
    gr.addColorStop(0,'rgba('+c+',.10)');gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba('+c+',0)');
    g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,N,N);
  }
  g.shadowColor=vein;g.shadowBlur=3;   // tomirlar
  for(let k=0;k<7;k++){
    const y0=rnd()*N,A1=20+rnd()*60,A2=6+rnd()*18,c1=1+Math.floor(rnd()*2),c2=3+Math.floor(rnd()*4),p1=rnd()*6.28,p2=rnd()*6.28;
    g.strokeStyle=vein;g.globalAlpha=.14+rnd()*.16;g.lineWidth=.7+rnd()*1.6;
    for(const dy of[-N,0,N]){
      g.beginPath();
      for(let x=0;x<=N;x+=5){
        const y=y0+dy+A1*Math.sin(6.283*(x/N)*c1+p1)+A2*Math.sin(6.283*(x/N)*c2+p2);
        x?g.lineTo(x,y):g.moveTo(x,y);
      }
      g.stroke();
    }
  }
  g.globalAlpha=1;g.shadowBlur=0;
  return cv;
}
function _genConcrete(base,seed){
  const cv=_genPlain(base,seed,5),N=256,g=cv.getContext('2d'),rnd=_rndGen(seed^0x9e37);
  for(let k=0;k<72;k++){
    const x=rnd()*N,y=rnd()*N,r=5+rnd()*30;
    g.fillStyle=rnd()<.6?'rgba(0,0,0,.026)':'rgba(255,255,255,.03)';
    for(const dx of[-N,0,N])for(const dy of[-N,0,N]){g.beginPath();g.arc(x+dx,y+dy,r,0,7);g.fill();}
  }
  return cv;
}
// muhit aks etishi (envMap): oddiy gradient "osmon-pol" — glyanets/oyna/metall
// yuzalarga real aks beradi (aks holda metalness'li material qop-qora ko'rinadi)
let _envTex=null;
function getEnv(){
  if(_envTex||!renderer)return _envTex;
  try{
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=64;c.height=32;
    const g=c.getContext('2d');
    const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,32);
    gr.addColorStop(0,'#f2f2f0');gr.addColorStop(.48,'#c9c9c7');gr.addColorStop(.55,'#75757a');gr.addColorStop(1,'#404044');
    g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,64,32);
    const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);t.mapping=THREE.EquirectangularReflectionMapping;
    const pm=new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
    _envTex=pm.fromEquirectangular(t).texture;
    t.dispose();pm.dispose();
  }catch(e){_envTex=null;}
  return _envTex;
}
function genMatTex(name){
  const key=name||'';
  if(_genCache[key])return _genCache[key];
  const f=_matFinish(key);
  let cv=null,mm=800;
  if(f.kind==='wood'){cv=_genWood(f.base,f.dark,f.seed);mm=900;}
  else if(f.kind==='marble'){cv=_genMarble(f.base,f.vein,f.seed);mm=1100;}
  else if(f.kind==='concrete'){cv=_genConcrete(f.base,f.seed);mm=800;}
  else if(f.kind==='plain'){cv=_genPlain(f.base,f.seed,f.gloss?2.5:6.5);mm=280;}
  const out={kind:f.kind,map:cv?_mkTexFromCanvas(cv):null,mm,
    rough:f.kind==='wood'?(f.gloss?.34:.72):f.kind==='marble'?.3:f.kind==='concrete'?.92:(f.gloss?.22:.8)};
  _genCache[key]=out;
  return out;
}

function main(){
  clock=new THREE.Clock();
  if(CLIENT){ document.body.classList.add('client'); }
  loadBrandName();
  if(ARTICLE){loadModel(ARTICLE, FIRST_TARGET);}
  else{document.getElementById('welcome').style.display='flex';splashHide();}   // skaner kutish ekrani
}
function loadBrandName(){
  const _bu = CLIENT ? ('/api/k/'+encodeURIComponent(CLIENT.t)+'/brand')
                     : ('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/brand');
  fetch(_bu).then(r=>r.json()).then(b=>{
    if(b&&b.name){
      document.getElementById('wBrand').textContent=b.name;
      splashName(b.name);                       // splashda ham brend nomi
      if(b.logo){const lg=document.getElementById('wLogo');lg.src=b.logo;lg.style.display='block';}
    }
    // 2026-08-31: foydalanuvchi hali TIL TANLAMAGAN bo'lsa (yangi qurilma/mehmon) —
    // ssilka brendning ILOVADAGI tiliga o'tadi (dastlab 'uz' bilan chizilgan bo'lsa ham
    // qayta chiziladi — «ochilgan ssilkalar ham o'sha tilda bo'lishi kerak»).
    if(b&&b.lang&&LANGS.includes(b.lang)&&!LANG_EXPLICIT&&b.lang!==LANG){
      LANG=b.lang; applyLang();
    }
  }).catch(()=>{});
}

function setLoading(v){document.getElementById('loading').classList.toggle('on',v);}

/* ===== BREND SPLASH boshqaruvi ===== */
/* Brauzerga KADR chizishga imkon beradi. buildScene() asosiy oqimni ~10 s bloklaydi;
   undan oldin bu funksiyani kutmasak, "Model quriladi…" yozuvi va foiz ekranga
   CHIQMASDAN qotib qolardi (foydalanuvchi: "1-2 sekund animatsiya, keyin 10 s qotib turadi"). */
function nextPaint(){ return new Promise(r=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>setTimeout(r,0)))); }
let _splashDone=false;
let _spRelease=false;   // splash hali ekranda, lekin 3D chizishga ruxsat berildi
let _spWarm=0;          // splash ostida chizilgan "isitish" kadrlari (2 ta: qimmat + arzon)
let _spGone=false;      // splash DOM'dan olingan — render() endi uni qidirmaydi
let _introT0=0;         // modelga "yaqinlashish" animatsiyasi boshlangan payt (0 = o'chiq)
function splashName(txt){ const e=document.getElementById('spName'); if(e&&txt)e.textContent=txt; }
function splashText(txt){ const e=document.getElementById('spTxt'); if(e)e.textContent=txt; }
function splashProgress(frac){          // 0..1 — aniq foiz bo'lsa yugurish to'xtaydi
  const bar=document.getElementById('spBar'), fill=document.getElementById('spFill');
  if(!bar||!fill)return;
  if(frac==null){ bar.classList.remove('det'); return; }
  bar.classList.add('det');
  fill.style.transform='scaleX('+Math.max(0.04,Math.min(1,frac)).toFixed(3)+')';
}
/* XAVFSIZLIK TO'RI: biror sabab bilan (masalan 3D kutubxona yuklanmasa) model qurilmay
   qolsa ham splash abadiy turib qolmasin — 30 soniyadan keyin o'zi yopiladi. */
setTimeout(function(){ if(!_splashDone){ splashText(t('spSlow')); splashHide(); } },30000);
/* Brend splash ekranda KAMIDA shuncha ms turadi. Model tez qurilsa (kesh tufayli 2 s dan
   ham tez bo'lishi mumkin) logo miltillab o'tib ketardi — brend esda qolmaydi. Model
   ertaroq tayyor bo'lsa, qolgan vaqt ichida progress silliq 100% ga to'ladi. */
const SP_MIN_MS=3000;
let _spPend=false;
/* ===== YASHIRIN: zagruzka ekraniga bosilsa majburiy kutish o'tkazib yuboriladi =====
   (foydalanuvchi so'rovi 2026-08-31). Ataylab hech qanday yozuv/ishora ko'rsatilmaydi.
   Ikki holat: (a) model allaqachon tayyor va faqat SP_MIN_MS to'lishi kutilyapti —
   bosilishi bilan splash darhol yopiladi; (b) model hali yuklanyapti — bosish «tayyor
   bo'lishi bilan kutmasdan yop» degani bo'lib eslab qolinadi. Model o'rniga bo'sh ekran
   chiqib qolmasligi uchun BIRINCHI KADR kutilishi (_spWaitWarm) baribir saqlanadi. */
let _spSkip=false, _spTimer=0;
function splashSkip(){
  if(_spSkip||_splashDone)return;
  _spSkip=true;
  if(_spTimer){ clearTimeout(_spTimer); _spTimer=0; }
  if(_spPend){ _spPend=false; splashHide(); }
}
(function(){ const _sp=document.getElementById('splash');
  if(_sp)_sp.addEventListener('pointerdown', splashSkip, {passive:true}); })();
/* So'nishni BOSHLASHDAN oldin 3D sahnaning ikkita kadri chizilib bo'lishini kutamiz:
   birinchisi qimmat (shader kompilyatsiyasi), ikkinchisi arzon. Shundan keyingina so'nish
   boshlanadi — aks holda o'tish paytida asosiy oqim band bo'lib, 600 ms lik animatsiyaga
   atigi 1-2 kadr tushardi (o'lchov: 3 kadr; kutilgach 22-35 kadr). Cheklov: 2.5 s. */
function _spWaitWarm(){
  return new Promise(res=>{
    const t0=performance.now();
    (function tick(){
      if(_spWarm>=2 || performance.now()-t0>2500) return res();
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    })();
  });
}
function splashHide(){                  // model tayyor — yumshoq yo'qoladi
  if(_splashDone)return;
  const sp=document.getElementById('splash'); if(!sp){_splashDone=true;return;}
  const _t0=(typeof window.__spT0==='number'?window.__spT0:0);
  const _left=_spSkip?0:(SP_MIN_MS-(performance.now()-_t0));
  if(_left>0){                          // erta tayyor — minimal vaqt to'lguncha kutamiz
    if(!_spPend){
      _spPend=true;
      // Kutish vaqti BEKORGA ketmasin: eng qimmat BIRINCHI KADR (shader kompilyatsiyasi +
      // 500+ mesh) shu 3 soniya ichida, splash ostida chizilib bo'ladi. Natijada splash
      // so'nganda model allaqachon tayyor turadi — o'tish sakramaydi.
      _spRelease=true;
      { const _bx=sp.querySelector('.sp-box');          // o'tishdan oldin kompozitor qatlami
        if(_bx)_bx.style.willChange='transform,opacity,filter'; }
      const fill=document.getElementById('spFill');
      if(fill)fill.style.transition='transform '+Math.round(_left)+'ms linear';
      splashProgress(1); splashText(t('spReady'));
      _spTimer=setTimeout(()=>{ _spTimer=0; _spPend=false; splashHide(); },_left);
    }
    return;
  }
  _splashDone=true;
  splashProgress(1); splashText(t('spReady'));
  // MUHIM: splash ostida 3D sahnaning BIRINCHI KADRI chizilsin, keyin so'nish boshlansin.
  // Aks holda so'nish boshida bo'm-bo'sh kanvas ko'rinib, "sakrab" o'tgandek tuyulardi.
  _spRelease=true;
  { const _bx=sp.querySelector('.sp-box');
    if(_bx)_bx.style.willChange='transform,opacity,filter'; }
  _spWaitWarm().then(()=>{
    // kamera yumshoq yaqinlashadi (pastda, render() ichida) — "animatsiyani kamaytirish"
    // yoqilgan bo'lsa bu ham o'chadi
    try{ if(!matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) _introT0=performance.now(); }
    catch(e){ _introT0=performance.now(); }
    sp.classList.add('hide');
    setTimeout(()=>{ if(sp.parentNode)sp.parentNode.removeChild(sp); },700);
  });
}
/* Model JSON'ini OQIM bilan o'qiymiz: Content-Length bo'lsa haqiqiy foiz ko'rsatiladi
   (katta modelda foydalanuvchi qancha qolganini ko'rib turadi). Oqim qo'llab-quvvatlanmasa
   oddiy r.json() ga tushamiz. */
async function fetchModelJSON(url){
  const r=await fetch(url);
  if(!r.ok)throw new Error('Model topilmadi');
  const total=parseInt(r.headers.get('Content-Length')||'0',10);
  if(!r.body||!r.body.getReader||!total){ splashProgress(null); return r.json(); }
  const reader=r.body.getReader(); const chunks=[]; let got=0;
  const mb=n=>(n/1048576).toFixed(1);
  for(;;){
    const {done,value}=await reader.read();
    if(done)break;
    chunks.push(value); got+=value.length;
    splashProgress(got/total);
    splashText(mb(got)+' / '+mb(total)+' MB');
  }
  let buf=new Uint8Array(got), off=0;
  for(const c of chunks){ buf.set(c,off); off+=c.length; }
  return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(buf));
}

// 2D ALOHIDA SAHIFA: URL'da ?d2=<des> bo'lsa — sahifa to'liq 2D chizma rejimida ochiladi
// (3D UI yashirin, #drill butun ekran). true = d2 rejim ishga tushdi.
function maybeOpenD2(){
  let des=null; try{des=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('d2');}catch(e){}
  if(des==null||des==='')return false;
  const p=((DATA&&DATA.panels)||[]).find(x=>String(x.des)===String(des));
  if(!p)return false;
  document.body.classList.add('d2page');
  selectByDes(String(des));   // 3D holat ham mos bo'lsin (closeDrill qaytishда detal tanlangan qoladi)
  openDrillView(p);
  return true;
}

function loadModel(article, target){
  setLoading(true);
  splashText(t('spLoading'));
  const _mu = CLIENT ? ('/api/k/'+encodeURIComponent(CLIENT.t)+'/model')
                     : ('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/model/'+encodeURIComponent(article));
  fetchModelJSON(_mu).then(d=>{
    // mijoz rejimida keshga YOZILMAYDI (havola boshqa qurilmaga o'tishi mumkin)
    if(!CLIENT){ try{localStorage.setItem('m_'+SLUG+'_'+article,JSON.stringify(d));localStorage.setItem('last_'+SLUG,article);}catch(e){} }
    splashText(t('spBuild'));
    splashProgress(0);          // qurish bosqichi 0 dan boshlanadi
    return nextPaint().then(()=>{     // yozuv va foiz EKRANGA chiqsin, keyin bloklovchi qurish
      DATA=d;ARTICLE=article;
      return buildScene().then(()=>{
      if(target){ selectByDes(target); if(selected)flyToMesh(selected); }
      if(!CLIENT){
        loadDrawings(article);   // shu modelda Bazis chizmasi bor detallar
        loadModelFiles(article); // 📎 biriktirilgan fayllar (bo'lsa menyuda chiqadi)
        loadChert(article);      // 📐 yig'ish chizmasi bo'limi (faqat admin kaliti bilan)
      }
      setLoading(false);splashHide();
      if(!maybeOpenD2())startOnboarding();   // d2-sahifa emas: kirishda tugma nomlari + 10s idle qo'l imo-ishorasi
      if(SHOT){document.body.classList.add('shot');
        // bir necha kadr render bo'lgach (tekstura/env tayyor) tayyorlik bayrog'i
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{window.__shotReady=true;})));}
      });
    });
  }).catch(e=>{
    console.warn('[DetalQR] /api/model so\'rov xato:',e);
    const c=localStorage.getItem('m_'+SLUG+'_'+article);
    let _building=false;
    if(c){_building=true;DATA=JSON.parse(c);ARTICLE=article;
         buildScene().then(()=>{ if(target)selectByDes(target);toast(t('offline'));maybeOpenD2();splashHide(); });}
    else if(meshes.length){toast('✗ "'+article+'" '+t('modelMiss'),true);}  // model OCHIQ -> bloklamaymiz
    else{const el=document.getElementById('err');el.style.display='flex';el.textContent=e.message;}
    // MUHIM: oflayn keshdan qurish BOSHLANGAN bo'lsa splashni SHU YERDA yopmaymiz —
    // aks holda progress orqaga qaytib, model qurilib bo'lmasdan splash yo'qolardi
    // (buildScene() birinchi 'await' da qaytadi, ya'ni bu qator qurish o'rtasida ishlardi).
    setLoading(false); if(!_building)splashHide();
  });
}

// Fon — Bazis-Cloud (viewer.bazissoft.ru) uslubidagi quyoshli osmon gradienti:
// zenit to'yingan havorang -> ufqda oqish -> pastda neytral kulrang; yuqorida yumshoq
// quyosh halo'si. Och/bej detallar havorang osmonda aniq ajralib turadi.
let _bgTex=null, _groundGroup=null;
function bgTexture(){
  if(_bgTex)return _bgTex;
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=512;cv.height=512;
  const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  const g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,512);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#5d89ae');     // zenit — to'yingan havorang (Bazis #5682a8)
  g.addColorStop(0.28,'#7ea4bf');  // o'rta osmon
  g.addColorStop(0.46,'#aebfc6');  // ufq zonasi boshlanishi = FOG rangi
  g.addColorStop(0.64,'#acbac2');  // keng tekis ufq zonasi — yer cheti shu oraliqda singadi
  g.addColorStop(0.84,'#a3adb4');  // ufq osti
  g.addColorStop(1,'#9ba4ab');     // past — neytral kulrang (Bazis pastki zonasi)
  x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,512,512);
  // QUYOSH halo — yuqori markazda yumshoq oq porlash ("ochiq havo" hissi, Bazis kabi)
  const h=x.createRadialGradient(256,30,0,256,30,320);
  h.addColorStop(0,'rgba(238,247,252,0.8)');
  h.addColorStop(0.35,'rgba(228,240,248,0.3)');
  h.addColorStop(1,'rgba(228,240,248,0)');
  x.fillStyle=h;x.fillRect(0,0,512,512);
  _bgTex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
  return _bgTex;
}
// KONTAKT SOYA — model tagidagi yumshoq soya (yer bilan "bog'lanadi", osilib turmaydi)
let _shadowTex=null;
function shadowTexture(){
  if(_shadowTex)return _shadowTex;
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cv.height=128;
  const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  const g=x.createRadialGradient(64,64,3,64,64,64);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(24,34,48,0.5)');
  g.addColorStop(0.45,'rgba(24,34,48,0.26)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(24,34,48,0)');
  x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,128,128);
  _shadowTex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
  return _shadowTex;
}
// 3D OLAM (Bazis-Cloud uslubi): alohida yer tekisligi YO'Q — "yer" hissini bg gradientning
// pastki kulrang zonasi beradi (yassi disk ufqda doim keskin chiziq hosil qilardi).
// Model ostida faqat KONTAKT SOYA — model "yerga bog'lanadi", osilib turmaydi.
function addGround(box){
  const ctr=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const sz=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
  const floorY=box.min.y;
  scene.fog=null;   // fog ishlatilmaydi (yer yo'q — chiziq chiqmasin)
  // kontakt soya (model tagida, o'lchamiga mos; kichik disk — ufq chizig'i muammosi yo'q)
  const sg=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(sz.x*1.5+300,sz.z*1.5+300); sg.rotateX(-Math.PI/2);
  const sm=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:shadowTexture(),transparent:true,depthWrite:false,opacity:.85});
  const shadow=new THREE.Mesh(sg,sm);
  shadow.position.set(ctr.x,floorY+0.4,ctr.z); shadow.renderOrder=-2;
  scene.add(shadow);
  _groundGroup=[shadow];
}

// ---- 45° ZAPIL ("паз на ус"): qirra panel qalinligi bo'ylab QIYA kesilgan — shunda ikki
// detal burchakda 45° bo'lib tutashadi. JSON: p.bevels=[{ei:kontur qirra indeksi,
// a:A yuzada olinadigan en, b:B yuzada olinadigan en}]. Ya'ni panelning ikki yuzasi
// HAR XIL konturga ega. Shuning uchun oddiy ExtrudeGeometry (to'g'ri prizma) qurilgach,
// UZOQ yuz (z=dep) uchlari B konturiga ko'chiriladi -> yon yuza qiya bo'ladi.
// (Avval bu zapil to'g'ri to'rtburchak o'yiq bo'lib kesilardi: qirradan 9mm butunlay
// yo'qolib, detallar burchakda mos tushmasdi — "paz noto'g'ri, ustma-ust".)
function _insetPoly(poly,off){
  const n=poly.length;
  let ar=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%n];ar+=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1];}
  const s=(ar>0)?1:-1;                         // CCW -> ichki normal (-dy,dx)
  const ln=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%n];
    let dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];const L=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(L<1e-9)return null;
    dx/=L;dy/=L;
    const nx=-dy*s,ny=dx*s,d=off[i]||0;
    ln.push({x:a[0]+nx*d,y:a[1]+ny*d,dx,dy,nx,ny,d});
  }
  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const l1=ln[(i-1+n)%n],l2=ln[i];
    const den=l1.dx*l2.dy-l1.dy*l2.dx;
    if(Math.abs(den)<1e-9){out.push([poly[i][0]+l2.nx*l2.d,poly[i][1]+l2.ny*l2.d]);continue;}
    const t=((l2.x-l1.x)*l2.dy-(l2.y-l1.y)*l2.dx)/den;
    out.push([l1.x+l1.dx*t,l1.y+l1.dy*t]);
  }
  // XAVFSIZLIK: siljish detal o'lchamidan katta bo'lsa kontur "ag'darilib" ketadi
  // (qirralar teskari yo'nalishga o'tadi) — bunda zapil chizilmaydi (to'g'ri prizma qoladi)
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=out[i],b=out[(i+1)%n];
    if((b[0]-a[0])*ln[i].dx+(b[1]-a[1])*ln[i].dy<-0.01)return null;
  }
  return out;
}
// QISMAN zapil/yumaloqlash (2026-08-30, report #42 — 1/206501 detal 4: 1995 mm qirraning
// 1000 mm ida 45° zapil): JSON'da b.t0/b.t1 — qirra bo'ylab oraliq (mm). Qirra bo'laklarga
// bo'linadi, bevel faqat [t0,t1] bo'lagiga. Uchlarida 0.1 mm li "pog'ona" bo'lak — tik
// to'xtash yuzasi (_insetPoly kollinear tugunda KEYINGI qirra siljishini oladi: shuning
// uchun boshdagi pog'ona bevelsiz, oxiridagi pog'ona bevelli). t0/t1 yo'q = butun qirra.
function bevelSplit(p){
  const poly=p.poly||[],n=poly.length;
  const bv=(p.bevels||[]).filter(b=>b&&b.ei>=0&&b.ei<n);
  if(!bv.some(b=>b.t0!=null||b.t1!=null))return {poly,bevels:bv};
  const EPS=0.1;
  const edgeL=i=>{const a=poly[i],c=poly[(i+1)%n];return Math.hypot(c[0]-a[0],c[1]-a[1]);};
  const rng=b=>{const L=edgeL(b.ei);let t0=b.t0==null?0:+b.t0,t1=b.t1==null?L:+b.t1;
    t0=Math.max(0,Math.min(L,t0));t1=Math.max(0,Math.min(L,t1));return [t0,t1,L];};
  const cutsAt=Array.from({length:n},()=>[]);
  for(const b of bv){const [t0,t1,L]=rng(b);
    if(t0>0.5)cutsAt[b.ei].push(t0,t0+EPS);
    if(t1<L-0.5)cutsAt[b.ei].push(t1-EPS,t1);}
  const poly2=[],map=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=poly[i],c=poly[(i+1)%n],L=edgeL(i);
    const ts=[...new Set(cutsAt[i].map(t=>Math.round(t*100)/100))].filter(t=>t>0.01&&t<L-0.01).sort((x,y)=>x-y);
    map[i]={start:poly2.length,bounds:[0,...ts,L]};
    poly2.push(a);
    for(const t of ts)poly2.push([a[0]+(c[0]-a[0])*t/L,a[1]+(c[1]-a[1])*t/L]);
  }
  const bevels2=[];
  for(const b of bv){
    const [t0,t1]=rng(b),m=map[b.ei],lo=(b.t0==null?-1:t0+EPS-0.02),hi=t1+0.02;
    for(let k=0;k<m.bounds.length-1;k++){const s0=m.bounds[k],s1=m.bounds[k+1];
      if(s0>=lo&&s1<=hi)bevels2.push(Object.assign({},b,{ei:m.start+k}));}
  }
  return {poly:poly2,bevels:bevels2};
}
// har KONTUR QIRRASI uchun: A yuzada va B yuzada ichkariga qancha kirishi
// (qisman zapil bo'lsa — BO'LINGAN kontur uchun; .poly qaytariladi)
function bevelOffsets(p){
  if(!p.bevels||!p.bevels.length||!p.poly||p.poly.length<3)return null;
  const sp=bevelSplit(p),poly=sp.poly;
  const n=poly.length,oa=new Array(n).fill(0),ob=new Array(n).fill(0);let any=false;
  for(const b of sp.bevels){
    const i=b.ei;if(!(i>=0&&i<n))continue;
    oa[i]=Math.max(oa[i],+b.a||0);ob[i]=Math.max(ob[i],+b.b||0);any=true;
  }
  return any?{oa,ob,poly}:null;
}
function bevelFaces(p){
  const o=bevelOffsets(p);if(!o)return null;
  const A=_insetPoly(o.poly,o.oa),B=_insetPoly(o.poly,o.ob);
  return (A&&B)?{A,B,poly:o.poly}:null;
}
// kromka segmenti (butt.seg) qaysi kontur qirrasi ustida yotadi -> indeks (yo'q bo'lsa -1).
// butt.elem kontur ELEMENT indeksi (yoy/doira ham sanaladi), bevel.ei esa poly qirrasi —
// ular har doim mos kelavermaydi, shuning uchun GEOMETRIYA bo'yicha topamiz.
function bevelEdgeOfSeg(poly,seg){
  const n=poly.length;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%n];
    let dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];const L=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(L<1e-6)continue;
    dx/=L;dy/=L;
    let ok=true;
    for(const q of seg){
      const t=(q[0]-a[0])*dx+(q[1]-a[1])*dy;         // qirra bo'ylab masofa
      const d=Math.abs((q[0]-a[0])*(-dy)+(q[1]-a[1])*dx);   // chiziqdan chetlanish
      if(d>1.0||t<-1.0||t>L+1.0){ok=false;break;}
    }
    if(ok)return i;
  }
  return -1;
}
// ExtrudeGeometry INDEKSSIZ (har uchburchak o'z uchlari bilan) -> uzoq yuzdagi kontur
// uchlarini ko'chirish xavfsiz; normallar qayta hisoblanadi (qiya yuza to'g'ri yoritiladi)
function applyBevel(geo,fA,fB,dep){
  const pos=geo.attributes&&geo.attributes.position;if(!pos)return;
  const key=(x,y)=>Math.round(x*20)+'|'+Math.round(y*20);
  const map=new Map();
  for(let i=0;i<fA.length;i++)map.set(key(fA[i][0],fA[i][1]),fB[i]);
  for(let i=0;i<pos.count;i++){
    if(Math.abs(pos.getZ(i)-dep)>0.01)continue;
    const t=map.get(key(pos.getX(i),pos.getY(i)));
    if(t)pos.setXY(i,t[0],t[1]);
  }
  pos.needsUpdate=true;geo.computeVertexNormals();
}

// PANEL KONTUR QIRRALARI — poly + cuts KONTURLARIDAN to'g'ridan-to'g'ri quriladi.
// SABAB: teshikli (cut/slot) shaklni `ExtrudeGeometry` triangulyatsiya qilganда earcut
// har teshikni tashqi chegaraga "ko'prik" (seam) qirra bilan bog'laydi. `EdgesGeometry`
// bu ko'priklarni chizib qo'yardi -> "ichki kontur bilan tashqi kontur ORASIGA begona
// liniya" (foydalanuvchi shikoyati). Konturdan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri qurish bu artefaktni
// BUTUNLAY yo'qotadi. Natija = tashqi kontur + har cut konturi (old+orqa loop) + burchak
// vertikallari (qalinlik silueti). Faqat oddiy poly panellarga (box/bevel/subtract EMAS).
// QALINLIGI 0 bo'lgan panel (Bazisda o'lchami ko'rsatilmagan oyna/ko'zgu qo'shimchasi,
// masalan «Зеркало Блюм») — ilgari 16mm qilib chizilardi va yonidagi HAQIQIY oyna bilan
// kesishib ketardi (deluxe/634-01: ko'zgu z -20..-4, oyna z -5..0 -> z-fighting, ikkalasi
// ham shaffof bo'lgani uchun "vijirlash"). Endi YUPQA (1mm) qilib chiziladi: ko'rinadi,
// lekin qo'shni detal ichiga kirmaydi.
// (thick JSONда matn bo'lishi ham mumkin — '16.0'/'0.0', shuning uchun Number bilan.)
function panelDep(p){
  const v=Number(p&&p.thick);
  if(isFinite(v)&&Math.abs(v)>0.001) return Math.abs(v);
  return isFinite(v)?1:16;      // 0 -> yupqa 1mm; qiymat umuman yo'q -> eski 16mm
}
function contourEdges(p){
  const dep=panelDep(p);
  const z0=p.thick<0?-dep:0, z1=p.thick<0?0:dep;
  const pos=[];
  const loop=(pts)=>{ const n=pts.length; for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ const a=pts[i], b=pts[(i+1)%n];
    pos.push(a[0],a[1],z1, b[0],b[1],z1);   // old yuza konturi
    pos.push(a[0],a[1],z0, b[0],b[1],z0);   // orqa yuza konturi
  }};
  const verts=(pts)=>{ const n=pts.length; for(let i=0;i<n;i++){    // burchakda (>~25°) vertikal = qalinlik qirrasi
    const a=pts[(i-1+n)%n], b=pts[i], c=pts[(i+1)%n];
    const v1x=b[0]-a[0],v1y=b[1]-a[1], v2x=c[0]-b[0],v2y=c[1]-b[1];
    const l1=Math.hypot(v1x,v1y)||1, l2=Math.hypot(v2x,v2y)||1;
    if((v1x*v2x+v1y*v2y)/(l1*l2)<0.9) pos.push(b[0],b[1],z0, b[0],b[1],z1);
  }};
  const _cutLoop=(cut)=>{
    let pts=null;
    if(cut.t==='poly'&&cut.pts&&cut.pts.length>=3) pts=cut.pts;
    else if(cut.t==='circle'&&cut.r>0){ pts=[]; const seg=Math.min(48,Math.max(16,Math.round(cut.r)));
      for(let i=0;i<seg;i++){const a=2*Math.PI*i/seg; pts.push([cut.x+cut.r*Math.cos(a),cut.y+cut.r*Math.sin(a)]);} }
    if(pts){ loop(pts); verts(pts); }
  };
  const outer=p.poly; loop(outer); verts(outer);
  for(const cut of (p.cuts||[])) _cutLoop(cut);
  for(const pc of (p.pieces||[])){          // ajralgan qo'shimcha bo'laklar ham konturli bo'lsin
