    if(pc&&pc.poly&&pc.poly.length>=3){ loop(pc.poly); verts(pc.poly); }
    for(const cut of ((pc&&pc.cuts)||[])) _cutLoop(cut);
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));
  return g;
}

// KONTUR PO TRAEKTORII (Type 2001, обклад/карона): kesim (sec, u-v) yo'l (path, x-y,
// panel-lokal tekislik) bo'ylab suriladi. Burchaklarda MITER birikma (biseektrisa bo'ylab,
// 1/cos kompensatsiya) — haqiqiy obkladdek toza burchak. v o'qi lokal +Z ga chiqadi.
function buildSweepGeo(sw){
  const sec=sw.sec, path=sw.path, closed=!!sw.closed, n=path.length, S=sec.length;
  const dirs=[];
  const nseg=closed?n:n-1;
  for(let i=0;i<nseg;i++){ const a=path[i], b=path[(i+1)%n];
    const dx=b[0]-a[0], dy=b[1]-a[1], L=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1; dirs.push([dx/L,dy/L]); }
  const pos=[],uv=[],idx=[];
  // UV = MILLIMETRDA: u = yo'l bo'ylab bosib o'tilgan uzunlik, v = kesim v (en).
  // Sabab: oddiy panel (ExtrudeGeometry) UV'si mm da bo'ladi va material masshtabi
  // 1/mm bilan beriladi. Sweep avval 0..1 UV berardi -> tekstura butun detalga BITTA
  // marta cho'zilib, bukilgan panelda yog'och tolasi ulkan bo'lib chiqardi.
  // mm UV bukilgan panelda AYNAN yoyib chizilgan zagotovkaning koordinatasini beradi —
  // ya'ni tekstura yassi zagotovkaga bosilib, keyin bukilgandek ko'rinadi (haqiqatda ham shunday).
  let _len=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    let d0,d1;
    if(closed){ d0=dirs[(i-1+nseg)%nseg]; d1=dirs[i%nseg]; }
    else { d0=dirs[Math.max(0,i-1)]; d1=dirs[Math.min(nseg-1,i)]; }
    if(i>0){ const a=path[i-1], b=path[i]; _len+=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0], b[1]-a[1]); }
    const n0=[-d0[1],d0[0]], n1=[-d1[1],d1[0]];
    let mx=n0[0]+n1[0], my=n0[1]+n1[1]; const ml=Math.hypot(mx,my); let sc=1;
    if(ml<1e-6){ mx=n1[0]; my=n1[1]; }
    else { mx/=ml; my/=ml; sc=1/Math.max(0.35, mx*n1[0]+my*n1[1]); }   // miter kompensatsiya (<=~70° oʻtkir burchak chegarasi)
    for(let j=0;j<S;j++){ const u=sec[j][0], v=sec[j][1];
      pos.push(path[i][0]+mx*sc*u, path[i][1]+my*sc*u, v);
      uv.push(_len, v);
    }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<nseg;i++){ const i2=(i+1)%n;
    for(let j=0;j<S;j++){ const j2=(j+1)%S;
      const a=i*S+j, b=i*S+j2, c=i2*S+j, d=i2*S+j2;
      idx.push(a,b,d, a,d,c);
    }
  }
  if(!closed){   // uch qopqoqlari (yelpig'ich — kesim asosan qavariq)
    const b1=(n-1)*S;
    for(let j=1;j<S-1;j++){ idx.push(0,j+1,j); idx.push(b1,b1+j,b1+j+1); }
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos,3));
  g.setAttribute('uv',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uv,2));
  g.setIndex(idx); g.computeVertexNormals();
  return g;
}

async function buildScene(){
  window.__modelOpen=true;   // SW qayta yuklashi endi foydalanuvchi ishini buzmaydi (pastga qara)
  if(!renderer){
    const canvas=document.getElementById('c3d');
    renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas,antialias:true,powerPreference:'high-performance'});
    // CHIQISH RANG FAZOSI: teksturalar sRGB deb o'qiladi (getTex: encoding=sRGBEncoding),
    // shuning uchun render natijasi ham sRGB bo'lishi SHART. Aks holda sRGB->lineynыy
    // o'girish qaytarilmay qolib, dekor Bazisдagiga nisbatan TO'Q chiqardi.
    if(THREE.sRGBEncoding!==undefined)renderer.outputEncoding=THREE.sRGBEncoding;
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio,1.75));
    raycaster=new THREE.Raycaster();
    addControls(canvas);addEventListener('resize',resize);
    initViewCube();
    renderer.setAnimationLoop(render);
  }
  document.getElementById('welcome').style.display='none';   // model keldi — kutish ekrani yopiladi
  // sahnani yangidan qurish (yangi model kelganda)
  scene=new THREE.Scene();scene.background=bgTexture();   // tekis oq emas — gradient (och detal fon bilan qorishmasin)
  camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(50,innerWidth/innerHeight,1,100000);
  resize();   // kamera yaratilgach o'lcham/aspect o'rnatiladi (avval camera undefined edi → xato)
  // YORITISH — TEKIS (Bazisдagi kabi): pastdan ham yorug'lik keladi, shuning uchun
  // detalning TAGI qoraymaydi; yuqoridan tushuvchi nur kamaytirilgan, shuning uchun UST
  // yuzalar «yaltirab» ketmaydi. (Avval: yer rangi 0x303840 — to'q ko'k, dl=0.7 → tag qora,
  // ust yorqin edi.)
  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff,0xc2c8ce,0.92));
  const dl=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.42);dl.position.set(1,2,1.5);scene.add(dl);
  const dl2=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.24);dl2.position.set(-1.5,0.6,-1);scene.add(dl2);
  const dl3=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.16);dl3.position.set(0.2,-1.5,0.4);scene.add(dl3);  // pastdan to'ldiruvchi
  const env=getEnv();if(env)scene.environment=env;   // glyanets/oyna/metall uchun muhit aksi
  model=new THREE.Group();scene.add(model);meshes=[];selected=null;
  selectedFurn=null;_furnHi=null;furnMeshList=[];   // furnitura tanlovi holatini tozalash
  const box=new THREE.Box3();
  const showEdges=DATA.panels.length<=1500;   // qirralar (kontur) — faqat juda katta modeldagina o'tkaziladi
  assignMaterialColors(DATA.panels);        // har materialga alohida rang

  // QURISH BOSQICHI PROGRESSI: 1000+ panelli modelda qurish bir necha soniya oladi va
  // foydalanuvchi "Model qurilmoqda…" da qotib qolgandek his qilardi. Endi har ~90 ms da
  // brauzerga kadr beriladi va splashda "320 / 1051 detal" ko'rinib turadi.
  const _bTotal=DATA.panels.length; let _bDone=0, _bLast=performance.now();
  for(const p of DATA.panels){
    if(performance.now()-_bLast>350){
      // TO'XTASH NARXI: har rAF kutishi sekin qurilmada ~90 ms turadi. 120 ms da bir
      // to'xtaganda 71 ta to'xtash = 6.3 s ORTIQCHA vaqt (o'lchangan). 350 ms — hisoblagich
      // ~3 marta/sek yangilanadi (yetarli), ortiqcha vaqt esa uch barobar kamayadi.
      // Bar va logo animatsiyasi kompozitorda, ular to'xtashlardan qat'i nazar silliq.
      splashText(t('spBuild')+'  '+_bDone+' / '+_bTotal);
      splashProgress(_bDone/Math.max(1,_bTotal));
      await new Promise(r=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>r()));
      _bLast=performance.now();
    }
    _bDone++;
    let geo, isBox=false;
    if(p.sphere&&p.sphere.r>0){
      // SFERA tanasi — markazi p.pos, radiusi p.sphere.r (report #34).
      geo=new THREE.SphereGeometry(p.sphere.r,28,20);
    }else if(p.sweep&&p.sweep.sec&&p.sweep.sec.length>=3&&p.sweep.path&&p.sweep.path.length>=2){
      geo=buildSweepGeo(p.sweep);            // kontur po traektorii (обклад/карона)
    }else if(p.poly&&p.poly.length>=3){
      const bv=bevelFaces(p);            // 45° zapil bo'lsa: A yuza konturi (B keyin ko'chiriladi)
      const base=bv?bv.A:p.poly;
      const sh=new THREE.Shape();
      sh.moveTo(base[0][0],base[0][1]);
      for(let i=1;i<base.length;i++)sh.lineTo(base[i][0],base[i][1]);
      sh.closePath();
      // Ichkaridagi o'yiq -> HOLE (tez); chegaraga tegadigan/chiqib ketadigani -> CSG
      const _edgeCuts=[];
      for(const cut of (p.cuts||[])){
        if(_cutOnEdge(cut,base)) _edgeCuts.push(cut);
        else sh.holes.push(cutPath(cut));          // teshib o'tuvchi o'yiq
      }
      // NOTE: botiq cho'ntak (p.pockets) keyinroq qo'shiladi — hozircha o'tkazib yuboramiz
      const dep=panelDep(p);   // profilda uzunlik manfiy bo'lishi mumkin
      // AJRALGAN QO'SHIMCHA BO'LAKLAR (p.pieces): bitta Bazis detalining kesimi bir nechta
      // ajralgan shakldan iborat bo'lsa (masalan ikkita alohida truba) — hammasi AYNI
      // geometriyaga qo'shiladi (ExtrudeGeometry shakllar RO'YXATINI qabul qiladi).
      let _shapes=null;
      if(p.pieces&&p.pieces.length){
        _shapes=[sh];
        for(const pc of p.pieces){
          const pp=pc&&pc.poly;
          if(!pp||pp.length<3)continue;
          const s2=new THREE.Shape();
          s2.moveTo(pp[0][0],pp[0][1]);
          for(let i=1;i<pp.length;i++)s2.lineTo(pp[i][0],pp[i][1]);
          s2.closePath();
          for(const cut of (pc.cuts||[])) s2.holes.push(cutPath(cut));
          _shapes.push(s2);
        }
      }
      geo=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(_shapes||sh,{depth:dep,bevelEnabled:false});   // teshik default 12 segment (yuklama kam)
      if(bv)applyBevel(geo,bv.A,bv.B,dep);    // uzoq yuz -> B konturi (qiya yon yuza)
      if(_edgeCuts.length)geo=applyEdgeCuts(geo,p,_edgeCuts,dep);   // chegaradagi o'yiqlar
      if(p.thick<0)geo.translate(0,0,-dep);   // teskari yo'nalishda extrude
    }else{
      isBox=true;
      geo=new THREE.BoxGeometry(p.w||100,p.h||100,p.thick||16);
      geo.translate((p.w||100)/2,(p.h||100)/2,(p.thick||16)/2);
    }
    // Вычитание тел: ayirma qurollarini base'dan HAQIQIY o'yish (Boolean Subtract)
    if(geo && p.subtract && p.subtract.length){ geo=applyCSGSubtract(geo,p,p.subtract); isBox=false; }
    // QURILISH/MAKETLASH devori (построение) — yarim shaffof fon: mebelni bekitmaydi,
    // tanlanmaydi (meshes'ga qo'shilmaydi), ro'yxatda yo'q, kamera kadriga ta'sir qilmaydi (box kengaymaydi)
    // FAQAT KATTA (xona-devori kabi) elementlar. Kichik "layout" panel (masalan materiali
    // "Сталь для построение" bo'lgan gola/ruchka profili) aslida HAQIQIY detal — normal chiziladi.
    // DEVOR = ikkala YUZA o'lchami katta (qalinligi ahamiyatsiz: devor 50-200 mm bo'ladi).
    //   max(w,h)>=350  -> uzun INGICHKA profil (20x1082) ham "devor" bo'lib yashirinib qolardi
    //                     (mebelrum/1034 da 18 ta "Профиль" detali ro'yxatdan yo'qolgandi);
    //   min(w,h,thick)>=350 -> haqiqiy devorni (yupqa, lekin katta) TOPOLMASDI.
    const _bigLayout = p.layout && ((!(typeof p.anim==='number'&&p.anim>=0) && !(p.group||'')) || Math.min(p.w||0, p.h||0, Math.abs(p.thick||0)) >= 350);
    if(_bigLayout){
      const lm=new THREE.Mesh(geo,new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xc7ccd3,roughness:.92,metalness:0}));
      lm.position.set(p.pos[0],p.pos[1],p.pos[2]);
      lm.quaternion.set(p.quat[0],p.quat[1],p.quat[2],p.quat[3]);
      lm.userData=p;lm.renderOrder=-1;
      model.add(lm);
      continue;
    }
    // Profil (2004 pogonaj) ODATDA metall bo'ladi (alyumin/qora rels, truba, gola),
    // LEKIN unga dekor material biriktirilgan bo'lsa (обои/плитка/yog'och/mramor/oyna)
    // — o'sha dekor ko'rinishida chizilishi kerak, metall qilib emas. profMetal=true
    // faqat material metall/tanilmagan/bo'sh bo'lgan profil uchun.
    let profMetal=true;
    if(p.profile){
      const m=(p.mat||'').toLowerCase();
      const pf=_matFinish(p.mat||'');
      // dekor sirt (обои/плитка/кафель) yoki tekstura/shaffof beruvchi material
      // (yog'och/mramor/beton/oyna/ko'zgu) biriktirilgan profil -> dekor ko'rinishi.
      // Faqat rang (Черный) yoki metall nomi (алюмин, хром) -> metall bo'lib qoladi.
      const decorSurface = /обои|обой|oboy|кафел|kafel|плитк/.test(m);
      const decorKind = pf.kind==='wood'||pf.kind==='marble'||pf.kind==='concrete'
                        ||pf.kind==='glass'||pf.kind==='mirror';
      // PLITA materiali (ЛДСП/МДФ/ХДФ...) profil bo'lsa ham METALL EMAS (report #34).
      const boardMat = /лдсп|лмдф|мдф|хдф|дсп|двп|массив|шпон|фанер|плита|ldsp|mdf|hdf/.test(m);
      profMetal = !(decorSurface || decorKind || boardMat);
    }
    // REAL tekstura (skript/ZIP yuborgan rasm, DATA.tex_meta sozlamalari bilan) —
    // protsedural generatsiyadan USTUN. p.col = odnotonniy rang (palitradan tanlangan).
    // FAQAT 'real_tex' belgili modellarда (skript v5.2+ / ZIP orqali, bugundan) — ESKI
    // modellar (belgisiz) avvalgidek protsedural, o'zgarmaydi.
    const REAL_TEX=!!(DATA&&DATA.real_tex);
    // `grain` (tola yo'nalishi) ishonchlimi? 2026-09-03 gacha .b3d o'quvchi uni
    // buzib, hamma panelga 2 yozardi — o'shanda zaxira qoida ishlaydi.
    const GRAIN_OK=!!(DATA&&DATA.grain_ok);
    const TM=(DATA&&DATA.tex_meta)||{};
    let realMap=null, realRough=.72;
    if(REAL_TEX && p.tex && !p.col && !(p.profile&&profMetal)){
      const ts=TM[p.tex]||{tw:1000,th:1000,o:0};
      realMap=getTex(p.tex,p.mat||'');
      // tekstura naqshi (yog'och tolasi) panelning UZUN tomoni bo'ylab yotadi; Bazis orient
      // (o=1 -> vertikal) buni teskari aylantiradi. (Avval 90° teskari edi — tuzatildi.)
      // TOLA YO'NALISHI: Bazis `TextureOrientation` panelning O'Z xossasi (p.grain):
      //   1 = tola panel lokal X bo'ylab, 2 = lokal Y bo'ylab.
      // Avval bu qiymat REAL tekstura yo'lida E'TIBORGA OLINMASDAN, material orient'i +
      // panel proporsiyasidan taxmin qilinardi — shu sabab baland fasadда (masalan «Дуб
      // Честерфильд» 331×695, grain=2) tola ENIGA yotib qolardi.
      const long=(p.w||0)>=(p.h||0);       // uzun tomon X (gorizontal panel)mi?
      // RASMDA tola qaysi o'q bo'ylab yotgani ham muhim: dekor rasmlari ikki xil bo'ladi
      // (ts.g: 0 = rasm X bo'ylab, 1 = rasm Y bo'ylab — server o'lchab yozadi).
      // Kerakli natija: p.grain===2 bo'lsa tola panel BALANDLIGI bo'ylab, aks holda eni bo'ylab.
      const gdir=(ts.g===0||ts.g===1)?ts.g:0;
      // YOTIQ PANEL (krishka/dno/polka) — 2026-09-03, BAZIS ETALONI bo'yicha tuzatildi.
      // Dalil: `.b3d` fayli ichida Bazisning O'Z preview rasmi bor. Ikki modelda
      // tekshirildi: «Комод» krishka 1102×370 (grain=2) va «FAYZULLO_OKA» krisha
      // 2020×600 (grain=2, uzun tomoni dunyo Z bo'ylab) — IKKALASIDA ham Bazis tolani
      // UZUN tomon bo'ylab chizadi, konturning Y o'qi bo'ylab EMAS. Sababi: yotiq
      // panelda «вертикально» yo'nalish yo'q. Vertikal panellarda esa (fasad 1098×199,
      // bok 350×884) grain=2 aynan kontur Y bo'ylab — u yerda eski mantiq to'g'ri.
      // ZAXIRA (faqat ESKI modellarda, DATA.grain_ok yo'q bo'lsa): eski .b3d o'quvchi
      // TexDir'ni buzib, HAMMA panelga grain=2 yozardi. Bunday ma'lumotda yotiq panel
      // (krishka/dno/polka) uchun tolani UZUN tomon bo'ylab qo'yamiz — Bazis o'z
      // preview'ida shunday chizadi. Yangi (grain_ok) modellarda grain'ga TO'G'RIDAN
      // ishonamiz, hech qanday taxmin qilmaymiz.
      const flat=(!GRAIN_OK)&&isFlatPanel(p);
      let wantY;                            // tola panel LOKAL Y o'qi bo'ylab yotsinmi?
      if(flat)                  wantY=(p.grain===1)?false:((p.h||0)>(p.w||0));
      else if(p.grain===1)      wantY=false;
      else if(p.grain===2)      wantY=true;
      else                      wantY=null;              // ma'lumot yo'q — eski taxmin
      let swap;
      if(wantY!==null)     swap=(wantY!==(gdir===1));
      else                 swap=(ts.o===1)?!long:long;   // ma'lumot yo'q — eski taxmin
      const tw=ts.tw||1000, th=ts.th||1000, ang=ts.ang||0;
      // ОРИЕНТАЦИЯ — Bazis tekstura muharriridagi 90\u00b0 qadamli burilish (ts.r).
      // Muammo #79: foydalanuvchi muharrirda 90\u00b0 qo'ygan, biz o'qimasdik.
      // 90/270 = o'qlarni almashtirish (transpoza) — bu `swap` ning teskarisi;
      // 180 esa yotqizilgan naqshda ko'zga ilinmaydi (bir xil tayl qaytariladi).
      if(ts.r===90||ts.r===270) swap=!swap;
      // UV panel LOKAL koordinatasidan (fromPos) -> tekstura TOREC/kromkaga ham davom etadi.
      // SWEEP (bukilgan panel / molding) da esa position.xy = BUKILISH profili, zagotovka
      // koordinatasi EMAS — u yerda geometriya o'zi bergan mm UV (yoyilgan uzunlik × en) to'g'ri.
      const _mir=ts.mir?1:0;
      // `ts.s` («Растянуть») ATAYLAB e'tiborga olinmaydi — yuqoridagi izohga qarang.
      bakeUV(geo,1/tw,1/th,swap,ang,!p.sweep,_mir);
      if(KROMKA_NEW){const _c=polyCenter(p);bakeSideUV(geo,1/tw,1/th,_c&&_c[0],_c&&_c[1],p.quat,gdir===1);}   // torec: tola kromka UZUNLIGI bo'ylab
      p._gy=(swap?(gdir===0):(gdir===1))?1:0;       // tola panel LOKAL Y bo'ylab yotadimi
    }
    // PROTSEDURAVIY tekstura: material NOMIdan generatsiya (real tekstura/rang bo'lmaganda)
    const _hasCol=(REAL_TEX && p.col && !_emptyCol(p.col));
    const fin=(realMap||_hasCol||(p.profile&&profMetal))?null:genMatTex(p.mat||'');
    if(fin&&fin.map){
      // Tola yo'nalishi. Bazis "TexDir" (p.grain) panelning LOKAL o'qini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri
      // belgilaydi: 1 -> lokal-X (kontur eni), 2 -> lokal-Y (kontur bo'yi) bo'ylab — o'lchamdan
      // QAT'I NAZAR (baland eshik/стойка TexDir=2 => tola VERTIKAL). grain yo'q (eski modellar)
      // bo'lsa geometrik taxmin: tola uzun tomon bo'ylab.
      let swap=(p.h||0)>(p.w||0);
      if(p.grain===1) swap=false; else if(p.grain===2) swap=true;
      // ZAXIRA (eski, grain_ok'siz modellar): yotiq panelda tola uzun tomon bo'ylab.
      if(!GRAIN_OK && isFlatPanel(p) && p.grain!==1) swap=(p.h||0)>(p.w||0);
      p._gy=swap?1:0;                    // protsedural naqshda swap=true -> tola lokal Y bo'ylab
      if(isBox) bakeUV(geo,(p.w||100)/fin.mm,(p.h||100)/fin.mm,swap);   // Box UV 0..1
      else    { bakeUV(geo,1/fin.mm,1/fin.mm,swap);
                if(KROMKA_NEW){const _c=polyCenter(p);bakeSideUV(geo,1/fin.mm,1/fin.mm,_c&&_c[0],_c&&_c[1],p.quat);} }  // Extrude UV = mm
    }
    // Bazisda "zamena material" (замена материала) — hali material biriktirilmagan,
    // vaqtinchalik o'rin-bosar belgisi (haqiqiy dekor emas). Bazisdagi sozlamasiga mos —
    // butunlay shaffof (ko'rinmaydi), lekin tanlansa (ro'yxatdan) ODATDAGIDEK ko'rinadi.
    const isZamena=/zamena\s*material|замена\s*материал/i.test(p.mat||'');
    // odnotonniy rang -> '#rrggbb' hex son (Bazis palitrasidan tanlangan) — faqat real_tex modelда
    const colHex = (REAL_TEX && p.col && !_emptyCol(p.col)) ? parseInt(String(p.col).replace('#',''),16) : null;
    let mopt;
    if(isZamena)                 mopt={color:0xffffff,roughness:.82,metalness:.05,transparent:true,opacity:0,depthWrite:false};
    else if(colHex!=null)        mopt={color:colHex,roughness:.8,metalness:.05,transparent:true};   // odnotonniy rang
    else if(realMap)             mopt={color:(tintHex(p.tint)||0xffffff),map:realMap,roughness:realRough,metalness:0,transparent:true};
    else if(p.profile&&profMetal) mopt={color:matColor(p.mat||''),roughness:.35,metalness:.85,transparent:true};  // metall pogonaj (alyumin/qora rels)
    else if(fin.map)             mopt={color:(tintHex(p.tint)||0xffffff),map:fin.map,roughness:fin.rough,metalness:0,transparent:true};
    else if(fin.kind==='mirror') mopt={color:0xd3dade,roughness:.06,metalness:.9,transparent:true};
    else if(fin.kind==='glass')  mopt={color:0xbcd2e2,roughness:.1,metalness:.2,transparent:true,opacity:.55,depthWrite:false};
    else if(fin.kind==='metal')  mopt={color:fin.mcol||0xb8bcc2,roughness:.3,metalness:.85,transparent:true};
    else                         mopt={color:matColor(p.mat||''),roughness:.82,metalness:.05,transparent:true};
    // OYNA/SHISHA: rangli (colHex) yoki real teksturali panelda `fin` null bo'ladi,
    // shu sabab yuqoridagi fin.kind==='glass' shohobchasi ishlamay qolardi -> oyna
    // opacity:1 bilan, ya'ni xira chizilardi. Material turini qayta aniqlab, shaffoflikni
    // majburan qo'yamiz. Rang/tekstura saqlanadi. p.alpha - skript haqiqiy qiymat yuborsa.
    const _fk=(fin&&fin.kind)||((typeof _matFinish==='function')?_matFinish(p.mat||'').kind:null);
    if(_fk==='glass' && !isZamena){
      const _a=(typeof p.alpha==='number'&&p.alpha>=0&&p.alpha<=1)?p.alpha:.55;
      mopt.transparent=true; mopt.opacity=_a; mopt.depthWrite=false; p._glass=true;
      if(!realMap){   // teksturasiz oyna - silliq shisha; teksturali oyna o'z sirtida qoladi
        if(mopt.roughness===undefined||mopt.roughness>.15) mopt.roughness=.1;
        if(mopt.metalness===undefined||mopt.metalness<.2)  mopt.metalness=.2;
      }
    }
    p._op=mopt.opacity!==undefined?mopt.opacity:1;      // tanlov bekor qilinganda qaytadigan asl shaffoflik
    p._dw=mopt.depthWrite!==undefined?mopt.depthWrite:true;
    const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(mopt);
    _sRGBFix(mat);
    // matoviy teksturali yuzada muhit aksi kuchli "oqartirib" yuboradi — kamaytiramiz
    // (glyanetsda o'rtacha, oyna/ko'zgu/metallda to'liq qoladi)
    // muhit aksi — matoviy dekorda deyarli ko'rinmasin (yaltiroqlik faqat glyanets/oyna/metallda)
    if(fin&&fin.map)mat.envMapIntensity=fin.gloss?0.35:0.14;
    else if(realMap)mat.envMapIntensity=0.14;  // real tekstura yuzasida aks kuchaymasin
    // KROMKA (torec): "Узидан" bo'lsa panel materiali torecga o'zi tushadi (bitta material).
    // ALOHIDA rangли kromka bo'lsa (p.edge.col) — yon yuzalar (torec) shu rang bilan chiziladi:
    // Extrude geo 2 guruh (0=old/orqa yuza, 1=torec), Box 6 guruh (±Z=yuza, qolgani=torec).
    // KROMKA (torec/yon yuza) rangi: torec juda ingichka (2-18mm) — tekstura ko'rinmaydi,
    // shuning uchun RANG bilan chiziladi. "Узидан" -> panel materiali rangi (matColor),
    // alohida kromka -> o'z rangi. Shunda torec oq emas, kromka aniq ko'rinadi.
    let edgeC=null;
    if(!KROMKA_NEW && REAL_TEX && p.edge && p.poly){       // ESKI ko'rinish (boshqa brendlar) — o'zgarmadi
      if(p.edge.col && !_emptyCol(p.edge.col)) edgeC=parseInt(String(p.edge.col).replace('#',''),16);
      else if(p.edge.same) edgeC=(colHex!=null?colHex:matColor(p.mat||''));
    }
    let finalMat=mat;
    if(edgeC!=null){
      const em=_sRGBFix(new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:edgeC,roughness:.72,metalness:.05,
        transparent:true,opacity:p._op,depthWrite:p._dw}));
      finalMat = isBox ? [em,em,em,em,mat,mat] : [mat,em];   // Box: ±Z=yuza; Extrude: 0=yuza,1=torec
      p._edgeMat=em;
    }
    // YANGI KROMKA (yon yuza bo'yicha): kromkasi bor tomonning O'Z YUZASI kromka
    // rangi/teksturasida, qolgan yuzalar panel materialida (ajralib turmaydi).
    if(KROMKA_NEW && !isBox && geo.groups && geo.groups.length){
      const keys=splitSidesByEdge(geo,p);
      if(keys){
        // har kromka guruhiga O'Z tekstura sozlamasi (Шаг X/Y) bo'yicha UV
        keys.forEach((k,ki)=>{
          if(k.charAt(0)!=='t')return;
          const tk=k.slice(1).split('~')[0], ts=TM[tk]||{};
          let tw=ts.tw, th=ts.th;
          if(!tw||!th){
            // Bazis bu kromka uchun «Шаг» bermagan: qalinlik bo'ylab AYNAN bitta takror
            // qilamiz (kromka lentasi shunday yopishtiriladi), uzunlik bo'ylab qadamni
            // rasm nisbatidan olamiz (bo'lmasa kvadrat deb hisoblanadi).
            th=panelDep(p);
            const _im=getTex(tk,p.mat||''), _i=_im&&_im.image;
            const asp=(_i&&_i.height)?(_i.width/_i.height):1;
            tw=th*(asp>0?asp:1);
          }
          const g=(geo.groups||[]).find(x=>x.materialIndex===2+ki);
          const _c=polyCenter(p);
          // tola kromka UZUNLIGI bo'ylab (report #82) — rasmning o'z tola o'qi bo'yicha
          if(g)bakeSideUVGroup(geo,g,1/tw,1/th,_c&&_c[0],_c&&_c[1],p.quat,ts.g===1);
        });
        const ems=keys.map(k=>{
          const o={roughness:.72,metalness:.05,transparent:true,opacity:p._op,depthWrite:p._dw};
          if(k.charAt(0)==='t'){                       // kromkaning REAL teksturasi
            const kk=k.slice(1).split('~'), tk=kk[0], tnt=kk[1]||null;
            const tmap=getTex(tk,p.mat||'');
            if(tmap){o.color=(tintHex(tnt)||0xffffff);o.map=tmap;}else o.color=matColor(p.mat||'');
          }else{
            o.color=parseInt(String(k.slice(1)).replace('#',''),16);
            if(isNaN(o.color))o.color=matColor(p.mat||'');
          }
          return _sRGBFix(new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(o));
        });
        finalMat=[mat,mat].concat(ems);   // 0=asosiy yuza, 1=kromkasiz torec (panel), 2..=kromkalar
        p._edgeMats=ems;
      }
    }
    const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geo,finalMat);
    // KONTUR BARQARORLIGI ("vijir-vijir"ni yo'qotish): panel YUZA materiallariga kichik
    // UNIFORM polygonOffset — yuzani depth-buferда bir oz ORTGA suradi, shunda kontur chiziqlari
    // (LineSegments) o'z yuzasi bilan z-fight qilmay DOIM ustun chizadi. Offset QALINLIKKA
    // bog'liq EMAS (hammaga bir xil 1/1) -> ustma-ust turli qalinlikdagi panellar tartibini
    // buzmaydi (eski qalinlik-bog'liq offset "yog'och tokcha oqarib qolardi"; uniform undoq emas).
    // 1 unit ~ sub-mm depth: faqat COINCIDENT (0 oraliq) qirralarni ustun qiladi, 5mm+ orqadagi
    // yuzalar baribir oldingi yuza tomonidan to'sib qolinadi (orqa qirralar sizib chiqmaydi).
    for(const _m of (Array.isArray(mesh.material)?mesh.material:[mesh.material])){
      if(_m){ _m.polygonOffset=true; _m.polygonOffsetFactor=1; _m.polygonOffsetUnits=1; }
    }
    mesh.position.set(p.pos[0],p.pos[1],p.pos[2]);
    mesh.quaternion.set(p.quat[0],p.quat[1],p.quat[2],p.quat[3]);
    mesh.userData=p;
    if(showEdges){
      // CSG (o'yilgan) panelда kontur qirralari BSP polygonlaridan CREASE (tekislik-farqi)
      // usulida quriladi (geo._crease): faqat ikki XIL tekislik uchrashgan qirra chiziladi —
      // shu tufayli haqiqiy burchak/o'yiq chegarasi qoladi, coplanar T-junction begona chiziqlari
      // chizilmaydi. Oddiy panelда — standart EdgesGeometry.
      // oddiy poly panel (box/bevel/subtract EMAS) -> konturdan qurish (seam artefaktisiz).
      // MUHIM: sweep bilan qurilgan panelда (bukilgan detal) p.poly = YOYIB chizilgan
      // ZAGOTOVKA (2D chizma uchun), 3D shakl emas. Uni kontur manbai qilib olsak,
      // havoda zagotovkaning "arvoh" to'rtburchagi chizilardi, bukilgan yuzada esa
      // qirra qolmasdi — shuning uchun sweep'da HAR DOIM geometriyadan olamiz.
      const _useContour = p.poly && p.poly.length>=3 && !p.sweep
                          && !(p.bevels&&p.bevels.length) && !(p.subtract&&p.subtract.length);
      // geo._crease — CSG (subtract yoki chegaradagi o'yiq) natijasidan olingan qirralar.
      // Bunday panelда konturdan qurish YARAMAYDI: kontur va haqiqiy shakl endi boshqa.
      const egeo=geo._crease ? geo._crease
                 : (p.subtract && p.subtract.length) ? null
                 : _useContour ? contourEdges(p)
                 : new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geo, p.sweep?45:20);   // sweep: faqat keskin qirralar (chiziq bosimi vijirlatmasin)
      if(egeo){
        // obklad/karona (ingichka molding) qirralari xira — zich chiziq vijirlatmasin;
        // bukilgan PANEL esa oddiy detal, konturi to'liq quyuq bo'lishi kerak.
        const _eop=(p.sweep && !p.bentpanel)?0.4:0.65;
        const eg=new THREE.LineSegments(egeo,new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x000000,transparent:true,opacity:_eop}));
        mesh.add(eg);mesh.userData._eg=eg;
      }
    }
    // teshiklar faqat kerak bo'lganda quriladi (lazy) — katta modelда tezlik uchun
    const hg=new THREE.Group();hg.visible=false;mesh.add(hg);
    mesh.userData._hg=hg;mesh.userData._holesBuilt=false;
    if(!SHOT)buildPockets(mesh,p);   // paz/cho'ntak (hammaga). Kromka endi POLOSA emas — panelning yon yuzasi (splitSidesByEdge)
    // SWEEP (obklad/karona) tagi panel yuzasiga aynan tekis yotadi — polygonOffset molding'ни
    // depth'da ozgina oldinga tortadi, koplanar z-fighting (aylantirishда vijirlash) yo'qoladi.
    if(p.sweep){ for(const mt of _matsOf(mesh)){ mt.polygonOffset=true; mt.polygonOffsetFactor=-1; mt.polygonOffsetUnits=-2; } }
    model.add(mesh);meshes.push(mesh);box.expandByObject(mesh);
  }
  const ctr=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length();
  modelR=size;   // model "radiusi" (to'liq diagonal) — dinamik near/far uchun, dims+explode qamrab oladi
  // 3D OLAM foni: model ostiga yer + kontakt soya (bbox'дан KEYIN — kamera kadriga ta'sir qilmaydi)
  addGround(box);
  cx=ctr.x;cy=ctr.y;cz=ctr.z;dist=size*1.25;
  homeView={cx,cy,cz,dist};
  setupDims(size);
  // yoyib ko'rsatish (exploded view) — yangi modelda o'chiq holatga qaytariladi
  explodeOn=false; explodeMax=size*0.5;
  try{_explClearTags();}catch(e){}          // yangi model — eski yorliqlar/legenda qolmasin
  const eb=document.getElementById('explodeBar'); if(eb)eb.style.display='none';
  const es=document.getElementById('explodeSlider'); if(es)es.value=0;
  const me=document.getElementById('mExplode'); if(me)me.classList.remove('on');
  furn3D=false; furnMarkerGroup=null;   // yangi modelda furnitura 3D belgilari o'chiq
  // fasad-yashirish holati yangi modelда tiklanadi
  if(typeof _facSet!=='undefined'){ _facSet.clear(); facadesHidden=false;
    const _fl=document.getElementById('mFacadesLbl'); if(_fl)_fl.textContent=t('facHide');
    const _fb=document.getElementById('mFacades'); if(_fb)_fb.classList.remove('on'); }
  buildFurn();
  const lsq=document.getElementById('listSearch'); if(lsq)lsq.value='';
  buildList();
  select(null);
  setupAnims();   // animatsiya nishonlari (panellar) — furnitura instancelari toggleFurn3D'da qo'shiladi
  // STANDART YOQIQ: furnitura + razmerlar (toggle false->true: quradi va ko'rsatadi)
  // SKRINSHOT rejimida bularni chizmaymiz — toza model rasmi kerak
  if(!SHOT&&DATA.fittings&&DATA.fittings.length)toggleFurn3D();
  if(!SHOT&&((DATA.dims&&DATA.dims.length)||(DATA.texts&&DATA.texts.length)))toggleDims();
}

// 'Размер' o'lchovlari — faqat HOLATni tayyorlaydi (yuklashda OG'IRLIK qo'shmaydi).
// Haqiqiy chiziq/yorliqlar foydalanuvchi "ko'rsatish"ni bosganda quriladi (lazy).
function setupDims(modelSpan){
  dimGroup=null; dimsVisible=false; dimSpan=modelSpan;
  const mDims=document.getElementById('mDims');
  const has=DATA&&((DATA.dims&&DATA.dims.length)||(DATA.texts&&DATA.texts.length));
  if(mDims)mDims.style.display=has?'':'none';
  const l=document.getElementById('mDimsLbl');
  if(l)l.textContent=t('dimShow');
}

// Chiziq/yorliqlarni faqat bir marta (birinchi yoqilganda) quradi.
// Har razmer/2D-matn markazi bilan _dimItems'ga yoziladi — izolyatsiya qilinganda
// blokka (ko'rinadigan detallar bbox'iga) tegishli bo'lmaganlari yashiriladi.
let _dimItems=[];
function buildDimGeometry(){
  const modelSpan=dimSpan;
  dimGroup=new THREE.Group();model.add(dimGroup);
  _dimItems=[];
  const dims=(DATA&&DATA.dims)||[];
  const acc=0x111111;   // razmer chiziqlari + 2D elementlar — standart QORA (rang bo'lmasa)
  const lineMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:acc});
  // Har razmer O'Z rangida (Bazisdan kelgan d.col) chiziladi. Juda och rang oq fonda
  // ko'rinmay qolmasin: yorqinlik >0.86 bo'lsa standart qoraga qaytamiz (2026-08-11).
  const _dimMatCache={};
  function dimMat(col){
    if(!col) return lineMat;
    if(!_dimMatCache[col]){
      const c=new THREE.Color(col), lum=0.2126*c.r+0.7152*c.g+0.0722*c.b;
      _dimMatCache[col]=(lum>0.86)?lineMat:new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:c});
    }
    return _dimMatCache[col];
  }
  const HL=30, HW=8;   // strelka (o'q boshi) uzunligi va yarim eni — SOBIT (mm)
  const V=(p)=>new THREE.Vector3(p[0],p[1],p[2]);
  // MINIMAL SILJISH. Bazisda razmerning "Смещение" qiymati kichik bo'lsa (masalan 30 mm),
  // o'lchov chizig'i detalning USTIGA tushib qoladi va son o'qilmaydi. Yaxshi chizilgan
  // modellarda siljish ~150 mm (chiziq modeldan tashqarida). Shuning uchun siljish
  // DIM_MIN_OFF dan kichik bo'lsa, chiziqni SHU yo'nalish bo'yicha tashqariga suramiz —
  // tayanch chiziqlari (ea→a, eb→b) o'zi uzayadi, ya'ni chizma qoidasi buzilmaydi.
  // Siljishi allaqachon yetarli razmerlar (deluxe/634-01 kabi) UMUMAN o'zgarmaydi.
  const DIM_MIN_OFF=120;
  const _ctr=new THREE.Vector3(cx,cy,cz);   // model markazi — yo'nalish tanlash uchun
  for(const d of dims){
    const dg=new THREE.Group();               // bitta razmer = chiziq + strelka + raqam
    let a=V(d.a),b=V(d.b); const ea=V(d.ea),eb=V(d.eb);
    { const _off=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(a,ea), _ol=_off.length();
      if(_ol<DIM_MIN_OFF){
        let _od;
        if(_ol>0.5) _od=_off.clone().normalize();
        else {                                  // siljish umuman yo'q — chiziqqa perpendikulyar
          const _dd=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(eb,ea);
          if(_dd.lengthSq()<1e-6) _od=new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0);
          else{
            const _u=Math.abs(_dd.clone().normalize().y)<0.9?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);
            _od=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(_dd,_u).normalize();
            // modeldan TASHQARIGA qaragan tomonni tanlaymiz
            const _m=ea.clone().add(eb).multiplyScalar(0.5);
            if(_m.clone().add(_od).distanceTo(_ctr) < _m.clone().sub(_od).distanceTo(_ctr)) _od.negate();
          }
        }
        const _add=_od.multiplyScalar(DIM_MIN_OFF-_ol);
        a=a.clone().add(_add); b=b.clone().add(_add);
      } }
    const dm=dimMat(d.col);   // razmerning Bazisdagi rangi (bo'lmasa qora)
    const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([a,b, ea,a, eb,b]);
    dg.add(new THREE.LineSegments(g,dm));
    const dir=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(b,a).normalize();
    const up=Math.abs(dir.y)<0.9?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);
    const perp=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(dir,up).normalize();
    const di=dir.clone().multiplyScalar(HL), pe=perp.clone().multiplyScalar(HW);
    const ar=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([
      a,a.clone().add(di).add(pe),  a,a.clone().add(di).sub(pe),
      b,b.clone().sub(di).add(pe),  b,b.clone().sub(di).sub(pe)]);
    dg.add(new THREE.LineSegments(ar,dm));
    const mid=a.clone().add(b).multiplyScalar(0.5);
    // Raqamni razmer chizig'idan TASHQARIGA surib qo'yamiz: aks holda qora chiziq qora
    // raqam markazidan o'tib uni kesardi va son o'qilmasdi (foydalanuvchi so'rovi 2026-08-11:
    // "son razmer liniyasiga tushib qolyapti"). Surish yo'nalishi = chiziqning obyektdan
    // siljigan tomoni (a−ea, ya'ni "Смещение" yo'nalishi); siljish bo'lmasa chiziqqa
    // perpendikulyar. Masofa ~raqam balandligicha — chiziq raqam ostida qoladi.
    let nud=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(a,ea);
    if(nud.lengthSq()<1) nud.copy(perp);
    nud.normalize().multiplyScalar(26);
    // 2026-08-26 (foydalanuvchi): raqam endi O'LCHOV CHIZIG'I YO'NALISHIDA yotadi
    // (vertikal razmer — vertikal raqam), ekran-billboard emas.
    const spr=makeDimLabelOriented(Math.round(d.value)+'',32,dir,nud.clone().normalize());
    spr.position.copy(mid).add(nud); dg.add(spr);
    dimGroup.add(dg);
    // MARKAZ = razmer O'LCHAYOTGAN nuqtalar o'rtasi (tayanch ea/eb — detalga tegib turadi)
    _dimItems.push({obj:dg, c:V(d.ea).add(V(d.eb)).multiplyScalar(0.5)});
  }
  // 2D matn (Текст) — kontur chiziqlari (harflar)
  const txtMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:acc});
  for(const t of ((DATA&&DATA.texts)||[])){
    const tg=new THREE.Group(); const c=new THREE.Vector3(); let np=0;
    for(const poly of (t.polys||[])){
      if(poly.length<2)continue;
      const pts=poly.map(p=>new THREE.Vector3(p[0],p[1],p[2]));
      for(const q of pts){ c.add(q); np++; }
      tg.add(new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts),txtMat));
    }
    if(np){ dimGroup.add(tg); _dimItems.push({obj:tg, c:c.multiplyScalar(1/np)}); }
  }
  applyDimVis();
}
// Izolyatsiya/skrit faol bo'lsa — faqat KO'RINADIGAN detallar bbox'i ichidagi razmer/matn
// ko'rinadi (blokka tegishli bo'lmaganlari yashiriladi). Aks holda — hammasi.
function applyDimVis(){
  if(!dimGroup || !_dimItems.length)return;
  const isolated = meshes.some(m=>!m.visible);
  let box=null;
  if(isolated){
    box=new THREE.Box3();
    for(const m of meshes){ if(m.visible)box.expandByObject(m); }
    if(box.isEmpty())box=null; else box.expandByScalar(80);
  }
  for(const it of _dimItems){ it.obj.visible = !box || box.containsPoint(it.c); }
}

function makeDimLabel(text,worldSize,depthTest){
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=256;cv.height=128;
  const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  // ODDIY QORA 2D matn — oq soya/halo YO'Q (foydalanuvchi so'rovi: tekis qora tekst).
  x.font='500 60px system-ui,-apple-system,Arial';
  x.textAlign='center';x.textBaseline='middle';
  x.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';
  x.fillText(text,128,64);
  const tex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);tex.minFilter=THREE.LinearFilter;tex.anisotropy=4;
  // depthTest:true -> raqam model ichida (panel ortida) bo'lsa YASHIRINADI (eshik ochilsa ko'rinadi)
  const spr=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex,depthTest:true,depthWrite:false,transparent:true}));
  // worldSize = raqamning taxminiy balandligi (mm). Kanvas raqami ~46px/128px.
  const sh=worldSize*128/46;
  spr.scale.set(sh*2,sh,1);
  return spr;
}

function makeDimLabelOriented(text,worldSize,dirV,upV){
  // Chizma qoidasidagi kabi: raqam o'lchov chizig'i BO'YLAB yotadi (3D tekislikda).
  // O'qish yo'nalishi normallashtiriladi: gorizontalga yaqin chiziqda chapdan-o'ngga,
  // vertikalda pastdan-tepaga (aks holda raqam teskari chiqadi).
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=256;cv.height=128;
  const x2=cv.getContext('2d');
  x2.font='500 60px system-ui,-apple-system,Arial';
  x2.textAlign='center';x2.textBaseline='middle';
  x2.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';
  x2.fillText(text,128,64);
  const tex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);tex.minFilter=THREE.LinearFilter;tex.anisotropy=4;
  const sh=worldSize*128/46;
  // ORQA tomondan ko'zgu bo'lib o'qilmasin («2202» -> «5505»): DoubleSide o'rniga IKKI
  // bir tomonlama nusxa — old va orqa; orqa nusxa matn TAYANCH O'QI (ax) atrofida 180°
  // burilgan, shunda ikkala tomondan ham to'g'ri o'qiladi.
  const grp=new THREE.Group();
  try{
    let ax=dirV.clone().normalize();
    if(Math.abs(ax.y)>=0.7){ if(ax.y<0)ax.negate(); }
    else if(ax.x<0 || (Math.abs(ax.x)<1e-3 && ax.z>0)) ax.negate();
    let ay=(upV&&upV.lengthSq()>1e-6)?upV.clone():new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0);
    ay.sub(ax.clone().multiplyScalar(ay.dot(ax)));
    if(ay.lengthSq()<1e-6)ay=Math.abs(ax.y)<0.9?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);
    ay.normalize();
    const az=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(ax,ay);
    for(const bk of [false,true]){
      const geo=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(sh*2,sh);
      const mat=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex,transparent:true,side:THREE.FrontSide,
        depthTest:true,depthWrite:false});
      const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);
      const y2=bk?ay.clone().negate():ay, z2=bk?az.clone().negate():az;
      mesh.setRotationFromMatrix(new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(ax,y2,z2));
      grp.add(mesh);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return grp;
}

function toggleDims(){
  dimsVisible=!dimsVisible;
  if(dimsVisible&&!dimGroup)buildDimGeometry();   // lazy: faqat birinchi yoqilganda quriladi
  if(dimGroup)dimGroup.visible=dimsVisible;
  if(dimsVisible)applyDimVis();                   // izolyatsiya faol bo'lsa filtrlab ko'rsatadi
  const l=document.getElementById('mDimsLbl');
  if(l)l.textContent=dimsVisible?t('dimHide'):t('dimShow');
}

// Furnitura/krepyojni 3D modelda ko'rsatish — har FURNITURA bo'lagi uchun BITTA sharcha
// (teshik nuqtalari emas: 1 evrovint = 1 belgi). Rang turi bo'yicha (ro'yxatdagidek).
// Furnitura QORA KONTURI: panellardagi kabi EdgesGeometry qirra chiziqlari.
// Har mesh-instansiyaga bitta LineSegments (umumiy edges geometriya) — matritsasi
// instansiyanikidan ko'chiriladi; ko'rinish (makeFurnInstance) va animatsiya (animMat)
// bilan sinxron yuritiladi. Shu tufayli metall furnitura panel rangiga singib ketmaydi.
let _furnEdgeMat=null;   // lazy — THREE bu nuqtada hali yuklanmagan bo'lishi mumkin
function furnEdgeMat(){
  return _furnEdgeMat||(_furnEdgeMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x111111,transparent:true,opacity:.8}));
}
const FURN_EDGE_MAX=600;   // juda katta modellarda chiziqlar soni chegarasi (draw call)
function buildFurnMarkers(){
  furnMarkerGroup=new THREE.Group();model.add(furnMarkerGroup);
  furnInstances=[];furnMeshList=[];_furnEdgeCount=0;
  const list=(DATA&&DATA.fittings)||[];
  const fmeshes=(DATA&&DATA.furn_meshes)||[];

  // 1) HAQIQIY MESH (ruchka/nojka/petlya — Bazis TriData). Bir xil shablon -> InstancedMesh.
  const byMesh={};
  for(const f of list){if(f.mesh!=null)(byMesh[f.mesh]=byMesh[f.mesh]||[]).push(f);}
  {
    const mq=new THREE.Quaternion(),mm=new THREE.Matrix4(),mp=new THREE.Vector3(),ONE=new THREE.Vector3(1,1,1);
    for(const mi in byMesh){
      const md=fmeshes[mi];if(!md||!md.verts||!md.tris)continue;
      const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      const arr=new Float32Array(md.verts.length*3);
      for(let i=0;i<md.verts.length;i++){arr[i*3]=md.verts[i][0];arr[i*3+1]=md.verts[i][1];arr[i*3+2]=md.verts[i][2];}
      g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(arr,3));
      const idx=[];for(const t of md.tris)idx.push(t[0],t[1],t[2]);
      g.setIndex(idx);g.computeVertexNormals();
      const fs=byMesh[mi];
      const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:new THREE.Color(fs[0].color||'#b8bdc4'),
        roughness:.5,metalness:.55,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
      const im=new THREE.InstancedMesh(g,mat,fs.length);
      // umumiy qirra geometriyasi (shablon uchun bir marta) — har instansiyaga LineSegments
      const eg=_furnEdgeCount<FURN_EDGE_MAX ? new THREE.EdgesGeometry(g,25) : null;
      for(let i=0;i<fs.length;i++){
        const f=fs[i];
        mq.set(f.quat[0],f.quat[1],f.quat[2],f.quat[3]);
        mp.set(f.pos[0],f.pos[1],f.pos[2]);
        mm.compose(mp,mq,ONE);im.setMatrixAt(i,mm);
        let ls=null;
        if(eg&&_furnEdgeCount<FURN_EDGE_MAX){
          _furnEdgeCount++;
          ls=new THREE.LineSegments(eg,furnEdgeMat());
          ls.matrixAutoUpdate=false;ls.matrix.copy(mm);ls.renderOrder=1;
          furnMarkerGroup.add(ls);
        }
        furnInstances.push(makeFurnInstance(im,i,mm.clone(),f.gid,ls,f));
        if(f.anim!=null&&f.anim>=0&&ANIMS[f.anim])animInstances.push({im,idx:i,base:mm.clone(),chain:animChainOf(f.anim),root:ANIMS[f.anim].root,line:ls});
      }
      im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
      furnMarkerGroup.add(im);furnMeshList.push(im);
    }
  }

  // 2) SECTION (silindr) — teshikli krepyoj (evrovint pog'onali vint, shkant, minifix...)
  const geo=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1,1,1,14);
  const cylEdges=new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geo,25);   // qora kontur (cap doiralar + bo'ylama chiziqlar)
  const byColor={};
  for(const f of list){if(f.sections)for(const s of f.sections)(byColor[f.color]=byColor[f.color]||[]).push({s,anim:f.anim,gid:f.gid,f});}
  const UP=new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0),q=new THREE.Quaternion(),dir=new THREE.Vector3();
  const m=new THREE.Matrix4(),sc=new THREE.Vector3(),pos=new THREE.Vector3();
  for(const col in byColor){
    const ss=byColor[col];
    const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:new THREE.Color(col),
      emissive:new THREE.Color(col),emissiveIntensity:.35,roughness:.4,metalness:.45});
    const im=new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo,mat,ss.length);
    for(let i=0;i<ss.length;i++){
      const s=ss[i].s;
      const r=Math.max(1.6,s.r||3), len=Math.max(1,Math.min(80,s.len||14));
      dir.set(s.d?s.d[0]:0,s.d?s.d[1]:0,s.d?s.d[2]:1);
      if(dir.lengthSq()<1e-6)dir.set(0,0,1);
      dir.normalize();
      q.setFromUnitVectors(UP,dir);
      sc.set(r,len,r);
      // silindr p dan dir bo'yicha 'len' uzunlikda: markazi = p + dir*len/2
      pos.set(s.p[0]+dir.x*len/2, s.p[1]+dir.y*len/2, s.p[2]+dir.z*len/2);
      m.compose(pos,q,sc);
      im.setMatrixAt(i,m);
      let ls=null;
      if(_furnEdgeCount<FURN_EDGE_MAX){
        _furnEdgeCount++;
        ls=new THREE.LineSegments(cylEdges,furnEdgeMat());
        ls.matrixAutoUpdate=false;ls.matrix.copy(m);ls.renderOrder=1;
        furnMarkerGroup.add(ls);
      }
      furnInstances.push(makeFurnInstance(im,i,m.clone(),ss[i].gid,ls,ss[i].f));
      const an=ss[i].anim;
      if(an!=null&&an>=0&&ANIMS[an])animInstances.push({im,idx:i,base:m.clone(),chain:animChainOf(an),root:ANIMS[an].root,line:ls});
    }
    im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    furnMarkerGroup.add(im);furnMeshList.push(im);
  }
  console.log('[furn] instansiya=%d, kontur-chiziq=%d', furnInstances.length, _furnEdgeCount);
}
function toggleFurn3D(){
  furn3D=!furn3D;
  if(furn3D&&!furnMarkerGroup)buildFurnMarkers();   // lazy: faqat birinchi yoqilganda
  if(furnMarkerGroup)furnMarkerGroup.visible=furn3D;
  const l=document.getElementById('mFurn3DLbl');
  if(l)l.textContent=furn3D?t('furnHide'):t('furnShow');
  applyFurnVis();   // qatlam holatiga qarab nusxalar
  applyHoleVis();   // teshiklar ham shu (prisadka) qatlamiga bog'liq
}

// "Ochiq" teshik (polka derjatel / prisadka teshigi) — mahkamlagichi (evrovint/shkant/petlya)
// ALOHIDA furnitura sifatida chiziladigan teshiklarni bu yerda TAKRORLAMAYMIZ (aks holda ustma-ust).
function isOpenHole(h){
  return !/shkant|petl|конфирм|minifix|мини.?фикс|эксцентр|стяж|vint|винт|шкант|петл/i.test(h.type||'');
}
function buildHolePoints(p,hg){
  const th=panelDep(p);
  for(const h of (p.holes||[])){
    const torec=(h.face==='T');
    // torec (qirra) teshigi DOIM ko'rsatiladi — shtok/shkant qismi furnitura mesh'ida bo'lmaydi;
    // yuza teshiklaridan esa faqat "ochiq"lari (furnitura bilan takror bo'lmasin)
    if(!torec&&!isOpenHole(h))continue;
    const r=Math.max(2.8,(h.d||5)/2);
    const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:new THREE.Color(h.color),emissive:new THREE.Color(h.color),emissiveIntensity:.65,roughness:.5,metalness:.2});
    let cyl;
    if(torec){
      // TOREC teshigi: qirradagi (x,y) nuqtadan 'ang' (gradus) yo'nalishda panel TEKISLIGI
      // bo'ylab 'depth' mm ichkariga; balandligi z (qalinlik ichida). Qirradan 3mm chiqib turadi.
      const dep=Math.max(4,h.depth||20);
      const a=(h.ang||0)*Math.PI/180;
      const geo=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r,r,dep+3,14);
      geo.rotateZ(a-Math.PI/2);          // silindr o'qi (+Y) -> (cosA,sinA) yo'nalishga
      cyl=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);
      cyl.position.set(h.x+Math.cos(a)*(dep-3)/2, h.y+Math.sin(a)*(dep-3)/2,
                       (h.z!=null?h.z:th/2));
    }else{
      // YUZA teshigi: teshib o'tuvchi -> ikki yuzadan chiqadi; SAYOZ -> faqat o'z yuzasidan
      // ('A'=z0 yuza, 'B'=z=th yuza) depth ichkariga, 2mm chiqib ko'rinadi.
      const thru=h.thru!==false&&!(h.depth&&h.depth<th-0.6);
      const dep=thru?th+7:Math.max(3,h.depth);
      const geo=new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r,r,thru?dep:dep+2,16);geo.rotateX(Math.PI/2);
      cyl=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);
      let zc=th/2;
      if(!thru)zc=(h.face==='A')?(dep-2)/2:th-(dep-2)/2;
      cyl.position.set(h.x,h.y,zc);
    }
    cyl.userData._ht=h.type;
    hg.add(cyl);
  }
}
function ensureHoles(mesh){
  const u=mesh.userData;
  if(u._holesBuilt)return;
  buildHolePoints(u,u._hg);
  u._holesBuilt=true;
}

// ===== FAZOVIY bog'lanish: detal(lar)ga tegishli furnitura + teshik ko'rinishi =====
function meshWorldBox(m){
  if(!m.geometry.boundingBox)m.geometry.computeBoundingBox();
  return m.geometry.boundingBox.clone().applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld).expandByScalar(22);
}
let selRelFurn=null;              // tanlangan detal/blokka taluqli furnitura (null = tanlov yo'q)
let _fp=null;                     // THREE CDN'dan ASINXRON yuklanadi — top-level'da new THREE.* qilib bo'lmaydi (lazy)
// ===== DETAL/BLOK ↔ FURNITURA BOG'LANISHI (2026-08-29 qayta yozildi) =====
// ESKI usul: furnitura NUQTASI panel bbox'i + 22mm ichida bo'lsa "tegishli" — bu QO'SHNI
// panelning furniturasini ham tortib olardi (burchakdagi minifix, yon devordagi petlya
// eshik bilan birga "tanlanib" qolardi — foydalanuvchi, zias-case/AKMAL-AKA).
// YANGI:
//  • DETAL — furnitura instansiyasining namuna nuqtalari (bbox markazi + 8 burchak) panel
//    LOKAL koordinatasiga o'tkazilib, panel PLITASI ichidami tekshiriladi: kontur (poly)
//    ichida VA qalinlik oralig'ida (±3mm). Ya'ni faqat SHU panelga kirib turgan/tegib
//    turgan furnitura: eshikdagi petlya chashkasi, ostidagi oyoqcha, ichiga kirgan vint.
//  • BLOK — STRUKTURA bo'yicha: furnitura `gid`ining birinchi bo'g'ini blok kaliti bilan
//    bir xil (blockKeyOf). gid'siz (eski JSON) furnitura uchun plita usuli zaxira.
const _FURN_TOL=3;
function _ptInPolyTol(x,y,poly,tol){
  let inside=false; const n=poly.length, t2=tol*tol;
  for(let i=0,j=n-1;i<n;j=i++){
    const xi=poly[i][0],yi=poly[i][1],xj=poly[j][0],yj=poly[j][1];
    if(((yi>y)!==(yj>y)) && (x < (xj-xi)*(y-yi)/(yj-yi)+xi)) inside=!inside;
    const dx=xj-xi,dy=yj-yi,L2=dx*dx+dy*dy; let t=L2?((x-xi)*dx+(y-yi)*dy)/L2:0; t=t<0?0:(t>1?1:t);
    const px=xi+t*dx-x,py=yi+t*dy-y; if(px*px+py*py<=t2)return true;
  }
  return inside;
}
function _instWorldPts(fi){          // instansiya bbox markazi + 8 burchak — WORLD'da
  const g=fi.im.geometry; if(!g.boundingBox)g.computeBoundingBox();
  const bb=g.boundingBox, mw=new THREE.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(fi.im.matrixWorld, fi.base);
  const pts=[bb.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3())];
  for(const x of [bb.min.x,bb.max.x])for(const y of [bb.min.y,bb.max.y])for(const z of [bb.min.z,bb.max.z])pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x,y,z));
  return pts.map(q=>q.applyMatrix4(mw));
}
function _panelSlabHas(m, wpts, inv){   // world nuqtalardan birortasi panel plitasi ichidami
  const g=m.geometry; if(!g.boundingBox)g.computeBoundingBox();
  const bb=g.boundingBox, T=_FURN_TOL, poly=m.userData.poly, usePoly=!!(poly&&poly.length>=3&&!m.userData.sweep&&!m.userData.bent);
  for(const w of wpts){
    const l=w.clone().applyMatrix4(inv);
    if(l.z<bb.min.z-T||l.z>bb.max.z+T||l.x<bb.min.x-T||l.x>bb.max.x+T||l.y<bb.min.y-T||l.y>bb.max.y+T)continue;
    if(usePoly&&!_ptInPolyTol(l.x,l.y,poly,T))continue;
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function furnForMeshes(arr, isBlock){
  const set=new Set();
  if(!arr||!arr.length||!furnInstances.length)return set;
  model.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  let rest=furnInstances;
  if(isBlock){                          // STRUKTURA: blok kaliti bo'yicha
    const keys=new Set(arr.map(m=>blockKeyOf(m.userData)));
    rest=[];
    for(const fi of furnInstances){
      const g=fi.gid?String(fi.gid).split('/')[0]:'';
      if(g&&keys.has(g))set.add(fi); else if(!g)rest.push(fi);   // gid'siz -> plita usuli
    }
    if(!rest.length)return set;
  }
  const invs=arr.map(m=>new THREE.Matrix4().copy(m.matrixWorld).invert());
  for(const fi of rest){
    const pts=_instWorldPts(fi);
    for(let i=0;i<arr.length;i++){ if(_panelSlabHas(arr[i],pts,invs[i])){ set.add(fi); break; } }
  }
  return set;
}
// Furnitura ko'rinishi: skrit(hiddenSet) qilinmaganlarning HAMMASI ko'rinadi — detal
// tanlanganda ham butun modeldagi furnitura ko'rinib turadi (foydalanuvchi so'rovi).
// (teshiklar esa hamon faqat tanlangan panelda — 100+ teshik chalkashmasin.)
function applyFurnVis(){
  for(const fi of furnInstances){
    fi.visible = !hiddenSet.has(fi);
  }
}
// Teshik ko'rinishi: prisadka(furn3D) qatlami yoqiq VA panel TANLANGAN bo'lsa — o'sha panelning
// teshiklari ko'rsatiladi (overviewda 100+ teshik chalkashmasin; panelni bosib ko'riladi).
function applyHoleVis(){
  const selSet = blockSel ? new Set(blockSel) : (selected ? new Set([selected]) : null);
  for(const m of meshes){
    const show = furn3D && m.visible && selSet && selSet.has(m);
    if(show)ensureHoles(m);
    if(m.userData._hg)m.userData._hg.visible=show;
  }
}
// tanlov o'zgarganda furnitura+teshikni yangilash (selRelFurn'ni ham hisoblaydi)
function refreshSelVis(){
  const arr = blockSel ? blockSel : (selected ? [selected] : null);
  selRelFurn = arr ? furnForMeshes(arr, !!blockSel) : null;
  applyFurnVis();
  applyHoleVis();
}
// o'yiq/cho'ntak konturidan THREE.Path (teshik uchun) yoki THREE.Shape (to'ldirish uchun)
function cutPath(c){
  const pth=new THREE.Path();
  if(c.t==='circle'){pth.absarc(c.x,c.y,c.r,0,Math.PI*2,true);}
  else if(c.pts&&c.pts.length>=3){pth.moveTo(c.pts[0][0],c.pts[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<c.pts.length;i++)pth.lineTo(c.pts[i][0],c.pts[i][1]);pth.closePath();}
  return pth;
}
function cutShape(c){
  const s=new THREE.Shape();
  if(c.t==='circle'){s.absarc(c.x,c.y,c.r,0,Math.PI*2,false);}
  else if(c.pts&&c.pts.length>=3){s.moveTo(c.pts[0][0],c.pts[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<c.pts.length;i++)s.lineTo(c.pts[i][0],c.pts[i][1]);s.closePath();}
  return s;
}

// ===== CHEGARANI KESIB O'TUVCHI O'YIQ (2026-09-02) =====
// Bazis'da detal konturidan CHIQIB ketadigan yoki unga TEGIB turadigan kesim bo'ladi
// (masalan stol burchagidagi katta o'yiq). Bunday kesimni `Shape.holes` ga qo'shib
// BO'LMAYDI: earcut triangulyatsiyasi buzilib, detal "uchburchak yelpig'ich" bo'lib
// noto'g'ri chiziladi va o'yiq umuman kesilmaydi (foydalanuvchi shikoyati:
// «Стол для переговоров» 03.04). Bunday kesimlar tanadan CSG bilan AYIRILADI.
function _polyHasPt(x,y,poly,tol){
  let ins=false; const n=poly.length;
  for(let i=0,j=n-1;i<n;j=i++){
    const xi=poly[i][0],yi=poly[i][1],xj=poly[j][0],yj=poly[j][1];
    if(((yi>y)!==(yj>y)) && x < (xj-xi)*(y-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-12)+xi) ins=!ins;
    const dx=xj-xi, dy=yj-yi, L2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    let t=L2?((x-xi)*dx+(y-yi)*dy)/L2:0; t=t<0?0:(t>1?1:t);
    const ex=xi+t*dx-x, ey=yi+t*dy-y;
    if(ex*ex+ey*ey < tol*tol) return false;      // chegarada — "ichida" emas
  }
  return ins;
}
function _cutOnEdge(c,poly){
  if(!poly||poly.length<3)return false;
  const pts=[];
  if(c.t==='circle'){ if(!(c.r>0))return false;
    for(let i=0;i<24;i++){const a=i/24*Math.PI*2;pts.push([c.x+c.r*Math.cos(a),c.y+c.r*Math.sin(a)]);} }
  else if(c.pts&&c.pts.length>=3){ for(const q of c.pts)pts.push(q); }
  else return false;
  for(const q of pts) if(!_polyHasPt(q[0],q[1],poly,0.05)) return true;
  return false;
}
const _ecCache=new Map();
function applyEdgeCuts(geo,p,cuts,dep){
  const key=JSON.stringify([p.poly,dep,cuts]);
  const hit=_ecCache.get(key);
  if(hit){ const g=hit.g.clone(); if(hit.cr)g._crease=hit.cr; return g; }
  let out=geo;
  try{
    let acc=CSGop.toPolys(geo);
    const d=Math.abs(dep)+6;
    for(const c of cuts){
      const tg=new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(cutShape(c),{depth:d,bevelEnabled:false});
      tg.translate(0,0,-3);                       // butun qalinlikni kesib o'tsin
      const bp=CSGop.toPolys(tg);
      if(bp.length) acc=CSGop.subtract(acc,bp);
      tg.dispose&&tg.dispose();
    }
    if(acc.length){
      acc=CSGop.repairT(acc);
      out=csgUV(CSGop.toGeo(acc));
      out._crease=csgCreaseEdges(acc);
    }
  }catch(e){ console.warn('kesim-CSG xato ['+(p&&p.name)+']: '+((e&&e.message)||e)); out=geo; }
  _ecCache.set(key,{g:out.clone(),cr:out._crease||null});
  return out;
}

// PAZ / O'YIQ (qisman chuqurlikdagi cho'ntak — frezerovka/выемка, masalan LED yoki orqa devor pazi).
// 3D modelда panelning to'g'ri YUZASIDA (A=old, B=orqa) ko'rinadigan quyuq belgi bilan chiziladi:
// to'liq CSG kesim emas, lekin joyi va shakli 2D chizmadagidek aniq -> "paz ko'rinmayapti" hal bo'ladi.
// Panel MESH'ning BOLASI qilib qo'shiladi -> panel transformi, yashirish, izolyatsiya bilan birga.
function buildPockets(mesh,p){
  if(!p.pockets||!p.pockets.length)return;
  const th=panelDep(p);
  const decals=[];
  for(const k of p.pockets){
    let sh;
    if(k.t==='circle'){ if(!(k.r>0))continue; sh=new THREE.Shape(); sh.absarc(k.x,k.y,k.r,0,Math.PI*2,false); }
    else if(k.t==='poly'&&k.pts&&k.pts.length>=3){
      sh=new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(k.pts[0][0],k.pts[0][1]);
      for(let i=1;i<k.pts.length;i++)sh.lineTo(k.pts[i][0],k.pts[i][1]); sh.closePath();
    } else continue;
    const g=new THREE.ShapeGeometry(sh);
    const back=(k.face==='B');
    g.translate(0,0, back ? th+0.2 : -0.2);   // tegishli yuzadan 0.2mm tashqarida (z-fight yo'q)
    // 2026-08-26 kech (foydalanuvchi, brrauf/245): «oq liniya», «oval buzildi», «seriy
    // vyemka» shikoyatlari — material'dan rang taxmin qilish va oq KONTUR olib tashlandi.
    // Endi rang faqat PANEL MA'LUMOTIdan: odnoton TO'Q panelda (p.col) — yumshoq och-kulrang,
    // qolgan hammasida (teksturali yog'och ham) — avvalgidek to'q belgi (2 kun oldingi ko'rinish).
    let _dk=false;
    try{
      if(p&&p.col&&!p.tex){
        const _cs=String(p.col);
        const _c=new THREE.Color(_cs.charAt(0)==='#'?_cs:('#'+_cs));
        _dk=(0.299*_c.r+0.587*_c.g+0.114*_c.b)<0.45;
      }
    }catch(e){}
    const m=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:_dk?0x9aa4b0:0x2b2f35,
      roughness:.95,metalness:.05,
      transparent:true,opacity:_dk?0.55:0.9,side:THREE.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false,
      polygonOffset:true,polygonOffsetFactor:-2,polygonOffsetUnits:-2});
    const gm=new THREE.Mesh(g,m); gm.renderOrder=1; gm.userData._pk=true;
    mesh.add(gm); decals.push(gm);
  }
  if(decals.length)mesh.userData._pkDecals=decals;
}


function resize(){if(!renderer||!camera)return;renderer.setSize(innerWidth,innerHeight);camera.aspect=innerWidth/innerHeight;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();}
function render(){
  if(!scene)return;
  // SPLASH ochiq (model qurilmoqda) — 3D sahnani chizish BEHUDA: uni splash to'sib turadi,
  // lekin 1000+ mesh har kadrda qayta chizilib, qurishni ~4 barobar sekinlashtirardi
  // (o'lchov: 1.5 s -> 6.5 s). Splash yopilgach odatdagidek chiziladi.
  if(!_spGone){ const _sp=document.getElementById('splash');
    if(!_sp) _spGone=true;                     // splash olib tashlangan — boshqa qidirmaymiz
    else if(!_sp.classList.contains('hide') && !SHOT){
      // Splash ostida FAQAT 2 ta "isitish" kadri chiziladi — shader kompilyatsiyasi va
      // birinchi geometriya yuklamasi shu yerda tugaydi. HAR kadrda chizsak 500+ mesh
      // asosiy oqimni to'ldirib, minimal kutish taymeri ham, so'nish animatsiyasi ham
      // kechikib ketardi (o'lchov: 3 s o'rniga 8.4 s, so'nish esa kadrsiz "sakrardi").
      if(!_spRelease || _spWarm>=2) return;
      _spWarm++;
    } }
  // YOYISH YORLIQLARI — ekranda doimiy o'lchamda (kamera masofasiga proporsional).
  // Avval o'lcham `_explAmp` ga bog'liq edi: izolyatsiya qilingan kichik blokda yorliq
  // mayda bo'lib o'qilmasdi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30).
  if(_explTags.length){
    const hh=dist*0.030;
    for(const it of _explTags){ const a=it.spr.userData.asp||2; it.spr.scale.set(hh*a,hh,1); }
  }
  const t=clock.getElapsedTime();
  // aylanish inersiyasi (silliq to'xtash)
  if(!dragging&&(velY||velX)){rotY+=velY;rotX+=velX;velY*=0.90;velX*=0.90;
    if(Math.abs(velY)<1e-4)velY=0;if(Math.abs(velX)<1e-4)velX=0;}
  if(_camFly){                      // 📷 parvoz: foydalanuvchi aralashsa bekor bo'ladi
    if(dragging){_camFly=null;}
    else{
      const sg=_camFly.segs[_camFly.idx];
      let k=(performance.now()-_camFly.t0)/sg.dur; if(k>1)k=1;
      const e=k<0.5?4*k*k*k:1-Math.pow(-2*k+2,3)/2;    // easeInOutCubic
      cx=sg.from.cx+(sg.to.cx-sg.from.cx)*e;
      cy=sg.from.cy+(sg.to.cy-sg.from.cy)*e;
      cz=sg.from.cz+(sg.to.cz-sg.from.cz)*e;
      dist=sg.from.dist+(sg.to.dist-sg.from.dist)*e;
      if(k>=1){ _camFly.idx++; _camFly.t0=performance.now();
        if(_camFly.idx>=_camFly.segs.length)_camFly=null; }
    }
  }
  rotX=Math.max(-1.553,Math.min(1.553,rotX));dist=Math.max(120,Math.min(60000,dist));   // ±89° (qutbda lookAt buzilmasin)
  // KIRISH ANIMATSIYASI: splash yopilganda model 12% uzoqroqdan asl masofasiga silliq keladi.
  // Faqat kameraga ta'sir qiladi — 'dist' o'zgarmaydi, shuning uchun foydalanuvchi zoom'i buzilmaydi.
  let _dv=dist;
  if(_introT0){ const _k=(performance.now()-_introT0)/850;
    if(_k>=1){ _introT0=0; }
    else { const _e=1-Math.pow(1-_k,3); _dv=dist*(1+0.12*(1-_e)); } }
  camera.position.set(cx+_dv*Math.cos(rotX)*Math.sin(rotY),cy+_dv*Math.sin(rotX),cz+_dv*Math.cos(rotX)*Math.cos(rotY));
  camera.lookAt(cx,cy,cz);
  // DINAMIK near/far — z-fighting'ning ASOSIY sababini yo'qotadi. Kamera markazdan 'dist' uzoqda,
  // sahna markaz atrofida 'modelR' radiusda. near/far'ni shu tor qobiqqa bog'lasak, depth-buffer
  // aniqligi har qanday masofada yetarli qoladi (aks holda near=1, far=100000'da uzoqlashganda
  // aniqlik z²/near bo'yicha yo'qolib, ichki yuzalar/chiziqlar tashqariga "sizib" miltillaydi).
  // ⚠️ 2026-09-02 (xabar #67 «если приближать и отдалять несколько раз, появляются
  // артефакты»): near/far AVVAL `dist` (kamera→NISHON masofasi) dan hisoblanardi. Uzoqlashtirish
  // nishonni modeldan siljitadi (zoomAt kursor nuqtasidan uzoqlashtiradi), shuning uchun
  // `dist` bilan kamera→MODEL masofasi bir-biridan uzoqlashib ketardi: o'lchovda dist=60000
  // bo'lganda kamera modeldan atigi 31700 uzoqda edi, near esa 54711 — ya'ni BUTUN MODEL
  // near tekisligidan yaqin qolib, kesilib ketardi. Endi haqiqiy masofadan hisoblaymiz.
  { const _hx=homeView?homeView.cx:cx, _hy=homeView?homeView.cy:cy, _hz=homeView?homeView.cz:cz;
    const _dc=Math.hypot(camera.position.x-_hx,camera.position.y-_hy,camera.position.z-_hz);
    const _n=Math.max(1,_dc-modelR-300), _f=_dc+modelR+300;
    if(camera.near!==_n||camera.far!==_f){camera.near=_n;camera.far=_f;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();} }
  if(selected){const _ei=0.5+0.42*Math.sin(t*3.2);for(const mt of _matsOf(selected))mt.emissiveIntensity=_ei;}
  if(selectedFurn&&_furnHi){ furnHiMat().emissiveIntensity=0.45+0.4*Math.sin(t*3.2);
    selectedFurn.im.getMatrixAt(selectedFurn.idx,_furnHi.matrix); }   // explode/animatsiya bilan sinxron
  const _dt=(t-_animPrevT)*1000; _animPrevT=t;   // animatsiya kadr vaqti (ms)
  if(animActive.size)tickAnim(_dt);
  renderer.render(scene,camera);
  renderViewCube();
  if(loupeVisible)drawLoupe();
  if(_screenshotPending){_screenshotPending=false;grabScreenshot();}   // shu KADRning o'zidan olamiz (buffer tozalanmasdan)
}
// ---- SKRINSHOT / ULASHISH ----
// renderer 'preserveDrawingBuffer' bilan yaratilmagan (unnecessarily performance narxi bo'lmasin
// deb) — shu sabab canvas'ni FAQAT shu kadr render bo'lgan zahoti (render() ichida) o'qiymiz,
// aks holda brauzer bufferni tozalab bo'sh/qora rasm qaytarishi mumkin edi.
function takeScreenshot(){ _screenshotPending=true; }
function grabScreenshot(){
  if(!renderer)return;
  renderer.domElement.toBlob(blob=>{
    if(!blob){toast(t('shotFail'),true);return;}
    const fname=(ARTICLE||'detal')+'.png';
    if(navigator.canShare && navigator.share){
      const file=new File([blob],fname,{type:'image/png'});
      if(navigator.canShare({files:[file]})){
        navigator.share({files:[file],title:'Detal QR'}).catch(()=>{});
        return;
      }
    }
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=fname;
    document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),4000);
    toast(t('shotOk'));
  },'image/png');
}

// ============ ANIMATSIYA (eshik/tortma ochilishi — Bazis 'Анимация') ============
function animChainOf(id){             // [jamlovchi(root) ... eng yaqin] tartibida
  const ch=[]; let i=id;
  while(i!=null && i>=0 && ANIMS[i]){ch.unshift(ANIMS[i]); i=ANIMS[i].parent;}
  return ch;
}
let _fiMap=null;                 // (im.id:idx) -> furnInstance — applyAnim'da yashirinlikni tekshirish uchun
function setupAnims(){
  _fiMap=null;
  ANIMS=(DATA&&DATA.anims)||[]; animMeshes=[]; animInstances=[]; animActive.clear(); animRoot=-1;
  // Har animatsiya tuguni LEAF (bolasiz)mi? Jamlovchi (bolasi bor) bloklar — struktura
  // tuguni, o'zi AYLANMAYDI (limit=0). Faqat leaf (haqiqiy eshik) limit=0 bo'lsa 100°
  // fallback oladi ("Покупное" eshikда Bazis Limit eksport qilmaydi). Busiz jamlovchi
  // o'z o'qi bilan 100° burilib, chap/o'ng eshikni bir tomonга ochib yuborardi.
  const _hasChild=new Set();
  for(const a of ANIMS){ if(a && a.parent!=null && a.parent>=0) _hasChild.add(a.parent); }
  for(const a of ANIMS){ if(a) a._leaf = !_hasChild.has(a.id); }
  for(const m of meshes){
    const aid=m.userData.anim;
    if(aid!=null && aid>=0 && ANIMS[aid]){
      m.updateMatrix(); m.matrixAutoUpdate=false;
      m.userData._abase=m.matrix.clone();
      m.userData._achain=animChainOf(aid);
      m.userData._aroot=ANIMS[aid].root;
      animMeshes.push(m);
    }
  }
  // modeldagi barcha animatsiya root'lari (hammasini birdan o'ynatish uchun)
  animAllRoots=[...new Set(ANIMS.filter(a=>a).map(a=>a.root).filter(r=>r!=null&&r>=0))];
  // animatsiya bo'lsa — yuqori PLAY tugmasi doim ko'rinadi (detal tanlanmasa hammasi o'ynaydi)
  document.getElementById('btnAnimTop').style.display = animAllRoots.length ? 'flex' : 'none';
}
function animMat(a,t,out){              // bitta animatsiya tugunining t (0..1) dagi transformi
  if(a.type===1){                      // aylanish: p0->p1 o'qi atrofida limit*t gradus
    // "Покупное изделие" (sotib olingan tayyor eshik/blok) uchun Bazis Limit'ni EKSPORT
    // QILMAYDI (XML'da Limit maydoni umuman yo'q -> 0 bo'lib o'qiladi) -> aylanish burchagi
    // doim 0 bo'lib, eshik hech qachon ochilmasdi. Shu holatda korpusdagi eng ko'p uchraydigan
    // qiymat (100°, 7000+ haqiqiy Limit ichida eng ustuvori) standart sifatida olinadi —
    // aniq burchak noma'lum, lekin ko'rsatish uchun 0 dan ancha yaxshiroq.
    // jamlovchi (non-leaf) limit=0 -> 0 (aylanmaydi); leaf limit=0 -> 100° fallback
    const lim=a.limit || (a._leaf ? 100 : 0);
    const ang=lim*Math.PI/180*t;
    const ax=new THREE.Vector3(a.p1[0]-a.p0[0],a.p1[1]-a.p0[1],a.p1[2]-a.p0[2]);
    if(ax.lengthSq()<1e-9){out.identity();return out;}
    ax.normalize();
    out.makeTranslation(a.p0[0],a.p0[1],a.p0[2]);
    out.multiply(new THREE.Matrix4().makeRotationAxis(ax,ang));
    out.multiply(new THREE.Matrix4().makeTranslation(-a.p0[0],-a.p0[1],-a.p0[2]));
  }else{                               // siljish: (p1-p0)*t
    out.makeTranslation((a.p1[0]-a.p0[0])*t,(a.p1[1]-a.p0[1])*t,(a.p1[2]-a.p0[2])*t);
  }
  return out;
}
function totalMat(chain,t,base){       // jamlovchi->ichki kompozitsiya, so'ng base
  const tot=new THREE.Matrix4(), one=new THREE.Matrix4();
  for(const a of chain){ animMat(a,t,one); tot.multiply(one); }
  tot.multiply(base);
  return tot;
}
function applyAnim(root,t){
  for(const m of animMeshes){
    if(m.userData._aroot!==root)continue;
    m.matrix.copy(totalMat(m.userData._achain,t,m.userData._abase));
    m.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate=true;
  }
  const dirty=new Set();
  // YASHIRILGAN furnitura (izolyatsiya/yashir) animatsiyada QAYTA PAYDO BO'LMASIN:
  // setMatrixAt to'g'ridan-to'g'ri instansiya matritsasini yozib, makeFurnInstance'dagi
  // "ko'rinmas = nol-matritsa" holatini buzardi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-29, KUHNA-ABDULLOH-AKA:
  // gaz plitasini izolyatsiya qilib animatsiya bosilsa boshqa furnitura chiqib qolardi).
  if(!_fiMap){ _fiMap=new Map(); for(const fi of furnInstances)_fiMap.set(fi.im.id+':'+fi.idx,fi); }
  for(const it of animInstances){
    if(it.root!==root)continue;
    const _fi=_fiMap.get(it.im.id+':'+it.idx); if(_fi&&!_fi.visible)continue;
    const mm=totalMat(it.chain,t,it.base);
    it.im.setMatrixAt(it.idx, mm);
    if(it.line){it.line.matrix.copy(mm);it.line.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate=true;}   // kontur ergashadi
    dirty.add(it.im);
  }
  dirty.forEach(im=>im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true);
}
// animActive: Map<root, {ph,el,holdMs}> — HAR animatsiya (detal) MUSTAQIL timelinega ega:
// bir necha detalni KETMA-KET yoki bir vaqtda ochish mumkin, har biri o'z vaqtida yopiladi.
// HAMMA animatsiya funksiyalari uchun yagona qoida: qo'lda yopilmasa 10 soniyada avto-yopiladi.
const ANIM_HOLD=10000;
function playAnim(root, hold){         // detalni ochadi -> turadi -> yopiladi (hold ms, standart 10s)
  if(root==null||root<0)return;
  animActive.set(root, {ph:'open', el:0, holdMs:hold||ANIM_HOLD});
  updateAnimBtn();
}
function playAllAnims(hold){           // butun modeldagi BARCHA animatsiyalar birdan
  for(const r of animAllRoots) animActive.set(r, {ph:'open', el:0, holdMs:hold||ANIM_HOLD});
  updateAnimBtn();
}
// root berilsa — o'sha animatsiya hozir OCHIQmi; berilmasa — umuman ochiq biror narsa bormi.
// 'close' fazasi allaqachon yopilish yo'lida, shuning uchun "ochiq" hisoblanmaydi.
function animIsOpen(root){
  if(root!=null&&root>=0){ const st=animActive.get(root); return !!(st&&st.ph!=='close'); }
  for(const st of animActive.values()) if(st.ph!=='close') return true;
  return false;
}
// Tugma ko'rinishi: yopiq -> ▶ (ochish), ochiq -> ◼ (yopish). DOM'ga faqat holat
// O'ZGARGANDA tegamiz (tickAnim har kadr chaqiradi).
let _animBtnOpen=null;
function updateAnimBtn(){
  const b=document.getElementById('btnAnimTop'); if(!b)return;
  const open=animIsOpen(animRoot>=0?animRoot:null);
  if(open===_animBtnOpen)return;
  _animBtnOpen=open;
  b.classList.toggle('on',open);
  b.title=t(open?'animStop':'animPlay');
}
function closeAnim(root){              // ERTA yopish: root berilsa o'shani, aks holda HAMMASINI
  const cl=st=>{ if(st&&st.ph!=='close'){st.ph='close';st.el=0;} };
  if(root!=null){ cl(animActive.get(root)); }
  else { for(const st of animActive.values()) cl(st); }
  updateAnimBtn();
}
function tickAnim(dt){
  const OPEN=900, CLOSE=900;
  for(const [root,A] of animActive){
    const HOLD=A.holdMs||700;
    A.el+=dt; let t;
    if(A.ph==='open'){ t=Math.min(A.el/OPEN,1); if(A.el>=OPEN){A.ph='hold';A.el=0;} }
    else if(A.ph==='hold'){ t=1; if(A.el>=HOLD){A.ph='close';A.el=0;} }
    else { t=Math.max(1-A.el/CLOSE,0); if(A.el>=CLOSE){ applyAnim(root,0); animActive.delete(root); continue; } }
    const te=t<.5?2*t*t:1-Math.pow(-2*t+2,2)/2;   // ease-in-out
    applyAnim(root,te);
  }
  updateAnimBtn();     // avto-yopilganda ham tugma ▶ ga qaytadi
}

function selectByDes(des){
  const m=meshes.find(m=>m.userData.des===des);
  if(m){select(m);return true;}
  // topilmadi
  select(null);
  document.getElementById('iDes').textContent=des;
  document.getElementById('iName').textContent=t('notFound');
  document.getElementById('iL1').textContent='Bu oboznacheniya modelda yo‘q.';
  document.getElementById('iL2').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('info').style.display='block';
  return false;
}

// panel materiali BITTA bo'lishi ham, ARRAY bo'lishi ham mumkin (kromka torec materiali) —
// select/explode uchun barcha materiallarni bir xil yangilaymiz
function _matsOf(m){const x=m&&m.material;return Array.isArray(x)?x:(x?[x]:[]);}
function select(mesh){
  clearFurnSel();               // panel tanlansa furnitura tanlovi bekor bo'ladi
  clearSelGhost();
  selected=mesh; blockSel=null;
  for(const m of meshes){
    const sel=(m===mesh);
    const _own=(m.userData._op!==undefined?m.userData._op:1);   // detalning ASL shaffofligi (oyna .55)
    // RENTGEN REJIM (xrayOn): tanlanganda qolganlari xiralashadi (0.12), tanlangani esa
    // yarim-shaffof (0.85) - ichidagi teshik/furnitura ko'rinadi. O'chirilgan bo'lsa -
    // ODDIY tanlash: hech kim xiralashmaydi (faqat tanlangani sariq yoritiladi).
    const op=mesh?(xrayOn?(sel?0.85:0.12):_own):_own;
    // Tanlangan holatда yuza depthWrite=false — shaffof qilib ichidagi furnitura/teshik ko'rinsin.
    // LEKIN tanlangan YUPQA (≤6mm laser-kesim) panel uchun depthWrite=TRUE: yupqa panelда ichki
    // furnitura yo'q, va depthWrite=true panelning O'Z orqa yuzasi/slot-ramkasini to'sadi ->
    // through-slot (o'yiq) "ikkilangan chiziq"i yo'qoladi (2-muammo). Qalin panellar tegilmaydi.
    const selThin=sel && Math.abs(m.userData.thick||16)<=6;
    const _owndw=(m.userData._dw!==undefined?m.userData._dw:true);
    const dw=(mesh&&xrayOn)?(selThin?true:false):_owndw;
    for(const mt of _matsOf(m)){
      mt.opacity=op; mt.emissive=new THREE.Color(sel?0xff7a1a:0x000000);
      mt.emissiveIntensity=sel?0.5:0; mt.depthWrite=dw;
    }
    // Tanlangan detal konturi (edge) — OPAQUE (transparent=false) chiziladi. SABAB (video +
    // sabab-izolyatsiya bilan isbotlangan): transparent chiziq TRANSPARENT navbatning oxirida
    // chiziladi, u paytda tanlangan yuza depth yozib bo'lgan -> chiziq depth-testdan burchakка
    // qarab YIQILIB "goh bor goh yo'q" bo'lib vijirlar edi. OPAQUE chiziq esa OPAQUE navbatда
    // (hamma transparentdan OLDIN) chiziladi, depth yozadi, yuza esa polygonOffset bilan ortда
    // qolib chiziq ustiga bo'yay olmaydi -> kontur HAR BURCHAKDA barqaror qora ko'rinadi.
    // Tanlanmagan/ghost holatda esa yarim-shaffoflik kerak (0.65/0.1) -> transparent qoladi.
    if(m.userData._eg){ const em=m.userData._eg.material;
      // RENTGEN O'CHIQ bo'lsa qolgan detallar QUYUQ qoladi va tanlangan detal ularning
      // ortiga o'tib ko'rinmay qolardi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-16). Endi tanlangan detalning
      // KONTURI chuqurlik tekshiruvisiz (depthTest=false), to'q-sariq rangda va eng oxirida
      // chiziladi -> detal qayerdaligi har burchakda bilinadi. Yuza bo'yalmaydi.
      const thru=sel&&!!mesh;      // HAR IKKI rejimda: tanlangan detal konturi devor orqali
      em.depthTest=!thru;
      em.color.setHex(thru?0xff6a00:0x000000);
      // MUHIM: panel materiallari HAMMASI transparent:true bilan quriladi (rentgen rejimi
      // uchun kerak). Shu sababli OPAQUE chiziq panellardan OLDIN chizilib, ular ustidan
      // bo'yalib ketardi — rentgen O'CHIQ bo'lganda tanlangan detal konturi devor ortidan
      // UMUMAN ko'rinmasdi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-22). Chiziqni ham TRANSPARENT navbatga
      // qo'yamiz (opacity 1 — ko'rinishi o'zgarmaydi) va renderOrder bilan ENG OXIRIDA
      // chizamiz. depthTest=false bo'lgani uchun eski "vijirlash" muammosi qaytmaydi.
      if(sel){ em.transparent=true; em.opacity=1; }
      // Tanlanmaganда ham kontur KO'RINSIN, lekin YENGILROQ (foydalanuvchi so'rovi 2026-08-08):
      // avval 0.1 edi — deyarli ko'rinmasdi. Endi 0.3 — tanlangan (to'q qora) fonida boshqa
      // detallar joyi va shakli ochroq chiziq bilan bilinib turadi (kontekst yo'qolmaydi).
      else   { em.transparent=true;  em.opacity=(mesh&&xrayOn)?0.3:0.65; }
      em.needsUpdate=true; m.userData._eg.renderOrder=(sel&&mesh)?1001:0; }
    if(m.userData._pkDecals){const pop=(mesh&&xrayOn)?(sel?0.9:0.1):0.9;for(const d of m.userData._pkDecals)d.material.opacity=pop;}
  }
  if(mesh)addSelGhost(mesh);          // to'silgan bo'lsa ham ko'rinsin (yengil qatlam)
  const inf=document.getElementById('info');
  if(mesh){
    const p=mesh.userData;
    document.getElementById('iDes').textContent=p.des||'—';
    document.getElementById('iName').textContent=p.name||'';
    const W=Math.round(Math.max(p.w,p.h)),H=Math.round(Math.min(p.w,p.h));
    document.getElementById('iL1').innerHTML=`${esc(t('sz'))}: <span class="sz">${W} × ${H} × ${p.thick} mm</span>`+utolBadge(p);
    const butts=(p.butts||[]).map(b=>b.sign).filter((v,i,a)=>v&&a.indexOf(v)===i).join(', ');
    const nh=(p.holes||[]).length;
    // TEKSTURA YO'NALISHI (foydalanuvchi so'rovi 2026-09-03): material nomidan OLDIN
    // strelka turadi.
    // ❗ 13:15 TUZATISH (muammo xabari #79, SoupOrStat/«су»: «направление текстуры
    // отображается некорректно»): avval strelka «tola UZUN tomon bo'ylabmi?» degan
    // savolga javob berardi. Amalda deyarli hamma detal tolasi uzun tomon bo'ylab
    // kesiladi — natijada 121 panelli modelда HAMMA detal \u2194 ko'rsatardi, hatto
    // tolasi TIK turgan 380×1185 eshikda ham. Endi strelka Bazisning O'Z ma'nosini
    // takrorlaydi (`TextureOrientation`): \u2195 = tola detal BO'YI (kontur Y) bo'ylab,
    // \u2194 = ENI (kontur X) bo'ylab — ya'ni ko'z bilan ko'rinayotgan yo'nalish.
    const _gy=p._gy;
    const _arr=(_gy==null)?'':((_gy===1)?'\u2195':'\u2194');
    document.getElementById('iL2').innerHTML=esc(t('mat'))+': '
      +(_arr?'<span class="gdir" title="'+esc(t('grainDir'))+'">'+_arr+'</span> ':'')
      +esc(p.mat||'—');
    // KROMKA qatori (muammo xabarlari #74/#76, mebel-na-zakaz-rb «нет кромки», 2026-09-03):
    // kromka ma'lumoti JSON'da bor edi (butts), 2D chizmada chiqardi, lekin 3D kartada
    // KO'RSATILMASDI — foydalanuvchi «kromka yo'q» deb o'ylagan. Endi: «Кромка: ПВХ 0.8-19 · 4».
    { const l3=document.getElementById('iL3');
      if(l3){ const nb=(p.butts||[]).length;
        if(butts){ l3.style.display=''; l3.innerHTML=esc(t('kromka'))+': '+esc(butts)+(nb?' \u00b7 '+nb:''); }
        else l3.style.display='none'; } }
    const bd=document.getElementById('btnDrill');
    bd.style.display='flex';     // 2D chizma + razmerlar — har detal uchun
    updBazisBtn(p);              // «Bazis chizmasi» — faqat chizmasi bor detalda
    document.getElementById('drillLbl').textContent = nh ? tp('drillN',{n:nh}) : t('drill2d');
    // PLAY (yuqori) — detalda animatsiya bo'lsa o'shaniki, bo'lmasa tugma barchasini o'ynatadi
    const aid=p.anim;
    animRoot=(aid!=null&&aid>=0&&ANIMS[aid])?ANIMS[aid].root:-1;
    updateAnimBtn();
    inf.style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('hint').style.display='none';
    document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(d=>d.classList.toggle('sel',d.dataset.des===p.des));
    if(drillOpen)openDrill(p);   // 2D ochiq bo'lsa — yangi detalga (teshikli/teshiksiz) yangilanadi
  }else{
    inf.style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('btnDrill').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('btnBazis').style.display='none';
    animRoot=-1;   // detal tanlanmadi -> PLAY tugmasi BARCHA animatsiyani o'ynatadi (tugma ko'rinib turadi)
    updateAnimBtn();
    document.querySelectorAll('#list .it').forEach(d=>d.classList.remove('sel'));
    closeDrill();
  }
  updateHideBtn();   // detal holatiga qarab yashir/qaytar tugmasi ikonkasi yangilanadi
  refreshSelVis();   // tanlangan detalga taluqli furnitura + teshiklarni ko'rsatadi (qolganini yashiradi)
}

// ===== FURNITURA TANLASH =====
// Furnitura = InstancedMesh nusxalari (panel emas). Bosilganda porloq (highlight) mesh qo'yamiz
// va info panelini furnitura ma'lumoti bilan to'ldiramiz. Panel tanlansa bu bekor bo'ladi.
function furnHiMat(){
  return _furnHiMat||(_furnHiMat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color:0xff8a2a,emissive:0xff7a1a,emissiveIntensity:.6,
    roughness:.35,metalness:.3,transparent:true,opacity:.92,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));
}
function clearFurnSel(){
  if(!selectedFurn&&!_furnHi)return;
  selectedFurn=null;
  if(_furnHi){ if(_furnHi.parent)_furnHi.parent.remove(_furnHi); _furnHi=null; }
}
// ekran nuqtasidagi ko'rinadigan furnitura nusxasini topadi (raycaster oldindan sozlangan)
function furnHitAt(){
  if(!furn3D||!furnMarkerGroup||!furnMeshList.length)return null;
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(furnMeshList,false);
  for(const h of hits){
    if(h.instanceId==null)continue;
    const fi=furnInstances.find(x=>x.im===h.object&&x.idx===h.instanceId);
    if(fi&&fi.visible)return{fi,dist:h.distance};
  }
  return null;
}
function selectFurn(fi){
  select(null);                 // panellarni asl holatiga qaytaradi (info yashiriladi)
  clearFurnSel();
  selectedFurn=fi;
  // porloq mesh — shu nusxaning geometriyasi + joriy matritsasi (explode/animatsiya bilan sinxron)
  _furnHi=new THREE.Mesh(fi.im.geometry,furnHiMat());
  _furnHi.matrixAutoUpdate=false;_furnHi.renderOrder=2;
  fi.im.getMatrixAt(fi.idx,_furnHi.matrix);
  model.add(_furnHi);
  // info paneli
  const f=fi.fit||{};
  const kind=(f.kind&&f.kind!=='defaultobject')?f.kind:t('furnOne');
  let n=0;for(const x of furnInstances){if(((x.fit&&x.fit.kind)||'')===(f.kind||''))n++;}
  document.getElementById('iDes').textContent='🔩';
  document.getElementById('iName').textContent=kind;
  document.getElementById('iL1').textContent=tp('furnQty',{n});
  document.getElementById('iL2').textContent=t('furnSel');
  document.getElementById('btnDrill').style.display='none';   // furnituraning 2D chizmasi yo'q
  document.getElementById('info').style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('hint').style.display='none';
  // animatsiya: furniturada 'anim' bo'lsa yuqoridagi PLAY o'shani o'ynatadi
  const aid=f.anim;
  animRoot=(aid!=null&&aid>=0&&ANIMS[aid])?ANIMS[aid].root:-1;
  updateAnimBtn();
  updateHideBtn();
}

{const _ma=document.getElementById('mAll');if(_ma)_ma.onclick=()=>{select(null);closeMenu();};}   // menyudan olib tashlangan (bo'sh joyga bosish ham tanlovni bekor qiladi)
{const _md=document.getElementById('mDims');if(_md)_md.onclick=()=>{toggleDims();closeMenu();};}   // menyudan olib tashlangan
//===FASAD-BEGIN=== (bu belgilar orasidagi kod Node testida ham ishlatiladi:
//                   detalqr/tools/fasad/test_fac_v1.js — ikkinchi nusxa YO'Q, manba shu yer)
/* FASADNI ANIQLASH — signal-ball tizimi (2026-09-04, foydalanuvchi bergan tartib bo'yicha).
   Avvalgi usul FAQAT nomga qarardi va topilgan fasadning butun `gid` guruhini yashirardi —
   #59 muammosi (bomens): 32 fasad o'rniga 111 detal (44%) yashirinardi, chunki bu modelda
   `gid` guruhi = butun shkaf (бок/дно/крышка/зад стенка/фасад).
   Endi har panel BALL to'playdi, 3 balldan boshlab fasad hisoblanadi:
     nom              +3   (фасад/дверь/eshik… va bazadan topilgan xato yozilishlar)
     petlya kosasi    +3   (Ø25–45 mm ko'r teshik yoki holes[].type='Petlya')
     ruchka           +2   (ruchka furniturasi panel ichida yoki 96/128/160/192 teshik jufti)
