     animatsiya       +2   (FAQAT o'z anim guruhining eng oldingi paneliga — yashik oldi;
                            guruh butun shkaf bo'lsa umuman berilmaydi)
     ramka pazi       +2   (paz 4 tomonning kamida 3 tasi bo'ylab — oynali/vstavkali ramka)
     geometriya       +1   (tik panel, blok old tekisligida, yuzasi yetarli)
   Vetolar: yotiq panel (polka/tom/pol) va yupqa (<8 mm) panel — nom/petlya/ramka signali
   bo'lmasa fasad emas; nom bo'yicha «bok/dno/zad/planka…» — musbat nom signali bo'lmasa −3.
   Xavfsizlik: bir blokda 60% dan ko'p yoki modelda 45% dan ko'p chiqsa — faqat kuchli
   (nom yoki petlya) signallilar qoladi. Guruhni butunlay yashirish QOIDASI YO'Q; fasadga
   yopishgan nalichnik/oyna/shtapik faqat GEOMETRIK yaqinlik bo'yicha qo'shiladi. */
const FAC_POS=['фасад','fasad','fasat','fafad','fasda','фасат','дверь','двер','dver','эшик',
               'eshik','eshig','ешик','ешиг','створк','витрин','fasadcha'];
const FAC_NEG=['полка','polka','бок','боковин','дно','крышк','крыша','зад','стенка','планка',
               'цокол','стойка','стоев','перегород','tokcha','tom ','pol ','царга','ребро'];
const FAC_MINSC=3;

function _fq(q){const x=q[0],y=q[1],z=q[2],w=q[3];return[
  [1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-z*w),2*(x*z+y*w)],
  [2*(x*y+z*w),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-x*w)],
  [2*(x*z-y*w),2*(y*z+x*w),1-2*(x*x+y*y)]];}
function _fW(p){const m=_fq(p.quat||[0,0,0,1]),o=p.pos||[0,0,0];
  return(x,y,z)=>[o[0]+m[0][0]*x+m[0][1]*y+m[0][2]*z,
                  o[1]+m[1][0]*x+m[1][1]*y+m[1][2]*z,
                  o[2]+m[2][0]*x+m[2][1]*y+m[2][2]*z];}
function _fN(p){const m=_fq(p.quat||[0,0,0,1]);return[m[0][2],m[1][2],m[2][2]];}
const _fPtsC=new WeakMap();
function _fPts(p){let c=_fPtsC.get(p);if(c)return c;
  const w=_fW(p),t=Math.abs(+p.thick||0),o=[];
  for(const q of(p.poly||[])){o.push(w(q[0],q[1],0));o.push(w(q[0],q[1],t));}
  _fPtsC.set(p,o);return o;}
function _fBox(p){const pts=_fPts(p);if(!pts.length)return null;
  const b=[[1e18,-1e18],[1e18,-1e18],[1e18,-1e18]];
  for(const q of pts)for(let i=0;i<3;i++){if(q[i]<b[i][0])b[i][0]=q[i];if(q[i]>b[i][1])b[i][1]=q[i];}
  return b;}
const _fArC=new WeakMap();
function _fArea(p){let v=_fArC.get(p);if(v!==undefined)return v;
  const c=p.poly||[];let a=0;
  for(let i=0;i<c.length;i++){const u=c[i],w=c[(i+1)%c.length];a+=u[0]*w[1]-w[0]*u[1];}
  v=Math.abs(a)/2;_fArC.set(p,v);return v;}
function _fLoc(p){const c=p.poly||[];if(!c.length)return null;
  let x0=1e18,x1=-1e18,y0=1e18,y1=-1e18;
  for(const q of c){if(q[0]<x0)x0=q[0];if(q[0]>x1)x1=q[0];if(q[1]<y0)y0=q[1];if(q[1]>y1)y1=q[1];}
  return[x0,x1,y0,y1];}
function _fName(p){return String(p.name||'').toLowerCase().trim();}

// --- 1) NOM ---
// RAQAMLI KOD («8.2», «Ф 01.02», «R 8.4») — ba'zi brendlar fasadni faqat kod bilan
// nomlaydi (brrauf: «R 8.2» fasad, «R 8.1 karkaz» esa karkas). Bu KUCHSIZ signal (+2):
// o'zi yetmaydi, yoniga geometriya yoki petlya kerak. Qo'shimchali nom («…karkaz»,
// «Бок.шк.лев.») bu qolipga TUSHMAYDI — 58123 dagi «deyarli hamma detal yashirildi»
// muammosi shundan kelib chiqqan edi.
const FAC_CODE=/^[^\d]{0,4}\d+(?:\.\d+)+\s*$/;
function _sigName(p){const n=_fName(p);if(!n)return 0;
  for(const k of FAC_POS)if(n.indexOf(k)>=0)return 3;
  for(const k of FAC_NEG)if(n.indexOf(k)>=0)return -3;
  if(FAC_CODE.test(n))return 2;
  return 0;}
// --- 2) PETLYA KOSASI (Ø35) ---
// ⚠️ Detal HAQIQIY PLITA bo'lishi shart: «Гайка»/«Шпилька» kabi mayda yumaloq detallar ham
// Ø25–45 teshik bilan chiqadi (varlamov-sergey «Стеллаж»: 25/59 detal noto'g'ri yashirilgan edi).
// Fasadning eng kichik tomoni kamida 120 mm bo'ladi, kosa esa 35 mm — shu farq filtr bo'ladi.
function _sigHinge(p){
  const lb=_fLoc(p);if(!lb)return 0;
  const w=lb[1]-lb[0],h=lb[3]-lb[2];
  if(Math.min(w,h)<120||_fArea(p)<20000)return 0;
  const t2=Math.abs(+p.thick||0);
  if(t2<8||t2>60)return 0;
  let n=0;
  for(const q of(p.holes||[])){if(!q)continue;const d=+q.d||0,t=String(q.type||'');
    if(d>=25&&d<=45&&!q.thru&&(+q.depth||0)>=6)n++;
    else if(t==='Petlya'&&d>=25)n++;}
  // Eshikda petlya DOIM kamida ikkita. Bitta Ø35 ko'r teshik — ehtimol boshqa narsa
  // (kabel/quvur o'rni): u faqat 1 ball beradi, o'zi yetmaydi.
  return n>=2?3:(n===1?1:0);}
// --- 3) RUCHKA ---
const _RU=/ручк|ruchka|handle|скоба|skoba|рейлинг|knopka|кнопка-ручк/i;
function _sigHandle(p,fits){
  const b=_fBox(p);if(b){for(const f of(fits||[])){
    if(!f||!f.pos||!_RU.test(String(f.kind||'')))continue;
    let ok=true;for(let i=0;i<3;i++)if(f.pos[i]<b[i][0]-10||f.pos[i]>b[i][1]+10){ok=false;break;}
    if(ok)return 2;}}
  // ruchka teshiklari: FAQAT o'tkir (thru) — evrovint/shkant ham 4.5–5 mm bo'ladi va
  // 96/128 oraliqda uchraydi (yashikning orqa devorida ham), shuning uchun `thru` shart.
  const hs=(p.holes||[]).filter(h=>h&&h.thru&&(+h.d||0)>=3.5&&(+h.d||0)<=6.5);
  for(let i=0;i<hs.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<hs.length;j++){
    const dx=Math.abs(hs[i].x-hs[j].x),dy=Math.abs(hs[i].y-hs[j].y);
    const d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(Math.min(dx,dy)<=2&&[96,128,160,192,224,256,288,320].some(s=>Math.abs(d-s)<=2))return 2;}
  return 0;}
// --- 5) RAMKA PAZI (gir aylana) ---
function _sigFrame(p){
  const lb=_fLoc(p);if(!lb)return 0;
  const[x0,x1,y0,y1]=lb,W=x1-x0,H=y1-y0;
  if(W<80||H<80)return 0;
  const near=[0,0,0,0],tol=Math.max(30,Math.min(W,H)*0.12);
  for(const q of(p.pockets||[])){
    const pts=(q&&q.pts)||[];if(pts.length<2)continue;
    let a0=1e18,a1=-1e18,b0=1e18,b1=-1e18;
    for(const v of pts){if(v[0]<a0)a0=v[0];if(v[0]>a1)a1=v[0];if(v[1]<b0)b0=v[1];if(v[1]>b1)b1=v[1];}
    const ln=Math.max(a1-a0,b1-b0);
    if(a0-x0<=tol&&(b1-b0)>=H*0.4)near[0]=1;        // chap
    if(x1-a1<=tol&&(b1-b0)>=H*0.4)near[1]=1;        // o'ng
    if(b0-y0<=tol&&(a1-a0)>=W*0.4)near[2]=1;        // past
    if(y1-b1<=tol&&(a1-a0)>=W*0.4)near[3]=1;        // tepa
    if(ln<Math.min(W,H)*0.3)continue;
  }
  const k=near[0]+near[1]+near[2]+near[3];
  return k>=3?2:0;}
//===FASAD-MID===
// --- blok (gid) guruhlari: juda kichik guruh bo'lsa yuqoriga chiqamiz ---
function _fGroups(panels){
  const by=new Map(),root=new Map(),rootOf=new Map();
  for(const p of panels){const g=String(p.gid||'');if(!by.has(g))by.set(g,[]);by.get(g).push(p);
    const r=g.split('/')[0];if(!root.has(r))root.set(r,[]);root.get(r).push(p);rootOf.set(p,r);}
  const par=g=>{const i=g.lastIndexOf('/');return i>0?g.slice(0,i):'';};
  const of=new Map();
  for(const p of panels){
    let g=String(p.gid||''),grp=by.get(g)||[];
    let guard=0;
    while(grp.length<3&&g&&guard++<6){
      const q=par(g);if(!q)break;
      g=q;grp=panels.filter(x=>String(x.gid||'')===g||String(x.gid||'').indexOf(g+'/')===0);
    }
    of.set(p,grp.length?grp:[p]);
  }
  // blok = ILDIZ gid guruhi (butun shkaf). Geometrik test SHU daraja bo'yicha bo'lishi shart:
  // yashikning ichki guruhida orqa devor ham «eng chetda» chiqadi, shkaf darajasida esa yo'q.
  const blk=new Map();
  for(const p of panels){const r=rootOf.get(p);const g=root.get(r)||[p];
    blk.set(p,g.length>=3?g:(of.get(p)||[p]));}
  return {by,of,root,blk};
}
const _prC=new Map();
function _projRange(list,n,key){
  if(key){const k=key+'|'+n[0].toFixed(2)+','+n[1].toFixed(2)+','+n[2].toFixed(2);
    const h=_prC.get(k);if(h)return h;
    const r=_projRange(list,n);_prC.set(k,r);return r;}
  let a=1e18,b=-1e18;
  for(const p of list)for(const q of _fPts(p)){const v=q[0]*n[0]+q[1]*n[1]+q[2]*n[2];
    if(v<a)a=v;if(v>b)b=v;}
  return[a,b];}
// --- 4) ANIMATSIYA: faqat o'z guruhining OLDINGI paneliga (yashik oldi / eshik) ---
function _animFronts(panels,groups){
  const by=new Map(),out=new Set();
  for(const p of panels){const a=p.anim;if(a===undefined||a===null||a===-1)continue;
    const k=String(a);if(!by.has(k))by.set(k,[]);by.get(k).push(p);}
  for(const[,grp]of by){
    if(grp.length>10)continue;                       // butun shkafga qo'yilgan animatsiya
    let hi=-1e18,lo=1e18;
    for(const p of grp)for(const q of _fPts(p)){if(q[1]>hi)hi=q[1];if(q[1]<lo)lo=q[1];}
    if(grp.length>6&&hi-lo>900)continue;             // baland va ko'p detalli — shkaf bloki
    if(grp.length===1){out.add(grp[0]);continue;}
    const inG=new Set(grp);
    let best=null,bestv=-1e18;
    for(const p of grp){
      const n=_fN(p);if(Math.abs(n[1])>0.55)continue;             // yotiq — old panel emas
      const carc=(groups.of.get(p)||[]).filter(x=>!inG.has(x));
      const pr=_projRange([p],n);
      let v;
      if(carc.length>=2){const cr=_projRange(carc,n);v=pr[1]-cr[1];}  // karkasdan qancha oldinda
      else{const gr=_projRange(grp.filter(x=>x!==p),n);v=pr[1]-gr[1];}
      if(v>bestv){bestv=v;best=p;}
    }
    if(best&&bestv>-5)out.add(best);
  }
  return out;
}
// --- 6) GEOMETRIYA: tik, blok chegarasida, yuzasi yetarli ---
function _sigGeom(p,grp,key){
  const n=_fN(p);
  if(Math.abs(n[1])>0.55)return 0;                    // yotiq (polka/tom/pol)
  const pr=_projRange([p],n),gr=_projRange(grp,n,key);
  const out=(gr[1]-pr[1]<=2)||(pr[0]-gr[0]<=2);
  if(!out)return 0;
  // shu yo'nalishga PARALLEL panellarning eng katta yuzasi (blok×yo'nalish bo'yicha keshlanadi)
  let ga=0;
  const ak=key?('A'+key+'|'+n[0].toFixed(2)+','+n[1].toFixed(2)+','+n[2].toFixed(2)):null;
  if(ak&&_prC.has(ak))ga=_prC.get(ak);
  else{for(const q of grp){const nn=_fN(q);
        if(Math.abs(nn[0]*n[0]+nn[1]*n[1]+nn[2]*n[2])>0.85)ga=Math.max(ga,_fArea(q));}
       if(ak)_prC.set(ak,ga);}
  return(ga>0&&_fArea(p)>=ga*0.35)?1:0;
}

function computeFacades(panels,fittings){
  _prC.clear();
  const ps=(panels||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.poly&&p.poly.length>=3&&!p.layout);
  const info=new Map();
  if(!ps.length)return{set:new Set(),info};
  const groups=_fGroups(ps);
  const anim=_animFronts(ps,groups);
  for(const p of ps){
    const why=[];let s=0;
    const sn=_sigName(p);
    if(sn===3){s+=3;why.push('nom');}
    else if(sn===2){s+=2;why.push('kod');}
    else if(sn<0){s-=3;why.push('nom−');}
    if(_sigHinge(p)){s+=3;why.push('petlya35');}
    if(_sigHandle(p,fittings)){s+=2;why.push('ruchka');}
    if(anim.has(p)){s+=2;why.push('anim');}
    if(_sigFrame(p)){s+=2;why.push('ramka');}
    const grp=groups.of.get(p)||[p];
    if(_sigGeom(p,groups.blk.get(p)||grp,'B'+String(p.gid||'').split('/')[0])){s+=1;why.push('geom');}
    const t=Math.abs(+p.thick||0),n=_fN(p);
    const strong=(sn===3)||_sigHinge(p)||_sigFrame(p);
    if(Math.abs(n[1])>0.55&&!strong){s-=3;why.push('yotiq');}      // polka/tom/pol
    if(t<8&&!strong){s-=3;why.push('yupqa');}                       // ХДФ orqa devor
    info.set(p,{s,why,grp});
  }
  let set=new Set(ps.filter(p=>info.get(p).s>=FAC_MINSC));
  // --- XAVFSIZLIK TO'RI (ASOSIY aniqlash bo'yicha, yopishganlardan OLDIN):
  // blokda 60%, modelda 45% dan oshsa faqat kuchli signallilar qoladi. To'r yopishgan
  // detallardan OLDIN qo'llanadi — aks holda ko'p eshikli modelda (chizmapro «Ячейка»:
  // 54 eshik + har biriga dasta va plastina) to'r butun to'plamni yiqitib yuborardi.
  const strongOnly=p=>{const i=info.get(p);return i&&(i.why.indexOf('nom')>=0||i.why.indexOf('petlya35')>=0)&&i.s>=FAC_MINSC;};
  for(const[,grp]of groups.root){
    if(grp.length<3)continue;
    const hid=grp.filter(p=>set.has(p));
    if(hid.length>grp.length*0.6)for(const p of hid)if(!strongOnly(p))set.delete(p);
  }
  if(set.size>ps.length*0.45)for(const p of[...set])if(!strongOnly(p))set.delete(p);
  // --- yopishgan detallar (nalichnik / oyna / shtapik): FAQAT fasadning oldida turganlari
  const add=new Set();
  for(const f of set){
    const n=_fN(f),fr=_projRange([f],n),grp=info.get(f).grp,fa=_fArea(f);
    for(const q of grp){
      if(set.has(q)||add.has(q))continue;
      if(_fArea(q)>fa*0.75)continue;                 // katta detal (bok/dno) — hech qachon
      if(info.get(q)&&info.get(q).s<=-3)continue;    // nomi aniq karkas detali
      const qr=_projRange([q],n);
      const th=Math.abs(+f.thick||18);
      if(qr[0]>=fr[0]-2&&qr[1]<=fr[1]+th+22&&qr[1]>fr[0]) {
        // fasad qalinligi ichida yoki undan biroz oldinda — ustiga yopishgan detal
        // fasad IZI ichida bo'lsin: kichik detalning KO'LANKASI (fasad tekisligiga
        // proyeksiyasi) fasad izining ichiga kamida 60% tushsin. Ko'lanka FAQAT ikki
        // o'q bo'yicha o'lchanadi — normal bo'yicha emas: ruchka/dasta fasadning
        // OLDIDA turadi, 3D bbox kesishmasi bilan o'lchasak, u tushib qolardi
        // (chizmapro «Ячейка» modelida 54 ta dasta osilib qolgan edi).
        const b1=_fBox(f),b2=_fBox(q);
        let ai=0;for(let i=1;i<3;i++)if(Math.abs(n[i])>Math.abs(n[ai]))ai=i;
        let ar=1,own=1;
        for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
          if(i===ai)continue;
          const lo=Math.max(b1[i][0]-4,b2[i][0]),hi=Math.min(b1[i][1]+4,b2[i][1]);
          ar*=Math.max(0,hi-lo);own*=Math.max(1,b2[i][1]-b2[i][0]);}
        if(ar>=own*0.6)add.add(q);
      }
    }
  }
  for(const q of add){set.add(q);if(info.has(q))info.get(q).why.push('yopishgan');}
  return{set,info};
}
//===FASAD-END===
// Viewer ko'prigi: model panellaridan fasad to'plamini hisoblab, mesh'larga bog'laydi.
function isFacadePanel(ud){const r=_facCache();return !!(r&&r.set.has(ud));}
let _FACR=null,_FACSRC=null;
function _facCache(){
  const ps=(typeof DATA!=='undefined'&&DATA&&DATA.panels)||null;
  if(!ps)return null;
  if(_FACR&&_FACSRC===ps)return _FACR;
  _FACSRC=ps;_FACR=computeFacades(ps,(DATA&&DATA.fittings)||[]);
  return _FACR;
}
function _facadeMeshes(){
  const r=_facCache(),out=new Set();
  if(!r)return out;
  for(const m of meshes)if(m.userData&&r.set.has(m.userData))out.add(m);
  return out;
}
// Diagnostika (konsolda): fasadFikr() -> har panel uchun ball va signallar
function fasadFikr(){const r=_facCache();if(!r)return[];
  const o=[];for(const[p,i]of r.info)if(i.s>=FAC_MINSC-2)
    o.push({nom:p.name,gid:p.gid,ball:i.s,signal:i.why.join('+'),yashirin:r.set.has(p)});
  o.sort((a,b)=>b.ball-a.ball);return o;}

let facadesHidden=false; const _facSet=new Set();
function toggleFacades(){
  if(!facadesHidden){
    let n=0;
    const fac=_facadeMeshes();
    for(const m of fac){ if(m.visible){ m.visible=false; _facSet.add(m); n++; } }
    if(!n){ toast(t('facNone')); return; }
    facadesHidden=true;
    if(selected&&_facSet.has(selected))select(null);
    if(blockSel&&blockSel.some(b=>_facSet.has(b)))clearBlock();
    toast(tp('facN',{n}));
  }else{
    for(const m of _facSet){ if(!hiddenSet.has(m))m.visible=true; }
    _facSet.clear(); facadesHidden=false;
  }
  const l=document.getElementById('mFacadesLbl'); if(l)l.textContent=t(facadesHidden?'facShow':'facHide');
  const btn=document.getElementById('mFacades'); if(btn)btn.classList.toggle('on',facadesHidden);
  if(typeof refreshSelVis==='function')refreshSelVis();
  if(typeof applyDimVis==='function')applyDimVis();
}
document.getElementById('mFacades').onclick=()=>{toggleFacades();closeMenu();};

// ---- Teshilish (2D) ochish/yopish ----
// chiqish yo'llari: pastki katta tugma, tepadagi ✕, telefonda "orqaga" tugmasi/imosi, Esc
// 2D chizma ALOHIDA "SAHIFA" ko'rinishida (?d2=<des>), LEKIN sahifa QAYTA YUKLANMAYDI:
// URL history bilan almashadi (pushState). Shu tufayli 3D<->2D o'tishda brend ekrani
// (splash) qayta chiqmaydi va model qaytadan yuklanib qurilmaydi — o'tish bir zumda.
// "Orqaga" tugmasi/imosi va havolani ulashish avvalgidek ishlaydi.
const D2PAGE=()=>document.body.classList.contains('d2page');
let _d2Pushed=false;        // 2D holati tarixga pushState bilan qo'shildimi
function drillPageUrl(des){
  return '/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/m/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE)+'?d2='+encodeURIComponent(des||'');
}
function modelPageUrl(des){
  return '/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/m/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE)+(des?'/'+encodeURIComponent(des):'');
}
function openDrillView(p){
  drillOpen=true;
  _dFitPanel=null;          // har ochilishda chizma to'liq ekranga moslanadi
  document.getElementById('drill').style.display='flex';
  openDrill(p);
}
function enterD2(des){      // 3D -> 2D: sahifa yuklanmaydi, faqat ko'rinish almashadi
  const p=((DATA&&DATA.panels)||[]).find(x=>String(x.des)===String(des));
  if(!p)return false;
  try{history.pushState({d2:String(des)},'',drillPageUrl(des));_d2Pushed=true;}catch(e){}
  document.body.classList.add('d2page');
  selectByDes(String(des));
  openDrillView(p);
  return true;
}
function exitD2(){          // 2D -> 3D: sahna allaqachon qurilgan, shunchaki ko'rsatamiz
  document.body.classList.remove('d2page');
  drillOpen=false;
  document.getElementById('drill').style.display='none';
}
function closeDrill(){
  // DIQQAT: bu shart D2PAGE OSTIDA bo'lishi SHART — closeDrill() select(null) orqali
  // HAR model yuklanishida chaqiriladi.
  if(D2PAGE()){
    if(_d2Pushed){_d2Pushed=false;history.back();return;}   // popstate exitD2 ni chaqiradi
    // Havola bilan TO'G'RIDAN-TO'G'RI ?d2= ga kirilgan — tarixda bizning sahifamiz yo'q.
    // URL ni 3D manzilga almashtiramiz va 3D ni JOYIDA ochamiz (qayta yuklamasdan).
    try{history.replaceState(null,'',modelPageUrl(_drillPanel?_drillPanel.des:''));}catch(e){}
    exitD2();
    return;
  }
  drillOpen=false;
  document.getElementById('drill').style.display='none';
}
// "Orqaga"/"oldinga" (brauzer tugmasi yoki telefondagi imo-ishora) — holat URL'dan tiklanadi
addEventListener('popstate',()=>{
  let des=null; try{des=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('d2');}catch(e){}
  if(des){
    const p=((DATA&&DATA.panels)||[]).find(x=>String(x.des)===String(des));
    if(p){_d2Pushed=true;document.body.classList.add('d2page');selectByDes(String(des));openDrillView(p);return;}
  }
  _d2Pushed=false;
  exitD2();
});
addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&drillOpen)closeDrill();});
document.getElementById('btnDrill').onclick=()=>{if(selected)enterD2(selected.userData.des);};

};

const _ICON_HIDE='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M2 12s4-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-4 7-10 7S2 12 2 12z"/><line x1="3" y1="3" x2="21" y2="21"/></svg>';

// ---- Furnitura / krepyoj ro'yxati (model bo'yicha) ----
function buildFurn(){
  const list=(DATA&&DATA.furniture)||[];
  const mFurn=document.getElementById('mFurn');
  // 3D ko'rsatish tugmasi — modelda furnitura bo'lagi (fitting) bo'lsa ko'rinadi
  const mF3=document.getElementById('mFurn3D');
  const anyFit=((DATA&&DATA.fittings)||[]).length>0;
  if(mF3){mF3.style.display=anyFit?'':'none';
    const l=document.getElementById('mFurn3DLbl');
    if(l)l.textContent=furn3D?t('furnHide'):t('furnShow');}
  if(!list.length){ if(mFurn)mFurn.style.display='none'; return; }
  if(mFurn)mFurn.style.display='';
  const tot=list.reduce((s,f)=>s+(f.count||0),0);
  document.getElementById('furnTot').textContent=tp('furnTot',{n:tot,k:list.length});
  const fl=document.getElementById('furnList');fl.innerHTML='';
  for(const f of list){
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='fr';
    d.innerHTML=`<span class="dot" style="background:${esc(f.color||'#9aa4b0')}"></span>`+
                `<span>${esc(f.kind)}</span><span class="co">×${f.count}</span>`;
    fl.appendChild(d);
  }
}
function openFurn(){buildFurn();document.getElementById('furn').style.display='flex';}
function closeFurn(){document.getElementById('furn').style.display='none';}
document.getElementById('mFurn').onclick=()=>{openFurn();closeMenu();};

// Bazis kabi IERARXIK struktura: panel.group (" / " bilan) bo'yicha daraxt
// query berilsa — faqat kod/nomi mos kelgan detallar (va ularning ota-guruhlari) ko'rsatiladi.
function buildList(query){
  const q=(query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const body=document.getElementById('listBody');body.innerHTML='';
  const src=q?meshes.filter(m=>{
    const p=m.userData;
    return (p.des||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(p.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q);
  }):meshes;
  const root={children:{},parts:[],furn:{}};
  for(const m of src){
    const path=groupPath(m.userData);
    let node=root;
    for(const seg of path){
      if(!node.children[seg])node.children[seg]={children:{},parts:[],furn:{}};
      node=node.children[seg];
    }
    node.parts.push(m);
  }
  // ---- FURNITURA daraxtga (2026-08-24) ----
  // Bazis «Структура модели» panelida panellar bilan BIR QATORDA krepyoj/furnitura ham
  // turadi; bizda esa ro'yxat faqat panellardan qurilardi — yo'naltiruvchi, petlya, vint
  // ko'rinmasdi ("struktura to'liq emas"). Panel `group` (nomlar) + `gid` (id) juftini
  // beradi, furnitura esa faqat `gid` ni — shuning uchun avval gid->nom lug'atini
  // panellardan quramiz (jonli ma'lumotda 95% furnitura shu yo'l bilan o'z guruhini topadi;
  // topilmagani ildizda «Furnitura» tugunida turadi).
  const _g2n={};
  for(const m of meshes){
    const p=m.userData;
    const gs=((p.group||'').split(' / ')).map(x=>x.trim());
    const ids=((p.gid||'').split('/'));
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(gs.length,ids.length);i++){
      if(gs[i])_g2n[ids.slice(0,i+1).join('/')]=gs[i];
    }
  }
  for(const f of ((DATA&&DATA.fittings)||[])){
    if(f.sub)continue;                        // bitta furnituraning ikkinchi bo'lagi
    const kind=(f.kind&&f.kind!=='defaultobject')?f.kind:t('furnOne');
    if(q&&!kind.toLowerCase().includes(q))continue;
    const ids=(f.gid||'').split('/').filter(Boolean);
    const path=[];
    for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
      const nm=_g2n[ids.slice(0,i+1).join('/')];
      if(nm&&path[path.length-1]!==nm)path.push(nm);
    }
    while(path.length>1&&_ASM_ROOTS.indexOf(path[0].toLowerCase())>=0)path.shift();
    let node=root;
    for(const seg of path){
      if(!node.children[seg])node.children[seg]={children:{},parts:[],furn:{}};
      node=node.children[seg];
    }
    node.furn[kind]=(node.furn[kind]||0)+1;
  }
  const fcnt=n=>{let c=0;for(const k in (n.furn||{}))c+=n.furn[k];return c;};
  const cnt=n=>{let c=n.parts.length+fcnt(n);for(const k in n.children)c+=cnt(n.children[k]);return c;};
  const nodeMeshes=n=>{let out=n.parts.slice();for(const k in n.children)out=out.concat(nodeMeshes(n.children[k]));return out;};
  function partRow(m,depth){
    const p=m.userData;const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='it';d.dataset.des=p.des;
    d.style.paddingLeft=(11+depth*13)+'px';
    // O'LCHAMSIZ DETAL: Bazis faylining O'ZIDA konturi bo'sh (0×0) bo'lgan detallar uchraydi
    // (masalan perfect-mebel/ZW18901 dagi 4 ta «Вертикальная»). Ularni yashirmaymiz —
    // konstruktor ko'rib tuzatishi uchun ro'yxatda BELGILAB qo'yamiz.
    const _noSize = !(p.w>0) || !(p.h>0);
    const _sz = _noSize
      ? `<span style="color:#c2760b;font-size:11.5px" title="Bazis faylida bu detalning konturi bo'sh">⚠ o'lchamsiz</span>`
      : `<span style="color:var(--mut);font-size:11.5px">${Math.round(Math.max(p.w,p.h))}×${Math.round(Math.min(p.w,p.h))}×${p.thick}</span>`+utolBadge(p);
    d.innerHTML=`<b>${esc(p.des||'—')}</b>${esc(p.name||'')}<br>${_sz}`;
    d.onclick=()=>{select(m);if(innerWidth<700)document.getElementById('list').style.display='none';};
    return d;
  }
  // Furnitura qatori: 3D'da alohida obyekt emas (mesh ro'yxatida yo'q) -> bosilmaydi,
  // faqat ko'rsatkich. Bir guruhdagi bir xil turdagilar bitta qatorda ×N bo'lib turadi.
  function furnRow(kind,n,depth){
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='it fu';
    d.style.paddingLeft=(11+depth*13)+'px';
    d.style.cursor='default';
    d.innerHTML=`<b>🔩 ${esc(kind)}</b><br><span style="color:var(--mut);font-size:11.5px">${n} ${esc(t('pcs'))}</span>`;
    return d;
  }
  function render(node,depth,box){
    for(const k of Object.keys(node.children)){
      const c=node.children[k];
      const hdr=document.createElement('div');hdr.className='grp';hdr.style.paddingLeft=(4+depth*13)+'px';
      hdr.innerHTML=`<span class="caret">▾</span><b class="gname" style="color:#3a4654;margin:0">${esc(k)}</b><span class="gcnt">${cnt(c)}</span>`;
      const sub=document.createElement('div');
      // KARET (katta maydon) — faqat ochadi/yopadi
      hdr.querySelector('.caret').onclick=(e)=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        const h=sub.style.display==='none';
        sub.style.display=h?'':'none';
        hdr.querySelector('.caret').textContent=h?'▾':'▸';
      };
      // SARLAVHANING O'ZI — blokni (guruh ichidagi HAMMA detalni, ichki guruhlar bilan) TANLAYDI
      hdr.onclick=()=>{
        const set=nodeMeshes(c);
        if(!set.length)return;
        selectMeshSet(set,k);
        document.querySelectorAll('#list .grp').forEach(g=>g.classList.remove('blksel'));
        hdr.classList.add('blksel');
        if(innerWidth<700)document.getElementById('list').style.display='none';
      };
      box.appendChild(hdr);box.appendChild(sub);
      render(c,depth+1,sub);
    }
    for(const m of node.parts)box.appendChild(partRow(m,depth));
    const fu=node.furn||{};
    for(const k of Object.keys(fu).sort((x,y)=>fu[y]-fu[x]))box.appendChild(furnRow(k,fu[k],depth));
  }
  render(root,0,body);
  if(q&&!src.length){
    const none=document.createElement('div');
    none.style.cssText='padding:14px;color:var(--mut);font-size:12.5px;text-align:center';
    none.textContent=t('notFound');
    body.appendChild(none);
  }
}

// ---- Teshilish 2D (chertyoj) ----
// drillFace: qaysi yuza ko'rsatilmoqda (ALL=hammasi, A=old, B=orqa ko'zgu, T=torec)
let drillFace='ALL';
let dV={s:1,ox:0,oy:0,fitS:1};              // 2D transform: masshtab + siljish
let _dB={xmin:0,xmax:1,ymin:0,ymax:1};      // joriy panel chegaralari (mm)
// ekran koordinatalari: B tabда X ko'zgu qilinadi (detal orqa tomondan ko'rinadi)
function dTx(x){return dV.ox+((drillFace==='B')?(_dB.xmin+_dB.xmax-x):x)*dV.s;}
function dTy(y){return dV.oy-y*dV.s;}
function visHoles(p){
  const flt=drillFilter[p.des]||{};
  return (p.holes||[]).filter(h=>flt[h.type]!==false&&(drillFace==='ALL'||h.face===drillFace));
}
function openDrill(p){
  const holes=p.holes||[];
  if(_drillPanel!==p){drillSelHole=null;drillFace='ALL';_drillPanel=p;_dFitPanel=null;}
  document.getElementById('drill').style.display='flex';
  const W2=Math.round(Math.max(p.w,p.h)),H2=Math.round(Math.min(p.w,p.h));
  document.getElementById('dTitle').textContent=`${p.des||''} · ${W2}×${H2}×${p.thick} mm`+(holes.length?` · ${holes.length} ${t('holes')}`:'');
  // --- yuza tablari (A old / B orqa / T torec) ---
  const counts={A:0,B:0,T:0};
  for(const h of holes)counts[h.face]=(counts[h.face]||0)+1;
  if(drillFace!=='ALL'&&!counts[drillFace])drillFace='ALL';
  const tdiv=document.getElementById('dTabs');tdiv.innerHTML='';
  [['ALL',t('dAll'),holes.length],['A',t('dFaceA'),counts.A],['B',t('dFaceB'),counts.B],['T',t('dFaceT'),counts.T]].forEach(([k,lbl,n])=>{
    if(k!=='ALL'&&!n)return;
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.textContent=`${lbl} (${n})`;
    if(drillFace===k)b.className='on';
    b.onclick=()=>{drillFace=k;if(drillSelHole&&drillFace!=='ALL'&&drillSelHole.face!==drillFace)drillSelHole=null;openDrill(p);};
    tdiv.appendChild(b);
  });
  tdiv.style.display=holes.length?'flex':'none';
  const mb=document.getElementById('dMirror');
  mb.textContent=t('dMirror');mb.style.display=(drillFace==='B')?'block':'none';
  // --- tur bo'yicha filtr chiplari ---
  const types={};
  for(const h of holes){if(!types[h.type])types[h.type]={color:h.color,n:0};types[h.type].n++;}
  if(!(p.des in drillFilter))drillFilter[p.des]={};
  const fdiv=document.getElementById('filters');fdiv.innerHTML='';
  Object.keys(types).forEach(t=>{
    if(drillFilter[p.des][t]===undefined)drillFilter[p.des][t]=true;
    const chip=document.createElement('div');chip.className='chip'+(drillFilter[p.des][t]?'':' off');
    chip.innerHTML=`<span class="dot" style="background:${esc(types[t].color)}"></span>${esc(t)} (${types[t].n})`;
    chip.onclick=()=>{drillFilter[p.des][t]=!drillFilter[p.des][t];if(drillSelHole&&drillFilter[p.des][drillSelHole.type]===false)drillSelHole=null;openDrill(p);};
    fdiv.appendChild(chip);
  });
  // panel chet koordinatalari — eng yaqin chetdan offset hisoblash uchun
  let xmn=0,xmx=p.w,ymn=0,ymx=p.h;
  if(p.poly){const xs=p.poly.map(a=>a[0]),ys=p.poly.map(a=>a[1]);xmn=Math.min(...xs);xmx=Math.max(...xs);ymn=Math.min(...ys);ymx=Math.max(...ys);}
  // koordinata o'qi belgisi — X o'ngga, Y pastga
  const AXICON='<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="13" height="13" style="vertical-align:-2px;margin-right:3px"><path d="M3 3h9" stroke="#ff5c5c" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M13 3L10.4 1.4V4.6Z" fill="#ff5c5c"/><path d="M3 3v9" stroke="#3fd17a" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M3 13L1.4 10.4H4.6Z" fill="#3fd17a"/><circle cx="3" cy="3" r="1.3" fill="#1b2530"/></svg>';
  const hl=document.getElementById('hlist');hl.innerHTML='';
  const vis=visHoles(p);
  if(vis.length&&!drillSelHole){
    const hint=document.createElement('div');
    hint.style.cssText='padding:6px 12px;color:var(--mut);font-size:11px';
    hint.textContent=t('dTapHint');hl.appendChild(hint);
  }
  vis.forEach((h,i)=>{
    const fb=h.face==='T'
      ?`<span class="fb fT">${esc(t('faceT'))}${h.z!=null?' · h'+esc(h.z):''}</span>`
      :(h.face==='B'?`<span class="fb fB">B</span>`:`<span class="fb">A</span>`);
    const thru=h.thru?` <span class="thru">${esc(t('thru'))}</span>`:'';
    const ox=Math.round(Math.min(h.x-xmn,xmx-h.x)), oy=Math.round(Math.min(h.y-ymn,ymx-h.y));
    const r=document.createElement('div');r.className='hr'+(h===drillSelHole?' sel':'');
    r.innerHTML=`<span class="num">${i+1}</span><span class="dot" style="background:${esc(h.color)}"></span>${esc(h.type)} Ø${esc(h.d)}×${esc(h.depth)}${thru} ${fb}<span class="co">${AXICON}X${ox} Y${oy}</span>`;
    r.onclick=()=>{drillSelHole=(drillSelHole===h)?null:h;openDrill(p);};   // tanlash -> chetlargacha razmer
    hl.appendChild(r);
  });
  if(!holes.length)hl.innerHTML='<div style="padding:14px;color:var(--mut);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(t('noHoles'))+'</div>';
  drawHoles2D(p);
  const selRow=hl.querySelector('.hr.sel');if(selRow)selRow.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});
}
let _dFitPanel=null;   // fit qaysi panel uchun bajarilgan
function dFit(p,cssW,cssH){
  let xmin=0,xmax=p.w||1,ymin=0,ymax=p.h||1;
  if(p.poly){const xs=p.poly.map(a=>a[0]),ys=p.poly.map(a=>a[1]);xmin=Math.min(...xs);xmax=Math.max(...xs);ymin=Math.min(...ys);ymax=Math.max(...ys);}
  _dB={xmin,xmax,ymin,ymax};
  const pw=Math.max(xmax-xmin,1),ph=Math.max(ymax-ymin,1);
  const padL=44,padT=34,padR=16,padB=26;   // chap/tepa — gabarit razmerlar uchun joy
  const s=Math.min((cssW-padL-padR)/pw,(cssH-padT-padB)/ph);
  dV.s=s;dV.fitS=s;
  dV.ox=padL+(cssW-padL-padR-pw*s)/2-xmin*s;
  dV.oy=padT+(cssH-padT-padB-ph*s)/2+ymax*s;
}
function drawHoles2D(p){
  const cv=document.getElementById('cv2d'),wrap=document.getElementById('cvWrap');
  const cssH=Math.max(240,Math.min(Math.round(innerHeight*0.44),500));
  wrap.style.height=cssH+'px';
  const cssW=wrap.clientWidth||340;
  const dpr=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2.5);
  cv.width=Math.round(cssW*dpr);cv.height=Math.round(cssH*dpr);
  const g=cv.getContext('2d');g.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);g.clearRect(0,0,cssW,cssH);
  if(_dFitPanel!==p){dFit(p,cssW,cssH);_dFitPanel=p;}
  const {xmin,xmax,ymin,ymax}=_dB;
  const pw=xmax-xmin,ph=ymax-ymin,mir=(drillFace==='B');
  const tx=dTx,ty=dTy,poly=p.poly;
  // ---- kontur + teshib o'tuvchi o'yiqlar (evenodd) ----
  const trace=pts=>{pts.forEach((a,i)=>{const X=tx(a[0]),Y=ty(a[1]);i?g.lineTo(X,Y):g.moveTo(X,Y);});g.closePath();};
  g.beginPath();
  if(poly)trace(poly);
  else{g.moveTo(tx(xmin),ty(ymin));g.lineTo(tx(xmax),ty(ymin));g.lineTo(tx(xmax),ty(ymax));g.lineTo(tx(xmin),ty(ymax));g.closePath();}
  for(const c of (p.cuts||[])){
    if(c.t==='circle'){g.moveTo(tx(c.x)+c.r*dV.s,ty(c.y));g.arc(tx(c.x),ty(c.y),c.r*dV.s,0,7);}
    else if(c.t==='poly'&&c.pts)trace(c.pts);
  }
  g.fillStyle='#eef1f5';g.fill('evenodd');
  g.strokeStyle='#9aa4b2';g.lineWidth=1.5;g.stroke();
  // ---- cho'ntaklar (qisman chuqurlik) — shu yuzada ko'rinadi ----
  for(const pk of (p.pockets||[])){
    if(drillFace==='T')continue;
    if(drillFace!=='ALL'&&pk.face!==drillFace)continue;
    g.beginPath();
    let cx0,cy0;
    if(pk.t==='circle'){g.arc(tx(pk.x),ty(pk.y),pk.r*dV.s,0,7);cx0=tx(pk.x);cy0=ty(pk.y);}
    else if(pk.t==='poly'&&pk.pts){trace(pk.pts);cx0=tx(pk.pts.reduce((s,a)=>s+a[0],0)/pk.pts.length);cy0=ty(pk.pts.reduce((s,a)=>s+a[1],0)/pk.pts.length);}
    else continue;
    g.fillStyle=(pk.face==='B')?'#7a4de81a':'#5a86c422';g.fill();
    g.setLineDash([4,3]);g.strokeStyle=(pk.face==='B')?'#7a4de8':'#5a86c4';g.lineWidth=1.2;g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);
    g.fillStyle='#3a4654';g.font='10px system-ui';g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';
    g.fillText('−'+pk.depth,cx0,cy0);g.textAlign='start';g.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  }
  // ---- 45° ZAPIL: qirra qalinlik bo'ylab qiya kesiladi (detallar "на ус" qo'shiladi).
  // Chizmada — qirra bo'ylab shtrixli polosa (shu yuzada olinadigan en) + burchak yozuvi.
  const _bvs=(p.bevels||[]).filter(b=>b&&poly&&b.ei>=0&&b.ei<poly.length);
  if(_bvs.length){
    let ar=0;for(let i=0;i<poly.length;i++){const a=poly[i],b2=poly[(i+1)%poly.length];ar+=a[0]*b2[1]-b2[0]*a[1];}
    const sgn=(ar>0)?1:-1;
    for(const b of _bvs){
      const e1=poly[b.ei],e2=poly[(b.ei+1)%poly.length];
      let dx=e2[0]-e1[0],dy=e2[1]-e1[1];const L=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=L;dy/=L;
      // qisman zapil (t0/t1): polosa faqat shu oraliqda
      const s0=(b.t0==null)?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(L,+b.t0)),s1=(b.t1==null)?L:Math.max(0,Math.min(L,+b.t1));
      const a1=[e1[0]+dx*s0,e1[1]+dy*s0],a2=[e1[0]+dx*s1,e1[1]+dy*s1];
      const nx=-dy*sgn,ny=dx*sgn;   // kontur ichiga
      const wf=(drillFace==='B')?(+b.b||0):(drillFace==='A')?(+b.a||0):Math.max(+b.a||0,+b.b||0);
      const w=Math.max(wf,1.5);
      g.beginPath();
      [[a1[0],a1[1]],[a2[0],a2[1]],[a2[0]+nx*w,a2[1]+ny*w],[a1[0]+nx*w,a1[1]+ny*w]]
        .forEach((q,i)=>{const X=tx(q[0]),Y=ty(q[1]);i?g.lineTo(X,Y):g.moveTo(X,Y);});
      g.closePath();
      g.fillStyle='#ff8a2a2e';g.fill();
      g.setLineDash([5,3]);g.strokeStyle='#d9791c';g.lineWidth=1.3;g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);
      // yozuv — qirra bo'ylab, kromka nomidan ICHKARIROQDA (ustma-ust tushmasin)
      const X1=tx(a1[0]),Y1=ty(a1[1]),X2=tx(a2[0]),Y2=ty(a2[1]);
      const elen=Math.hypot(X2-X1,Y2-Y1);
      const txt=(b.ang?Math.round(b.ang):45)+'°'+((b.t0!=null||b.t1!=null)?(' · '+Math.round(s1-s0)+' mm'):'');
      g.font='bold 10.5px system-ui';
      if(elen>g.measureText(txt).width+16){
        let sx=tx(a1[0]+nx*w*3)-X1,sy=ty(a1[1]+ny*w*3)-Y1;   // ichkariga yo'nalish (ekranda)
        const sl=Math.hypot(sx,sy)||1;sx/=sl;sy/=sl;
        let ang=Math.atan2(Y2-Y1,X2-X1);
        if(ang>Math.PI/2)ang-=Math.PI;else if(ang<-Math.PI/2)ang+=Math.PI;
        g.save();g.translate((X1+X2)/2+sx*24,(Y1+Y2)/2+sy*24);g.rotate(ang);
        g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';
        g.strokeStyle='#ffffffd9';g.lineWidth=3;g.strokeText(txt,0,0);
        g.fillStyle='#b45f0e';g.fillText(txt,0,0);
        g.restore();g.textAlign='start';g.textBaseline='alphabetic';
      }
    }
  }
  // ---- teshiklar ----
  const vis=visHoles(p);
  const showNum=vis.length<=180;
  vis.forEach((h,i)=>{
    const X=tx(h.x),Y=ty(h.y);
    if(X<-40||X>cssW+40||Y<-40||Y>cssH+40)return;
    const rad=Math.max(2.4,(h.d||5)/2*dV.s);
    if(h.face==='T'){
      // torec: kirish nuqtasidan detal ichiga qarab yo'nalish chizig'i (chuqurlikcha)
      // ang faqat YANGI chiqarilgan JSONlarda bor — eskisida yo'nalishsiz belgi chizamiz
      if(h.ang!=null){
        let a=h.ang*Math.PI/180,dx=Math.cos(a),dy=Math.sin(a);
        if(mir)dx=-dx;
        const L=Math.max(8,Math.min((h.depth||10)*dV.s,42));
        g.strokeStyle=h.color;g.lineWidth=Math.max(2.5,rad*1.2);g.lineCap='round';
        g.beginPath();g.moveTo(X,Y);g.lineTo(X+dx*L,Y-dy*L);g.stroke();g.lineCap='butt';
        g.beginPath();g.arc(X,Y,Math.max(2.6,rad*0.8),0,7);g.fillStyle='#11161c';g.fill();
      }else{
        g.beginPath();g.arc(X,Y,Math.max(3,rad),0,7);g.fillStyle=h.color;g.fill();
        g.strokeStyle='#11161c';g.lineWidth=1.5;g.stroke();
      }
    }else{
      g.beginPath();g.arc(X,Y,rad,0,7);
      if(h.face==='B'&&drillFace==='ALL'){
        // ALL rejimда orqa yuza teshigi — shtrix halqa (old tomondan ko'rinmaydi)
        g.fillStyle=h.color+'44';g.fill();
        g.setLineDash([3,2]);g.strokeStyle=h.color;g.lineWidth=1.6;g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);
      }else{
        g.fillStyle=h.color;g.fill();
      }
      if(h.thru){   // teshib o'tuvchi — markaziy chiziqlar (CAD belgisi)
        g.strokeStyle='#11161c';g.lineWidth=1;
        g.beginPath();g.moveTo(X-rad-3,Y);g.lineTo(X+rad+3,Y);g.moveTo(X,Y-rad-3);g.lineTo(X,Y+rad+3);g.stroke();
      }
    }
    if(showNum){
      g.fillStyle='#33404e';g.font='9.5px system-ui';
      g.fillText(i+1,X+rad+2,Y-rad-1);
    }
  });
  // ---- KROMKALAR: tomon bo'ylab rangli chiziq + nomi (butt.seg — Elem indeksdan) ----
  const _bts=(p.butts||[]).filter(b=>b.seg||b.circ);
  if(_bts.length){
    for(const b of _bts){b._s=String(b.sign||b.mat||'').trim()||'kromka';}   // yozuv uchun nom
    // Sirojiddin brendida: HAR TOMON o'z material rangi. Boshqa brendlarда: eski palitra (sign bo'yicha).
    let _scol=null;
    if(!KROMKA_NEW){
      const PAL=['#d84a3f','#2f6fc4','#2f9e63','#a04ec9','#c07f2e','#2fa3ad'];
      _scol={};let _ci=0;
      for(const b of _bts){if(!(b._s in _scol))_scol[b._s]=PAL[_ci++%PAL.length];}
    }
    const _bcol=b=>KROMKA_NEW?buttColor2D(p,b):_scol[b._s];
    const ccx=tx((xmin+xmax)/2),ccy=ty((ymin+ymax)/2);
    g.lineCap='round';
    for(const b of _bts){
      const col=_bcol(b);   // Sirojiddin: real material rangi | boshqalar: palitra
      if(b.circ){   // dumaloq o'yiq chetiga kromka — halqa
        g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=3;
        g.beginPath();g.arc(tx(b.circ[0]),ty(b.circ[1]),b.circ[2]*dV.s+3,0,7);g.stroke();
        continue;
      }
      const x1=tx(b.seg[0][0]),y1=ty(b.seg[0][1]),x2=tx(b.seg[1][0]),y2=ty(b.seg[1][1]);
      let nx=y1-y2,ny=x2-x1;const nl=Math.hypot(nx,ny)||1;nx/=nl;ny/=nl;
      let mx=(x1+x2)/2,my=(y1+y2)/2;
      if((mx-ccx)*nx+(my-ccy)*ny<0){nx=-nx;ny=-ny;}   // normal tashqariga qarasin
      g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=3;
      // YOY qirra (oval detal): vatar emas, yoyning O'ZI chiziladi — har nuqtada
      // konturdan tashqariga siljitib (avval to'g'ri chiziq bo'lib ko'rinardi).
      const _ap=b.pts;
      if(_ap&&_ap.length>1){
        g.beginPath();
        for(let i=0;i<_ap.length;i++){
          const X=tx(_ap[i][0]),Y=ty(_ap[i][1]);
          const px2=tx(_ap[Math.max(0,i-1)][0]),py2=ty(_ap[Math.max(0,i-1)][1]);
          const nx2=tx(_ap[Math.min(_ap.length-1,i+1)][0]),ny2=ty(_ap[Math.min(_ap.length-1,i+1)][1]);
          let ux=ny2-py2,uy=-(nx2-px2);const ul=Math.hypot(ux,uy)||1;ux/=ul;uy/=ul;
          if((X-ccx)*ux+(Y-ccy)*uy<0){ux=-ux;uy=-uy;}
          if(i===0)g.moveTo(X+ux*2.5,Y+uy*2.5);else g.lineTo(X+ux*2.5,Y+uy*2.5);
        }
        g.stroke();
        const _m=_ap[Math.floor(_ap.length/2)];   // yozuv yoy O'RTASIGA
        mx=tx(_m[0]);my=ty(_m[1]);
      }else{
        g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x1+nx*2.5,y1+ny*2.5);g.lineTo(x2+nx*2.5,y2+ny*2.5);g.stroke();
      }
      // nomi — tomon bo'ylab, kontur ICHIDA (tashqarida gabarit razmerlar bor)
      const elen=Math.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1);
      g.font='bold 10px system-ui';
      let txt=b._s,tw=g.measureText(txt).width;
      if(tw>elen-8){txt=txt.replace(/\s*мм\.?$|\s*mm$/i,'');tw=g.measureText(txt).width;}
      if(tw>elen-8&&b.thick){txt=String(b.thick);tw=g.measureText(txt).width;}
      if(tw>elen-8)continue;    // sig'masa yozmaymiz — zoom qilinganda chiqadi
      let ang=Math.atan2(y2-y1,x2-x1);
      if(ang>Math.PI/2)ang-=Math.PI;else if(ang<-Math.PI/2)ang+=Math.PI;   // teskari yozilmasin
      g.save();g.translate(mx-nx*9,my-ny*9);g.rotate(ang);
      g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';
      g.strokeStyle='#ffffffd9';g.lineWidth=3;g.strokeText(txt,0,0);
      g.fillStyle=col;g.fillText(txt,0,0);
      g.restore();
    }
    g.lineCap='butt';g.textAlign='start';g.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  }
  // ---- gabarit razmerlar (kontur ekran bboxiga bog'lab) ----
  const bx1=Math.min(tx(xmin),tx(xmax)),bx2=Math.max(tx(xmin),tx(xmax));
  const by1=ty(ymax),by2=ty(ymin);
  const acc='#ff8a2a';
  g.strokeStyle=acc;g.fillStyle=acc;g.lineWidth=1.2;g.font='bold 12px system-ui';g.textBaseline='alphabetic';
  const yT=Math.max(14,by1-14);
  if(yT<=by1){   // zoom qilinganda kontur tepasi ekrandan chiqib ketsa — chizilmaydi
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(bx1,yT);g.lineTo(bx2,yT);
    g.moveTo(bx1,yT-4);g.lineTo(bx1,yT+4);g.moveTo(bx2,yT-4);g.lineTo(bx2,yT+4);g.stroke();
    g.textAlign='center';g.fillText(Math.round(pw)+' mm',(bx1+bx2)/2,yT-4);
  }
  const xL=Math.max(14,bx1-18);
  if(xL<=bx1){
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(xL,by1);g.lineTo(xL,by2);
    g.moveTo(xL-4,by1);g.lineTo(xL+4,by1);g.moveTo(xL-4,by2);g.lineTo(xL+4,by2);g.stroke();
    g.save();g.translate(xL-5,(by1+by2)/2);g.rotate(-Math.PI/2);g.textAlign='center';g.fillText(Math.round(ph)+' mm',0,0);g.restore();
  }
  g.textAlign='start';
  // ---- masshtab paneli (pastki chap) ----
  const nice=[5,10,20,25,50,100,200,250,500,1000].find(v=>v*dV.s>=54)||2000;
  const sx0=10,sy0=cssH-10,sl=nice*dV.s;
  g.strokeStyle='#3a4654';g.fillStyle='#3a4654';g.lineWidth=1.4;
  g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sx0,sy0);g.lineTo(sx0+sl,sy0);g.moveTo(sx0,sy0-4);g.lineTo(sx0,sy0);g.moveTo(sx0+sl,sy0-4);g.lineTo(sx0+sl,sy0);g.stroke();
  g.font='10px system-ui';g.fillText(nice+' mm',sx0+4,sy0-4);
  // ---- TANLANGAN teshik: chetlargacha offset + belgi ----
  if(drillSelHole&&vis.indexOf(drillSelHole)>=0){
    const h=drillSelHole,hx=tx(h.x),hy=ty(h.y);
    const cX='#ff5c5c',cY='#3fd17a';   // X = qizil, Y = yashil
    g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy,Math.max(8,(h.d||5)/2*dV.s+4),0,7);
    g.strokeStyle='#e08600';g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();
    // Ø×chuqurlik yorlig'i teshik ustida
    g.font='bold 11px system-ui';g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='bottom';
    const info=`Ø${h.d}×${h.depth}${h.thru?' · '+t('thru'):''}${h.face==='T'&&h.z!=null?' · h'+h.z:''}`;
    const iw=g.measureText(info).width+8,iy=hy-Math.max(16,(h.d||5)/2*dV.s+12);
    g.fillStyle='#11161cd9';
    g.fillRect(hx-iw/2,iy-15,iw,14);
    g.fillStyle='#fff';g.fillText(info,hx,iy-2);
    g.lineWidth=1.4;g.font='bold 12px system-ui';
    // eng yaqin ENI cheti (gorizontal offset) — QIZIL (X)
    const dl=h.x-xmin,dr=xmax-h.x,exX=(dl<=dr)?xmin:xmax,distW=Math.min(dl,dr),ex=tx(exX);
    g.strokeStyle=cX;g.fillStyle=cX;
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(ex,hy);g.lineTo(hx,hy);
    g.moveTo(ex,hy-4);g.lineTo(ex,hy+4);g.moveTo(hx,hy-4);g.lineTo(hx,hy+4);g.stroke();
    g.fillText(Math.round(distW)+'',(ex+hx)/2,hy-5);
    // eng yaqin BO'YI cheti (vertikal offset) — YASHIL (Y)
    const db=h.y-ymin,dt=ymax-h.y,eyY=(db<=dt)?ymin:ymax,distH=Math.min(db,dt),ey=ty(eyY);
    g.strokeStyle=cY;g.fillStyle=cY;
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx,ey);g.lineTo(hx,hy);
    g.moveTo(hx-4,ey);g.lineTo(hx+4,ey);g.moveTo(hx-4,hy);g.lineTo(hx+4,hy);g.stroke();
    g.save();g.translate(hx-6,(ey+hy)/2);g.rotate(-Math.PI/2);g.fillText(Math.round(distH)+'',0,0);g.restore();
    // ---- nol burchagi: X/Y o'q, paneldan tashqarida; TEPA burchak -> 180° ----
    let xd=Math.sign(hx-ex)||1,yd=Math.sign(hy-ey)||-1;
    if(yd>0){xd=-xd;yd=-yd;}
    const G=16,L=12,oX=ex-xd*G,oY=ey-yd*G;
    g.lineWidth=1.5;g.font='bold 10px system-ui';g.textBaseline='middle';g.textAlign='center';
    g.strokeStyle=cX;g.fillStyle=cX;
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(oX,oY);g.lineTo(oX+L*xd,oY);g.stroke();
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(oX+L*xd,oY);g.lineTo(oX+(L-5)*xd,oY-2.6);g.lineTo(oX+(L-5)*xd,oY+2.6);g.closePath();g.fill();
    g.fillText('X',oX+(L+6)*xd,oY);
    g.strokeStyle=cY;g.fillStyle=cY;
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(oX,oY);g.lineTo(oX,oY+L*yd);g.stroke();
    g.beginPath();g.moveTo(oX,oY+L*yd);g.lineTo(oX-2.6,oY+(L-5)*yd);g.lineTo(oX+2.6,oY+(L-5)*yd);g.closePath();g.fill();
    g.fillText('Y',oX,oY+(L+6)*yd);
    g.fillStyle='#11161c';g.beginPath();g.arc(oX,oY,2.2,0,7);g.fill();
    g.textBaseline='alphabetic';g.textAlign='start';
  }
}
// ---- 2D zoom/pan/tanlash (pinch + drag + wheel + tap) ----
function dZoomAt(cx,cy,ns){
  ns=Math.max(dV.fitS*0.4,Math.min(dV.fitS*60,ns));
  const wx=(cx-dV.ox)/dV.s,wy=(dV.oy-cy)/dV.s;
  dV.s=ns;dV.ox=cx-wx*ns;dV.oy=cy+wy*ns;
}
function dRedraw(){if(drillOpen&&_drillPanel)drawHoles2D(_drillPanel);}
(function(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('cv2d');
  const pos=e=>{const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();return[e.clientX-r.left,e.clientY-r.top];};
  const pts=new Map();
  let moved=0,px=0,py=0,pinch0=0,s0=1,pinched=false;
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    pts.set(e.pointerId,pos(e));
    if(pts.size===1){moved=0;pinched=false;[px,py]=pos(e);}
    else if(pts.size===2){pinched=true;const a=[...pts.values()];pinch0=Math.hypot(a[0][0]-a[1][0],a[0][1]-a[1][1]);s0=dV.s;}
    e.preventDefault();
  });
  cv.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!pts.has(e.pointerId))return;
    pts.set(e.pointerId,pos(e));
    if(pts.size===1){
      const[qx,qy]=pos(e),dx=qx-px,dy=qy-py;
      moved+=Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy);
      if(moved>3){dV.ox+=dx;dV.oy+=dy;px=qx;py=qy;dRedraw();}
    }else if(pts.size===2&&pinch0>4){
      const a=[...pts.values()];
      const d=Math.hypot(a[0][0]-a[1][0],a[0][1]-a[1][1]);
      dZoomAt((a[0][0]+a[1][0])/2,(a[0][1]+a[1][1])/2,s0*d/pinch0);
      dRedraw();
    }
  });
  const reseed=()=>{   // pinchdan bitta barmoq qolganda pan langarini qayta o'rnatamiz (sakramasin)
    if(pts.size===1){const a=[...pts.values()][0];px=a[0];py=a[1];moved=99;}
  };
  const end=e=>{
    if(pts.has(e.pointerId)&&pts.size===1&&!pinched&&moved<6&&e.button===0&&_drillPanel){
      // TAP — eng yaqin teshikni tanlash (20px radius ichida)
      const[qx,qy]=pos(e);let best=null,bd=20;
      for(const h of visHoles(_drillPanel)){
        const dd=Math.hypot(dTx(h.x)-qx,dTy(h.y)-qy);
        if(dd<bd){bd=dd;best=h;}
      }
      drillSelHole=(best&&best!==drillSelHole)?best:null;
      openDrill(_drillPanel);
    }
    pts.delete(e.pointerId);pinch0=0;reseed();
  };
  cv.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
  cv.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{pts.delete(e.pointerId);pinch0=0;reseed();});
  cv.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
  cv.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{
    e.preventDefault();
    const[qx,qy]=pos(e);
    dZoomAt(qx,qy,dV.s*(e.deltaY<0?1.15:1/1.15));
    dRedraw();
  },{passive:false});
  document.getElementById('dzIn').onclick=()=>{dZoomAt((cv.clientWidth||300)/2,(cv.clientHeight||240)/2,dV.s*1.4);dRedraw();};
  document.getElementById('dzOut').onclick=()=>{dZoomAt((cv.clientWidth||300)/2,(cv.clientHeight||240)/2,dV.s/1.4);dRedraw();};
  document.getElementById('dzFit').onclick=()=>{_dFitPanel=null;dRedraw();};
  addEventListener('resize',()=>{_dFitPanel=null;if(drillOpen)dRedraw();});
})();

// ---- Skanerlash: detalni almashtirish ----
function gotoCode(text){
  text=(text||'').trim();if(!text)return;
  if(navigator.vibrate)navigator.vibrate(50);
  // URL bo'lsa
  let art=ARTICLE, des=null;
  let m=text.match(/\/p\/([^/]+)\/([^/?#]+)/);
  if(m){art=decodeURIComponent(m[1]);des=decodeURIComponent(m[2]);}
  else if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)){
    // BIZNING QR'lar har xil ko'rinishda bo'lishi mumkin: eski chop etilganlarida
    // `http://65.21.3.105/...` yoki `www.` bilan, yangilarida `https://detalqr.uz/...`.
    // Ular uchun `origin` TENG BO'LMAYDI va avval "Tashqi havola bloklandi" chiqib,
    // usta o'z stikerini skanerlay olmasdi. Endi O'Z hostlarimiz tanib olinadi va
    // manzil joriy origin'ga ko'chiriladi (http -> https, IP -> domen).
    // Begona manzil esa avvalgidek BLOKLANADI (zararli QR sahifani olib ketmasin).
    try{
      const u=new URL(text), h=u.hostname, tail=u.pathname+u.search+u.hash;
      const isIP=/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$/.test(h);          // eski chop etilgan IP-QR
      const ours=/(^|\.)detalqr\.uz$/i.test(h);               // o'z domenimiz yoki subdomeni
      if(isIP || h===location.hostname) location.href = location.origin + tail;
      else if(ours)                     location.href = 'https://' + h + tail;
      else                              toast(t('extBlocked')+': '+h, true);
    }catch(e){ toast(t('extBlocked'),true); }
    return;
  }
  else{
    // ARTIKUL_OBOZNACHENIYA — joriy artikulga mos kelsa, faqat detalni almashtiramiz
    if(text.startsWith(ARTICLE+'_')){des=text.slice(ARTICLE.length+1);art=ARTICLE;}
    else{ _scanResolve(text); return; }   // boshqa artikul — modellar ro'yxatiga solishtiramiz
  }
  _scanGo(art,des);
}
function _scanGo(art,des){
  if(art!==ARTICLE){
    // boshqa model — qayta yuklaymiz
    loadModel(art,des);
  }else{
    const ok=selectByDes(des);
    reportScan(ARTICLE,des,ok);
    if(ok){ toast('✓ '+des); flyToMesh(selected); }
    else toast('✗ '+des+' '+t('notFound'),true);
  }
}
// Artikulning O'ZIDA '_' bo'lishi mumkin (masalan EV_48): «EV_48_06_02» ni birinchi
// '_' dan bo'lish EV / 48_06_02 deb noto'g'ri ajratar va «EV modeli topilmadi» chiqarar
// edi (2026-08-28, coda brendi — boshqa model birkasini skanerlash ishlamasdi).
// Endi brend modellari ro'yxatidan ENG UZUN mos artikul olinadi; ro'yxat bir marta
// yuklanib keshda turadi. Ro'yxat kelmasa — eski (birinchi '_') usulga qaytamiz.
let _artsCache=null;
async function _scanResolve(text){
  if(_artsCache===null){
    try{const r=await fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/arts');
        _artsCache=r.ok?(((await r.json())||{}).arts||[]):[];}
    catch(e){_artsCache=[];}
  }
  let art=null,des=null;
  for(const a of _artsCache){
    if(!a||(art&&a.length<=art.length))continue;
    if(text===a){art=a;des='';}
    else if(text.startsWith(a+'_')){art=a;des=text.slice(a.length+1);}
  }
  if(!art){                     // ro'yxatda yo'q — eski usul (birinchi '_' dan)
    const i=text.indexOf('_');
    if(i>0){art=text.slice(0,i);des=text.slice(i+1);}
    else{art=ARTICLE;des=text;}
  }
  _scanGo(art,des);
}
function reportScan(article,des,found){
  try{fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/scan',{method:'POST',
    headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
    body:JSON.stringify({article,des,found:!!found})});}catch(e){}
}

// HID tashqi skaner (doim tinglaydi)
// Telefon/planshet (touch) — avto-fokus YO'Q: yashirin inputga fokus klaviaturani
// ochib yuboradi. Tashqi skaner asosan kompyuterda; telefonda kamera ishlatiladi.
// "Mobil" = asosiy ko'rsatkich barmoq (telefon/planshet). Sensorli noutbuk
// (sichqoncha/trackpad bor) mobil emas — unda tashqi skaner inputi ishlashi shart.
const TOUCH = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches && !matchMedia('(any-pointer: fine)').matches;
// TASHQI SKANER (USB/Bluetooth) — MENYUDAGI TUGMA bilan yoqiladi/o'chiriladi.
// MUHIM: skanerni kutish uchun HECH QANDAY matn maydoni FOKUSLANMAYDI. Ilgari yashirin
// <input id=hid> har 0.9 s da va HAR POINTERDOWN'da (ya'ni detal tanlaganda ham)
// fokuslanardi; S-Pen/stylusli Android (any-pointer: fine) qurilmalarida bu ekran
// klaviaturasini ochib yuborardi. Endi kodni fokusdan MUSTAQIL global ushlovchi oladi —
// shu sababli Android'da ham, iPhone'da ham klaviatura umuman chiqmaydi.
var scanOn = (localStorage.getItem('dqr_scan') || '1') === '1';   // standart: YOQILGAN (hamma qurilmada)
function focusHid(){ /* ataylab bo'sh — eski chaqiruvlar uchun qoldirildi (fokus olinmaydi) */ }
function updateScanUi(){
  if(scanOn===undefined)return;            // hali e'lon qilinmagan (til birinchi qo'llanganda)
  const l=document.getElementById('mScanLbl'); if(l)l.textContent=t(scanOn?'scanOnLbl':'scanOffLbl');
  const b=document.getElementById('mScan'); if(b)b.classList.toggle('on',scanOn);
  const h=document.getElementById('hint'); if(h)h.textContent=t(scanOn?'hint':'hintOff');
}
function toggleScan(){
  scanOn=!scanOn;
  try{localStorage.setItem('dqr_scan',scanOn?'1':'0');}catch(e){}
  updateScanUi();
  toast(t(scanOn?'scanOnMsg':'scanOffMsg'));
}
updateScanUi();

// Skaner klaviatura kabi TEZ yozib Enter bosadi — shu ketma-ketlikni kod deb qabul qilamiz.
(function(){
  let buf='',last=0;
  addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(!scanOn)return;                       // skaner o'chirilgan — tugmalar e'tiborsiz
    const a=document.activeElement;
    // haqiqiy maydon (qidiruv/qo'lda kiritish/report) fokusda bo'lsa — o'sha o'zi hal qiladi
    if(a&&(a.tagName==='INPUT'||a.tagName==='TEXTAREA'))return;
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-last>300)buf='';        // uzoq tanaffus -> yangi ketma-ketlik (tasodifiy bosish emas)
    last=now;
    if(e.key==='Enter'){const v=buf.trim();buf='';if(v){e.preventDefault();gotoCode(v);}return;}
    if(e.key&&e.key.length===1)buf+=e.key;
  });
})();

// ---- Kamera ----
const secure=location.protocol==='https:'||location.hostname==='localhost'||location.hostname==='127.0.0.1';
let scanner=null,camOn=false;
document.getElementById('btnCam').onclick=()=>{
  if(!secure){toast(t('camHttps'),true);return;}
  openCam();
};
document.getElementById('camclose').onclick=()=>closeCam();
function openCam(){
  document.getElementById('camwrap').style.display='flex';
  const start=()=>{
    scanner=new Html5Qrcode('reader');camOn=true;
    const onScan=txt=>{closeCam();gotoCode(txt);};   // QR o'qilgach kamera DARROV o'chadi
    Html5Qrcode.getCameras().then(cams=>{
      let id=(cams&&cams.length)?cams[cams.length-1].id:null;
      const back=cams&&cams.find(c=>/back|rear|environment|задн/i.test(c.label));if(back)id=back.id;
      const conf={fps:10,qrbox:{width:230,height:230}};
      (id?scanner.start(id,conf,onScan,()=>{}):scanner.start({facingMode:'environment'},conf,onScan,()=>{})).catch(camErr);
    }).catch(()=>scanner.start({facingMode:'environment'},{fps:10,qrbox:{width:230,height:230}},onScan,()=>{}).catch(camErr));
  };
  if(typeof Html5Qrcode!=='undefined')start();
  else loadScript(['https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/html5-qrcode/2.3.8/html5-qrcode.min.js','https://unpkg.com/html5-qrcode@2.3.8/html5-qrcode.min.js'],start,()=>{document.getElementById('camst').textContent='Skaner kutubxonasi yuklanmadi.';});
}
function closeCam(){
  const fin=()=>{document.getElementById('camwrap').style.display='none';camOn=false;focusHid();};
  if(scanner&&camOn){scanner.stop().then(()=>{scanner.clear();fin();}).catch(fin);}else fin();
}
function camErr(e){
  const msg=(e&&e.toString())||'';
  document.getElementById('camst').textContent=/NotAllowed|Permission|denied/i.test(msg)?'Kameraga ruxsat berilmadi.':/NotFound/i.test(msg)?'Kamera topilmadi.':'Kamera ochilmadi.';
}

// ===== BAZIS CHIZMASI (.ldw → SVG) =====
// Chizmalar modeldan MUSTAQIL: bot orqali `.bln`/`.ldw` yuklanadi va serverda `drawings`
// jadvalida turadi. Model ochilganda qaysi detalda chizma borligi bir marta so'raladi —
// tugma faqat o'shalarda chiqadi. Xom `.ldw` berilmaydi, server SVG qilib qaytaradi.
let DRAWINGS=new Set();
// ---- 📎 modelga biriktirilgan fayllar (bot /fayl orqali keladi) ----
// (MFILES e'loni applyLang'dan oldin — u 3230-qatorda darhol chaqiriladi, TDZ bo'lmasin)
function loadModelFiles(article){
  MFILES=[];
  fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/files/'+encodeURIComponent(article))
    .then(r=>r.ok?r.json():null).then(d=>{
      MFILES=(d&&d.files)||[];
      const b=document.getElementById('mFiles');
      if(b)b.style.display=MFILES.length?'':'none';
      const l=document.getElementById('mFilesLbl');
      if(l)l.textContent=t('filesBtn')+(MFILES.length?' ('+MFILES.length+')':'');
    }).catch(()=>{});                 // fayl bo'lmasa tugma shunchaki chiqmaydi
}
function fmtFSize(n){
  if(n>1048576)return (n/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MB';
  if(n>1024)return Math.round(n/1024)+' KB';
  return (n||0)+' B';
}
function fileIcon(name){
  const e=(String(name).split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
  if(e==='pdf')return '📕';
  if(['png','jpg','jpeg','webp','heic'].includes(e))return '🖼';
  if(['b3d','ldw','bln','frw','grb','kit'].includes(e))return '🪑';
  if(['zip','rar','7z'].includes(e))return '🗜';
  if(['dwg','dxf'].includes(e))return '📐';
  return '📄';
}
function _fileViewable(name){          // brauzer o'zi ocha oladiganlar — yangi varaqda ko'rsatamiz
  const e=(String(name).split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
  return ['pdf','png','jpg','jpeg','webp','gif'].includes(e);
}
function openFiles(){
  const fl=document.getElementById('filesList');fl.innerHTML='';
  for(const f of MFILES){
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.className='fr';
    const base='/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/file/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(f.name);
    if(_fileViewable(f.name)){ a.href=base+'?inline=1'; a.target='_blank'; a.rel='noopener'; }
    else a.href=base;
    a.innerHTML='<span class="fi">'+fileIcon(f.name)+'</span>'+
      '<span class="fnm"><b>'+esc(f.name)+'</b><i>'+fmtFSize(f.size)+(f.at?(' \u00b7 '+esc(f.at)):'')+
        (_fileViewable(f.name)?'':' \u00b7 '+t('fileDl'))+'</i></span>'+
      '<span class="fgo">'+(_fileViewable(f.name)?'\uD83D\uDC41':'\u2B07\uFE0F')+'</span>';
    fl.appendChild(a);
  }
  document.getElementById('filesp').style.display='flex';
}
document.getElementById('mFiles').onclick=()=>{openFiles();closeMenu();};
document.getElementById('filesClose').onclick=()=>{document.getElementById('filesp').style.display='none';};

// ===== 📐 YIG'ISH CHIZMASI bo'limi (2026-09-03, foydalanuvchi: «menyuda sborochniy chertyoj
// bo'limida alohida funksiya, nomini ustiga bosganda/kursor olib borganda detal podsvetka»).
// FAQAT admin: Mini App havolaga ?ck=<kalit> qo'shadi, bu yerda localStorage'ga olinadi va
// URL'dan o'chiriladi; server (/api/{slug}/chert/{art}) kalit + admin brendini tekshiradi,
// boshqalarga 404 — tugma umuman chiqmaydi. 3 funksiya: 1-Sborka PDF, 2-Montaj PDF
// (biriktirilgan fayllardan), 3-Interaktiv (SVG: yuzalar/vinoskalar/qatorlar data-k bilan).
let CH_KEY=null;
const CH={data:null,lang:null,sheet:0,scale:1,busy:false};
(function(){ try{
  const u=new URL(location.href), ck=u.searchParams.get('ck');
  if(ck){ localStorage.setItem('dq_ck',ck); u.searchParams.delete('ck');
          history.replaceState(null,'',u.pathname+u.search+u.hash); }
  CH_KEY=localStorage.getItem('dq_ck');
}catch(e){} })();
function _chUrl(art,extra){
  return '/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/chert/'+encodeURIComponent(art)+'?k='+encodeURIComponent(CH_KEY||'')+extra;
}
function loadChert(article){
  CH.data=null; CH.lang=null;
  const b=document.getElementById('mChert'); if(b)b.style.display='none';
  if(!CH_KEY)return;
  fetch(_chUrl(article,'&probe=1')).then(r=>r.ok?r.json():null).then(d=>{
    if(d&&d.ok&&b)b.style.display='';
  }).catch(()=>{});
}
function openChert(){
  const p=document.getElementById('chertp'); p.style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('chInter').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('chBack').style.display='none';
  const lst=document.getElementById('chList'); lst.innerHTML=''; lst.style.display='';
  for(const f of MFILES.filter(f=>/^(1-Sborka_|2-Montaj_)/.test(f.name))){
    const a=document.createElement('a'); a.className='fr';
    a.href='/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/file/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(f.name)+'?inline=1';
    a.target='_blank'; a.rel='noopener';
    a.innerHTML='<span class="fi">\uD83D\uDCD5</span><span class="fnm"><b>'+esc(f.name)+'</b><i>PDF \u00b7 '+fmtFSize(f.size)+'</i></span><span class="fgo">\uD83D\uDC41</span>';
    lst.appendChild(a);
  }
  const a=document.createElement('a'); a.className='fr'; a.href='#';
  a.innerHTML='<span class="fi">\uD83D\uDD8D</span><span class="fnm"><b>3 \u00b7 '+esc(t('chertInter'))+'</b><i>'+esc(t('chertHint'))+'</i></span><span class="fgo">\u25B6</span>';
  a.onclick=ev=>{ev.preventDefault();openChertInter();};
  lst.appendChild(a);
}
async function openChertInter(){
  const lst=document.getElementById('chList'), box=document.getElementById('chInter'), st=document.getElementById('chStatus');
  lst.style.display='none'; box.style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('chBack').style.display='';
  if(CH.data&&CH.lang===LANG){ chRender(); return; }
  if(CH.busy)return; CH.busy=true;
  st.textContent=t('chertLoad'); st.style.display='';
  [...document.querySelectorAll('#chTabs .chip')].forEach(c=>c.remove());
  document.getElementById('chFrame').srcdoc='';
  try{
    const r=await fetch(_chUrl(ARTICLE,'&lang='+LANG)); const d=r.ok?await r.json():null;
    if(!d||!d.ok)throw new Error('chert');
    CH.data=d; CH.lang=LANG; CH.sheet=0; CH.scale=1; st.style.display='none'; chRender();
  }catch(e){ st.textContent=t('chertErr'); }
  CH.busy=false;
}
function _chDoc(){ const f=document.getElementById('chFrame'); try{return f.contentDocument;}catch(e){return null;} }
function chApplyScale(){
  const d=_chDoc(); if(!d||!d.body)return;
  const w=document.getElementById('chBody').clientWidth||800;
  const pw=((CH.data&&CH.data.page&&CH.data.page.w)||210)*3.7795;   // mm -> px (albom/tik)
  const fit=Math.max(0.25, Math.min(2.2, (w-24)/pw));
  d.body.style.zoom = (fit*CH.scale).toFixed(3);
}
// Varaqlar (A4) iframe ichida — CSS server bergani (PDF bilan AYNI), shu bilan chop etiladi.
const CH_EXTRA = "html,body{margin:0;background:#e9edf1}"
  + ".sh{margin:10px auto;box-shadow:0 2px 10px #00000026;background:#fff;page-break-after:always}"
  + "tr[data-k],[data-k]{cursor:pointer}"
  + "tr.hl>td{background:#ff8a2a3d!important}"
  + "path.hl{fill:#ff7a1a!important;fill-opacity:.92!important;stroke:#b3400a!important}"
  + "path.w.hl{fill:none!important;stroke:#d9480f!important;stroke-width:2.4!important}"
  + "path.fm.hl{fill:#ff7a1a!important;fill-opacity:1!important;stroke:#b3400a!important;stroke-width:1.2!important}"
  + "path.hit.hl{fill:none!important;stroke:#d9480f!important;stroke-opacity:1!important;stroke-width:1.6!important;stroke-dasharray:none}"
  + "g.lb.hl text{fill:#d9480f!important;font-weight:700}g.lb.hl path{stroke:#d9480f!important;stroke-width:1.4!important}g.lb.hl circle{fill:#d9480f!important}"
  + "@media print{html,body{background:#fff;zoom:1!important}.sh{margin:0;box-shadow:none}.hl,tr.hl>td{background:none!important}}";
function chRender(){
  const d=CH.data; if(!d)return;
  const tabs=document.getElementById('chTabs'); const tools=document.getElementById('chTools');
  [...tabs.querySelectorAll('.chip')].forEach(c=>c.remove());
  d.sheets.forEach((s,i)=>{ const b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='chip'+(i===CH.sheet?' on':'');
    b.textContent=(i+1)+'. '+s.title; b.title=s.title;
    b.onclick=()=>{CH.sheet=i; [...tabs.querySelectorAll('.chip')].forEach((c,j)=>c.classList.toggle('on',j===i));
      const doc=_chDoc(); const el=doc&&doc.querySelectorAll('.sh')[i]; if(el)el.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'}); };
    tabs.insertBefore(b, tools); });
  const f=document.getElementById('chFrame');
  f.srcdoc='<!doctype html><html lang="'+esc(d.lang||'uz')+'"><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
    +'<style>'+d.css+CH_EXTRA+'</style></head><body>'+d.sheets.map(s=>s.html).join('')+'</body></html>';
  f.onload=()=>{ const doc=_chDoc(); if(!doc)return; CH.scale=1; chApplyScale(); chBind(doc);
    doc.addEventListener('scroll',chSpy,true); doc.defaultView.addEventListener('scroll',chSpy); };
}
function chSpy(){                       // ko'rinib turgan varaq -> chip
  const doc=_chDoc(); if(!doc)return;
  const shs=[...doc.querySelectorAll('.sh')]; if(!shs.length)return;
  const y=(doc.defaultView.scrollY||doc.documentElement.scrollTop||0)+80;
  let i=0; shs.forEach((el,j)=>{ if(el.offsetTop<=y)i=j; });
  if(i!==CH.sheet){ CH.sheet=i;
    document.querySelectorAll('#chTabs .chip').forEach((c,j)=>c.classList.toggle('on',j===i)); }
}
function chHL(k, from, el){
  const doc=_chDoc(); if(!doc)return;
  doc.querySelectorAll('.hl').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('hl'));
  if(!k)return;
  let sel; try{ sel='[data-k="'+CSS.escape(k)+'"]'; }catch(e){ sel='[data-k="'+String(k).replace(/"/g,'')+'"]'; }
  // ⚠️ kalitlar VARAQ ichida yagona (har blokda pozitsiya 1 dan boshlanadi) — shuning
  // uchun yoritish faqat SHU varaq ichida bo'ladi, aks holda boshqa varaqlardagi
  // bir xil raqamli detallar ham yonardi.
  const root=(el&&el.closest&&el.closest('.sh'))||doc;
  root.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(e=>e.classList.add('hl'));
  // ⚠️ 2026-09-03 (foydalanuvchi): kursor detal ustiga kelganda jadval qatoriga FOKUS
  // (avtomatik siljish) QILINMAYDI — varaq o'z-o'zidan sakrab ketmasin. Faqat yoritish.
}
function chBind(doc){
  const kOf=ev=>{ const e=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('[data-k]'); return e?e.getAttribute('data-k'):null; };
  const src=ev=>'row';   // fokus/siljish yo'q — manba ahamiyatsiz
  const elOf=ev=>(ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('[data-k]'))||null;
  doc.addEventListener('pointerover',ev=>{ if(ev.pointerType!=='mouse')return;
    const k=kOf(ev); if(k)chHL(k,src(ev),elOf(ev)); });
  doc.addEventListener('click',ev=>{ chHL(kOf(ev),src(ev),elOf(ev)); });
}
{ document.getElementById('chZi').onclick=()=>{CH.scale=Math.min(4,CH.scale*1.25);chApplyScale();};
  document.getElementById('chZo').onclick=()=>{CH.scale=Math.max(0.4,CH.scale/1.25);chApplyScale();};
  document.getElementById('chPrint').onclick=()=>{ const f=document.getElementById('chFrame');
    try{ const d=_chDoc(); if(d&&d.body)d.body.style.zoom=1; f.contentWindow.focus(); f.contentWindow.print();
         setTimeout(chApplyScale,600); }catch(e){ toast(t('chertErr')); } };
  addEventListener('resize',()=>{ if(document.getElementById('chertp').style.display==='flex')chApplyScale(); });
  document.getElementById('mChert').onclick=()=>{openChert();closeMenu();};
  document.getElementById('chertClose').onclick=()=>{document.getElementById('chertp').style.display='none';};
  document.getElementById('chBack').onclick=()=>{openChert();};
}

// ===== SPETSIFIKATSIYA — detallar jadvali (2026-08-29, foydalanuvchi: SmartWood/Bazis
// spetsifikatsiyasi kabi). Manba: DATA.panels. Bir xil des+nom+o'lcham = bitta qator, soni.
// Kromka tomoni `butts[].seg` (panel-lokal kesma) ni kontur bbox qirralari bilan solishtirib
// topiladi; chiziq QALINLIGI kromka qalinligidan: ≥1mm — yaxlit, <1mm — shtrix.
function _spIsRect(poly){
  if(!poly||poly.length<4)return false;
  const pts=poly.slice(); if(pts.length===5&&Math.abs(pts[0][0]-pts[4][0])<1e-6&&Math.abs(pts[0][1]-pts[4][1])<1e-6)pts.pop();
  if(pts.length!==4)return false;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const a=pts[i],b=pts[(i+1)%4]; if(Math.abs(a[0]-b[0])>0.5&&Math.abs(a[1]-b[1])>0.5)return false;}
  return true;
}
// KROMKA CHIZIG'I — foydalanuvchi qoidasi (2026-08-30):
//   yaxlit chiziq  = o'sha tomonda KROMKA BOR (qalinligi ko'rsatilMAYDI)
//   shtrix chiziq  = kromka bor, lekin KONTUR NOSTANDART (yoki o'sha tomonda vyrez bor)
//                    -> «chertyojga qara»
//   chiziq yo'q    = kromka yo'q
// Ilgari chiziq turi kromka QALINLIGIGA bog'liq edi (≥1mm / <1mm) — bu xato edi.
// Ichki qiymatlar: 2 = yaxlit, 1 = shtrix, 0 = yo'q (CSS sinflari t2/b2, t1/b1 o'zgarmadi).
function _spCutOnSide(c, side, mnX, mxX, mnY, mxY, tol){
  let a,b2,cc,d;                                    // kesim/o'yiq bbox'i
  if(c.t==='circle'){ a=c.x-c.r; b2=c.x+c.r; cc=c.y-c.r; d=c.y+c.r; }
  else{ const pts=c.pts||[]; if(!pts.length)return false;
        a=b2=pts[0][0]; cc=d=pts[0][1];
        for(const q of pts){ if(q[0]<a)a=q[0]; if(q[0]>b2)b2=q[0]; if(q[1]<cc)cc=q[1]; if(q[1]>d)d=q[1]; } }
  if(side==='ymax')return Math.abs(d-mxY)<=tol && b2>mnX-tol && a<mxX+tol;
  if(side==='ymin')return Math.abs(cc-mnY)<=tol && b2>mnX-tol && a<mxX+tol;
  if(side==='xmin')return Math.abs(a-mnX)<=tol && d>mnY-tol && cc<mxY+tol;
  return              Math.abs(b2-mxX)<=tol && d>mnY-tol && cc<mxY+tol;
}
function _spEdges(p){                 // {wA,wB,hA,hB: 0|1|2, other:n}
  const r={wA:0,wB:0,hA:0,hB:0,other:0};
  const poly=p.poly||[]; const bs=p.butts||[];
  if(poly.length<3){ r.other=bs.length; return r; }
  let minX=1e9,maxX=-1e9,minY=1e9,maxY=-1e9;
  for(const q of poly){ if(q[0]<minX)minX=q[0]; if(q[0]>maxX)maxX=q[0]; if(q[1]<minY)minY=q[1]; if(q[1]>maxY)maxY=q[1]; }
  const bw=maxX-minX, bh=maxY-minY, tol=Math.max(3,0.02*Math.max(bw,bh));
  const xIsW = Math.abs(bw-(p.w||0)) <= Math.abs(bh-(p.w||0));      // x o'qi = "eni" (w) bo'lsa
  const rect=_spIsRect(poly);                       // butun detal konturi to'g'ri to'rtburchakmi
  const nonStd=!rect||!!p.sweep||(p.bevels||[]).length>0;
  const cuts=(p.cuts||[]).concat(p.pockets||[]);
  const cov={wA:0,wB:0,hA:0,hB:0}, len={wA:0,wB:0,hA:0,hB:0}, cut={wA:false,wB:false,hA:false,hB:false};
  const keyOf=(side)=>{
    const isW = xIsW ? (side==='ymax'||side==='ymin') : (side==='xmin'||side==='xmax');
    const first = (side==='ymax'||side==='xmin');
    return (isW?'w':'h')+(first?'A':'B');
  };
  for(const side of ['ymax','ymin','xmin','xmax']){
    const k=keyOf(side);
    len[k]=(side==='ymax'||side==='ymin')?bw:bh;
    if(cuts.some(c=>_spCutOnSide(c,side,minX,maxX,minY,maxY,tol)))cut[k]=true;
  }
  for(const b of bs){
    if(!b.seg||b.seg.length<2){ r.other++; continue; }
    const [x1,y1]=b.seg[0],[x2,y2]=b.seg[1]; const mx=(x1+x2)/2,my=(y1+y2)/2;
    let side=null;
    if(Math.abs(y1-y2)<=tol&&Math.abs(my-maxY)<=tol)side='ymax';
    else if(Math.abs(y1-y2)<=tol&&Math.abs(my-minY)<=tol)side='ymin';
    else if(Math.abs(x1-x2)<=tol&&Math.abs(mx-minX)<=tol)side='xmin';
    else if(Math.abs(x1-x2)<=tol&&Math.abs(mx-maxX)<=tol)side='xmax';
    if(!side){ r.other++; continue; }               // tomoni aniqlanmadi -> kontur nostandart
    cov[keyOf(side)]+=Math.hypot(x2-x1,y2-y1);
  }
  for(const k of ['wA','wB','hA','hB']){
    if(cov[k]<=0.5){ r[k]=0; continue; }             // kromka yo'q -> chiziq yo'q
    const full = cov[k] >= 0.95*(len[k]||1);         // butun tomon bo'ylab tutashmi
    r[k] = (!nonStd && full && !cut[k]) ? 2 : 1;     // 2 = yaxlit, 1 = shtrix («chertyojga qara»)
  }
  return r;
}
function _spCell(v,a,b){ return '<span class="k'+(a?' t'+a:'')+(b?' b'+b:'')+'">'+esc(fmtN(v))+'</span>'; }
function fmtN(v){ v=Math.round((v||0)*10)/10; return String(v).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,'\u202f'); }
function _spDesCmp(a,b){ return String(a).localeCompare(String(b),undefined,{numeric:true,sensitivity:'base'}); }
function buildSpec(){
  const ps=((DATA&&DATA.panels)||[]).filter(p=>!p.layout && (p.des||p.name));
  const body=document.getElementById('specBody');
  if(!ps.length){ body.innerHTML='<div style="padding:30px;text-align:center;color:var(--mut)">'+esc(t('specEmpty'))+'</div>'; return; }
  // eng ko'p uchraydigan qalinlik — "utolshenie" (2x) shunga nisbatan
  const tc=new Map(); for(const p of ps){const k=Math.abs(p.thick||0); tc.set(k,(tc.get(k)||0)+1);}
  let base=16,bn=-1; for(const [k,v] of tc){ if(v>bn){bn=v;base=k;} }
  // TEKIS ro'yxat (2026-08-30, foydalanuvchi): material bo'yicha GURUHLASH YO'Q — material
  // oddiy ustunga aylandi. Saralash: OBOZNACHENIYA bo'yicha (natural, «2» < «10»).
  // des bo'sh bo'lgan modellar ham bor (AKMAL-AKA) — ular oxirida, nom bo'yicha saralanadi.
  const rows=new Map();
  for(const p of ps){
    const rk=String(p.des||'')+'|'+(p.name||'')+'|'+(p.mat||'')+'|'
            +Math.round(p.w||0)+'x'+Math.round(p.h||0)+'x'+Math.abs(p.thick||0);
    if(!rows.has(rk))rows.set(rk,{p,n:0});
    rows.get(rk).n++;
  }
  const C=t('specCols'); let no=0;
  let h='<table class="sptbl"><thead><tr>'+C.map(c=>'<th>'+esc(c)+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>';
  const list=[...rows.values()].sort((a,b)=>{
    const da=(a.p.des||'').trim(), db=(b.p.des||'').trim();
    if(!!da!==!!db)return da?-1:1;                       // oboznacheniyasizlar OXIRIDA
    return _spDesCmp(da,db)||_spDesCmp(a.p.name,b.p.name)||((b.p.w||0)-(a.p.w||0));
  });
  for(const r of list){
    const p=r.p, e=_spEdges(p);
    const holes=(p.holes||[]).length, paz=(p.pockets||[]).length;
    const kont=!_spIsRect(p.poly)||(p.cuts||[]).length>0||(p.bevels||[]).length>0||!!p.sweep;
    const th=Math.abs(p.thick||0), utol=base>0&&th>=base*2-0.5;
    const _up=utolParts(p);          // ANIQ tarkib (Bazis `Plastics`): 18+18
    h+='<tr><td>'+(++no)+'</td><td class="des">'+esc(p.des||'')+'</td><td class="l">'+esc(p.name||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td class="l mt">'+esc(p.mat||'')+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+_spCell(p.w,e.wA,e.wB)+'</td><td>'+_spCell(p.h,e.hA,e.hB)+'</td>'
      +'<td>'+fmtN(th)+'</td><td><b>'+r.n+'</b></td>'
      +'<td class="'+(holes?'ok':'no')+'">'+(holes?('\u2699 '+holes):'\u2014')+'</td>'
      +'<td class="'+(paz?'ok':'no')+'">'+(paz?('\u25AD '+paz):'\u2014')+'</td>'
      +'<td class="'+(kont?'ok':'no')+'">'+(kont?'\u2726':'\u2014')+'</td>'
      +'<td class="'+((utol||_up)?'ok':'no')+'">'
        +(_up?('\u2261 '+_up.map(fmtN).join('+'))
             :(utol?('\u2261 '+fmtN(th)):'\u2014'))+'</td>'
      +'<td class="otk">'+(e.other?('K\u00d7'+e.other):'')+'</td></tr>';
  }
  h+='</tbody></table>';
  body.innerHTML='<div id="specZoom">'+h+'</div>'; _spApplyZoom();
  document.getElementById('specLegend').innerHTML=esc(t('specLegend'))
    +' <span class="k t2 b2">'+esc(t('specThick'))+'</span> <span class="k t1 b1">'+esc(t('specThin'))+'</span>'
    +' <span class="k">'+esc(t('specNoEdge'))+'</span>'
    +' \u00b7 \u2699 '+esc(t('specHoles'))+' \u00b7 \u25AD '+esc(t('specPaz'))+' \u00b7 K\u00d7n \u2014 '+esc(t('specOther'));
  document.getElementById('specTitle').textContent=t('specTitle')+' \u00b7 '+(ARTICLE||'')+' \u00b7 '+no;
}
