function closeSpec(){ document.getElementById('spec').style.display='none'; document.body.classList.remove('specprint'); }
document.getElementById('btnSpec').onclick=openSpec;
document.getElementById('specClose').onclick=closeSpec;
document.getElementById('specPrint').onclick=()=>{ try{window.print();}catch(e){} };
// ---- masshtab (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-29: «hujjatni masshtablashtirib bo'lmayapti») ----
// Sahifada user-scalable=no (3D uchun), shu sabab jadvalni O'ZIMIZ kattalashtiramiz:
// CSS `zoom` (matn ham, chiziqlar ham mos o'sadi, aylantirish oddiy scroll bilan).
let _spZ=1;
function _spApplyZoom(){
  const z=document.getElementById('specZoom'); if(z)z.style.zoom=_spZ;
  const l=document.getElementById('specZoomLbl'); if(l)l.textContent=Math.round(_spZ*100)+'%';
}
function _spZoomTo(v){ _spZ=Math.max(0.5,Math.min(3,v)); _spApplyZoom(); try{localStorage.setItem('specZoom',_spZ);}catch(e){} }
try{ const z=parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('specZoom')); if(z>0)_spZ=z; }catch(e){}
document.getElementById('specIn').onclick=()=>_spZoomTo(_spZ*1.2);
document.getElementById('specOut').onclick=()=>_spZoomTo(_spZ/1.2);
(function(){
  const b=document.getElementById('specBody'); let d0=0,z0=1;
  b.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{ if(e.touches.length===2){ d0=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY); z0=_spZ; } },{passive:true});
  b.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>{ if(e.touches.length===2&&d0>0){ e.preventDefault();
    const d=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY);
    _spZoomTo(z0*d/d0); } },{passive:false});
  b.addEventListener('touchend',()=>{ d0=0; },{passive:true});
  b.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{ if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey){ e.preventDefault(); _spZoomTo(_spZ*(e.deltaY<0?1.1:1/1.1)); } },{passive:false});
})();

function loadDrawings(article){
  DRAWINGS=new Set();
  fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/drawings/'+encodeURIComponent(article))
    .then(r=>r.ok?r.json():null).then(d=>{
      if(d&&d.des)DRAWINGS=new Set(d.des.map(String));
      if(selected)updBazisBtn(selected.userData);   // model kelguncha tanlangan bo'lsa ham yangilansin
    }).catch(()=>{});                                // chizma bo'lmasa tugma shunchaki chiqmaydi
}
function updBazisBtn(p){
  const b=document.getElementById('btnBazis');
  if(b)b.style.display=(p&&DRAWINGS.has(String(p.des)))?'flex':'none';
}

// ===== 🧩 BLOK (SBORKA) CHIZMASI — 2026-08-30 =====
// Bazis `.bln` ichida detal chizmalaridan TASHQARI sborka chizmalari ham keladi va ular
// allaqachon saqlanadi (`drawings` jadvali), lekin viewer ularni hech qayerda ko'rsatmasdi:
// tugma faqat `DRAWINGS.has(p.des)` bo'lganda chiqardi. Real modelda (92 detal, 72 chizma)
// 14 tasi shunday "ko'rinmas" edi: `00_000`/`00_00` (umumiy sborka) va `NN_000` (blok sborkasi).
// ANIQLASH: sborka chizmasi = biror detalning `des` iga MOS KELMAYDIGAN chizma. Blokniki —
// shu detal bilan bir xil prefiksda (`01_007` -> `01_...`). Nom tartibi Bazisning
// «Формат наименования чертежей» sozlamasiga bog'liq, shuning uchun bitta nomga bog'lanib
// qolmaymiz: `01_000` -> `01_00` -> `01` tartibida qidiramiz va faqat MAVJUDINI olamiz.
let ASM=new Set();                 // detalga tegishli bo'lmagan chizmalar (sborkalar)
function asmScan(){
  ASM=new Set();
  const pset=new Set(((DATA&&DATA.panels)||[]).map(p=>String(p.des||'')).filter(Boolean));
  DRAWINGS.forEach(d=>{ d=String(d); if(!pset.has(d))ASM.add(d); });
}
function blockDrawOf(des){         // '01_007' -> '01_000' (yoki null)
  const s=String(des||''), i=s.indexOf('_');
  if(i<1||ASM.has(s))return null;  // sborkaning "bloki" bo'lmaydi (o'zi sborka)
  const pre=s.slice(0,i);
  for(const c of [pre+'_000', pre+'_00', pre]) if(ASM.has(c)&&c!==s)return c;
  return null;
}
// Ko'rish: rasm CSS transform bilan suriladi/kattalashadi (2D chizmadagi imo-ishoralar bilan bir xil).
// s=1 — chizma ekran ENIGA sig'gan holat (rasm `width:100%`).
// MUAMMO (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-23): Bazis chizmalarining ko'pchiligi TIK — bo'yi enidan
// 1.3–1.7 barobar katta (o'lchandi: viewBox 1730x2914, 1812x2976, 1900x2412...). Eniga
// sig'dirilganda bo'yi ekrandan chiqib ketardi, uzoqlashtirish esa `Math.max(1,...)` bilan
// cheklangani uchun ORQAGA QAYTA OLMASDI — chizmani to'liq ko'rishning iloji yo'q edi.
// Yechim: `fit` — butun chizma (eni ham, bo'yi ham) sig'adigan masshtab. Ochilganda shu
// qo'yiladi va uzoqlashtirishning eng past chegarasi ham shu bo'ladi.
const bzV={s:1,ox:0,oy:0,fit:1};
function bzFit(){
  const b=document.getElementById('bzBody'), im=document.getElementById('bzImg');
  const H=b?b.clientHeight:0, ih=im?im.clientHeight:0;      // ih — `width:100%` dagi bo'yi
  bzV.fit=(H>0&&ih>0)?Math.min(1,H/ih):1;                   // chizma pastroq bo'lsa 1 qoladi
  return bzV.fit;
}
function bzReset(){ bzFit(); bzV.s=bzV.fit; bzV.ox=0; bzV.oy=0; bzApply(); }
function bzApply(){
  const b=document.getElementById('bzBody'), im=document.getElementById('bzImg');
  const W=b.clientWidth, H=b.clientHeight, iw=im.clientWidth*bzV.s, ih=im.clientHeight*bzV.s;
  bzV.ox = iw<=W ? (W-iw)/2 : Math.min(0,Math.max(W-iw,bzV.ox));   // chetdan chiqib ketmasin
  bzV.oy = ih<=H ? (H-ih)/2 : Math.min(0,Math.max(H-ih,bzV.oy));
  im.style.transform='translate('+bzV.ox.toFixed(1)+'px,'+bzV.oy.toFixed(1)+'px) scale('+bzV.s.toFixed(3)+')';
}
function bzZoomAt(cx,cy,ns){
  ns=Math.max(bzV.fit,Math.min(14,ns));      // fit dan pastga tushmaydi
  const wx=(cx-bzV.ox)/bzV.s, wy=(cy-bzV.oy)/bzV.s;
  bzV.s=ns; bzV.ox=cx-wx*ns; bzV.oy=cy-wy*ns; bzApply();
}
function bzZoomMid(k){
  const b=document.getElementById('bzBody');
  bzZoomAt(b.clientWidth/2, b.clientHeight/2, bzV.s*k);
}
let bzCur='', bzFrom='';        // hozir ochiq chizma / qaysi DETAL chizmasidan kelingan
function bzUpUpd(){              // pastki chap tugma: blokka o'tish yoki detalga qaytish
  const b=document.getElementById('bzUp');
  if(!b)return;
  asmScan();
  let go='', lbl='';
  if(bzFrom&&bzCur!==bzFrom){ go=bzFrom; lbl=t('bzPart'); }        // sborkadamiz -> detalga
  else { const blk=blockDrawOf(bzFrom||bzCur); if(blk){ go=blk; lbl=t('bzBlk'); } }
  b.dataset.go=go;
  b.textContent=lbl;
  b.style.display=go?'block':'none';
}
function openBazis(des, keepFrom){
  const img=document.getElementById('bzImg'), msg=document.getElementById('bzMsg');
  asmScan();                                                       // sarlavhada «sborka» yozuvi uchun ham kerak
  if(!keepFrom)bzFrom=String(des);                                 // detal tugmasidan ochildi
  bzCur=String(des);
  document.getElementById('bzTitle').textContent=t('bazis')+' · '+des
        +(ASM.has(String(des))?' · '+t('bzAsm'):'');
  document.getElementById('bz').style.display='flex';
  bzV.s=1; bzV.ox=0; bzV.oy=0; bzV.fit=1;
  img.style.display='none'; msg.style.display='flex'; msg.textContent=t('bazisLoad');
  // MUHIM: display='block' bzReset() dan OLDIN — yashirin elementning clientHeight'i 0
  img.onload=()=>{msg.style.display='none';img.style.display='block';bzReset();};
  img.onerror=()=>{msg.style.display='flex';msg.textContent=t('bazisErr');};
  img.src='/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/ldw/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE)
          +'/'+encodeURIComponent(des);
  bzUpUpd();
}
function closeBazis(){
  bzCur=''; bzFrom='';
  const _u=document.getElementById('bzUp'); if(_u)_u.style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('bz').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('bzImg').src='';       // xotirani bo'shatamiz (SVG katta bo'lishi mumkin)
}
document.getElementById('btnBazis').onclick=()=>{
  if(selected&&selected.userData)openBazis(String(selected.userData.des));
};
document.getElementById('bzUp').onclick=function(){
  const g=this.dataset.go;
  if(g)openBazis(g, true);       // keepFrom: qaysi detaldan kelganimiz esda qoladi
};
document.getElementById('bzClose').onclick=closeBazis;
document.getElementById('bzIn').onclick=()=>bzZoomMid(1.6);
document.getElementById('bzOut').onclick=()=>bzZoomMid(1/1.6);
document.getElementById('bzFitBtn').onclick=()=>bzReset();
// oyna o'lchami/burilish o'zgarsa fit qayta hisoblanadi
addEventListener('resize',()=>{
  const bz=document.getElementById('bz');
  if(!bz||bz.style.display!=='flex')return;
  const old=bzV.fit; bzFit();
  if(bzV.s<=old+1e-6) bzV.s=bzV.fit;        // "sig'dirilgan" holatda edi -> shunday qoladi
  else bzV.s=Math.max(bzV.fit,bzV.s);
  bzApply();
});
(function(){                       // surish + ikki barmoq bilan kattalashtirish
  const b=document.getElementById('bzBody');
  const pos=e=>{const r=b.getBoundingClientRect();return[e.clientX-r.left,e.clientY-r.top];};
  const pts=new Map();
  let px=0,py=0,pinch0=0,s0=1,pinched=false,moved=0,lastTap=0;
  b.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    b.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); pts.set(e.pointerId,pos(e));
    if(pts.size===1){moved=0;pinched=false;[px,py]=pos(e);}
    else if(pts.size===2){pinched=true;const a=[...pts.values()];
      pinch0=Math.hypot(a[0][0]-a[1][0],a[0][1]-a[1][1]);s0=bzV.s;}
  });
  b.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!pts.has(e.pointerId))return;
    pts.set(e.pointerId,pos(e));
    if(pts.size===1){
      const[qx,qy]=pos(e),dx=qx-px,dy=qy-py;
      moved+=Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy);
      if(moved>3){bzV.ox+=dx;bzV.oy+=dy;px=qx;py=qy;bzApply();}
    }else if(pts.size===2&&pinch0>4){
      const a=[...pts.values()], d=Math.hypot(a[0][0]-a[1][0],a[0][1]-a[1][1]);
      bzZoomAt((a[0][0]+a[1][0])/2,(a[0][1]+a[1][1])/2,s0*d/pinch0);
    }
  });
  const end=e=>{
    if(pts.has(e.pointerId)&&pts.size===1&&!pinched&&moved<6){
      const now=Date.now(), [qx,qy]=pos(e);         // ikki marta bosish — yaqinlash/qaytish
      if(now-lastTap<320)bzZoomAt(qx,qy,bzV.s>1.05?1:3);
      lastTap=now;
    }
    pts.delete(e.pointerId);
    if(pts.size===1){const a=[...pts.values()][0];px=a[0];py=a[1];moved=99;}  // pan langari
  };
  b.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
  b.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
  b.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{const[qx,qy]=pos(e);e.preventDefault();
    bzZoomAt(qx,qy,bzV.s*(e.deltaY<0?1.18:1/1.18));},{passive:false});
})();

document.getElementById('btnReport').onclick=openReport;
document.getElementById('repCancel').onclick=()=>{document.getElementById('repwrap').style.display='none';focusHid();};
document.getElementById('repSend').onclick=async()=>{
  // ISM MAJBURIY (2026-08-26): xabar kimdan kelganini bilish uchun — anonim xabarning
  // haqiqiyligini tekshirib bo'lmaydi. Server tomonda ham tekshiriladi (app.py).
  const nEl=document.getElementById('repName');
  const sender=nEl.value.trim();
  if(sender.length<3){ nEl.classList.add('bad'); toast(t('repNoName'),true); nEl.focus(); return; }
  nEl.classList.remove('bad');
  try{localStorage.setItem('repName',sender);}catch(e){}
  const message=document.getElementById('repMsg').value.trim();
  if(!message){toast(t('repWrite'),true);return;}
  const contact=document.getElementById('repContact').value.trim();
  const des=selected?selected.userData.des:'';
  document.getElementById('repwrap').style.display='none';
  // modelning joriy ko'rinishini skrinshot qilamiz (adminga muammoni ko'rsatish uchun)
  let shot='';
  try{renderer.render(scene,camera);shot=renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.7);}catch(e){}
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/report',{method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({article:ARTICLE,des,message,contact,sender,shot})});
    if(r.ok)toast(t('repSent'));
    else if(r.status===429){                       // limit: IP (5/10daq) yoki model (10/soat)
      let j=null; try{j=await r.json();}catch(e){}
      toast(j&&j.error==='model_limit'?t('repLimitModel'):t('repLimitIp'),true,4000);
    }
    else toast(t('repFail'),true);
  }catch(e){toast(t('serverErr'),true);}
  focusHid();
};

// ---- AR (xonangizda ko'rish) — model-viewer (Android: Scene Viewer, iPhone: Quick Look) ----
let _mvLoaded=false;
function loadModelViewer(cb){
  if(_mvLoaded||customElements.get('model-viewer')){cb();return;}
  const s=document.createElement('script');s.type='module';
  s.src='https://unpkg.com/@google/model-viewer@3.5.0/dist/model-viewer.min.js';
  s.onload=()=>{_mvLoaded=true;cb();};s.onerror=()=>cb();
  document.head.appendChild(s);
}
function loadExporter(cb){
  if(THREE.GLTFExporter){cb();return;}
  loadScript(['https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/js/exporters/GLTFExporter.js',
              'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/exporters/GLTFExporter.js'],cb,()=>cb());
}
function arFail(msg){document.getElementById('arLoad').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('arwrap').style.display='none';toast(msg||'AR xato',true);}
function startAR(){
  closeMenu();
  if(!ARTICLE||!model||!meshes.length){toast(t('openModel'),true);return;}
  const wrap=document.getElementById('arwrap');wrap.style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('arHost').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('arLoad').style.display='flex';
  loadExporter(()=>{
    if(!THREE.GLTFExporter){arFail('Eksport kutubxonasi yuklanmadi');return;}
    exportAndUpload();
  });
}
function exportAndUpload(){
  const prevSel=selected;
  select(null);   // toza ko'rinish (hammasi opaque)
  const dV=dimGroup&&dimGroup.visible, fV=furnMarkerGroup&&furnMarkerGroup.visible;
  if(dimGroup)dimGroup.visible=false;             // razmer chiziqlari AR'da kerak emas
  if(furnMarkerGroup)furnMarkerGroup.visible=false;// v1: faqat panel struktura (furnitura keyin)
  for(const m of meshes)for(const mt of _matsOf(m))mt.transparent=false;
  model.scale.setScalar(0.001);                   // mm -> metr (AR real o'lcham)
  model.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const restore=()=>{
    model.scale.setScalar(1);
    for(const m of meshes)for(const mt of _matsOf(m))mt.transparent=true;
    if(dimGroup)dimGroup.visible=dV;
    if(furnMarkerGroup)furnMarkerGroup.visible=fV;
    model.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    if(prevSel)select(prevSel);
  };
  try{
    const exp=new THREE.GLTFExporter();
    exp.parse(model,glb=>{restore();uploadGLB(glb);},{binary:true,onlyVisible:true});
  }catch(e){restore();arFail('Eksport xatosi');}
}
async function uploadGLB(glb){
  try{
    const r=await fetch('/api/'+encodeURIComponent(SLUG)+'/ar/'+encodeURIComponent(ARTICLE),
      {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'model/gltf-binary'},body:glb});
    const j=await r.json();
    if(!j||!j.ok||!j.url){arFail('Yuklab bo\'lmadi');return;}
    showModelViewer(j.url);
  }catch(e){arFail('Server xatosi');}
}
function showModelViewer(url){
  loadModelViewer(()=>{
    if(!customElements.get('model-viewer')){arFail('AR kutubxonasi yuklanmadi');return;}
    const host=document.getElementById('arHost');host.innerHTML='';
    const mv=document.createElement('model-viewer');
    mv.setAttribute('src',url);
    mv.setAttribute('ar','');
    mv.setAttribute('ar-modes','webxr scene-viewer quick-look');
    mv.setAttribute('ar-scale','fixed');
    mv.setAttribute('camera-controls','');
    mv.setAttribute('auto-rotate','');
    mv.setAttribute('shadow-intensity','1');
    mv.setAttribute('exposure','1');
    // KATTA AR tugmasi (slot) — AR mavjud bo'lsa model-viewer ko'rsatadi, bosilsa AR ochiladi
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.setAttribute('slot','ar-button');btn.id='arGo';
    btn.textContent=t('arGo');
    mv.appendChild(btn);
    host.appendChild(mv);
    document.getElementById('arLoad').style.display='none';
  });
}
document.getElementById('mAR').onclick=()=>{closeMenu();toast(t('soon'));};   // AR hali tayyor emas (startAR saqlanib turibdi)
document.getElementById('arClose').onclick=()=>{
  document.getElementById('arwrap').style.display='none';
  document.getElementById('arHost').innerHTML='';
};

// ---- toast ----
let toastTimer=null;
function toast(t,err,ms){const el=document.getElementById('toast');el.textContent=t;el.className=err?'err':'';el.style.display='block';clearTimeout(toastTimer);toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>el.style.display='none',ms||2000);}

// ============ ONBOARDING: tugma nomlari (kirishda) + 10s harakatsizlikda qo'l imo-ishorasi ============
let _obIdle=null, _obCoachHidden=false, _obStarted=false;
function _obCoachShow(){
  const coach=document.getElementById('coach'); const top=document.getElementById('top');
  if(!coach||!top)return; coach.innerHTML=''; _obCoachHidden=false;
  const W=window.innerWidth;
  // aniq QISQA nomlar (dinamik title'lar o'rniga) — telefon toolbarига sig'sin
  const CO={btnCam:t('cam').split(' ')[0],btnBlockTop:t('coBlock'),btnAnimTop:t('coAnim'),
            btnHideTop:t('coHide'),btnMenu:t('menu'),btnReport:t('report').split(' ')[0],btnLang:t('coLang')};
  top.querySelectorAll('button,a').forEach(function(btn){
    if(btn.offsetParent===null)return;                 // ko'rinmas tugma
    const title=btn.getAttribute('title'); if(!title)return;
    const short=CO[btn.id]||title.split(/[—·\/]/)[0].trim().split(' ')[0];
    const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    const b=document.createElement('div'); b.className='coachB'; b.textContent=short;
    let cx=r.left+r.width/2;
    coach.appendChild(b);
    const hw=b.offsetWidth/2+4;                         // chetdan chiqib ketmasin
    cx=Math.max(hw, Math.min(W-hw, cx));
    b.style.left=cx+'px'; b.style.top=(r.bottom+7)+'px';
    requestAnimationFrame(function(){b.classList.add('show');});
  });
  clearTimeout(_obCoachShow._t); _obCoachShow._t=setTimeout(_obCoachHide, 5500);
}
function _obCoachHide(){
  if(_obCoachHidden)return; _obCoachHidden=true;
  const coach=document.getElementById('coach'); if(!coach)return;
  coach.querySelectorAll('.coachB').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('show');});
  setTimeout(function(){if(coach)coach.innerHTML='';}, 400);
}
/* Sichqonchami yoki barmoqmi? Bu MATNNI ham, o'rtadagi animatsiyani ham belgilaydi
   (avval kompyuterda ham "barmoq bilan suring" deb turardi — foydalanuvchi ko'rsatdi). */
const _OB_MOUSE=(function(){
  // FAQAT media-so'rovga tayanib bo'lmaydi: ba'zi brauzerlar (jumladan headless Chromium)
  // sichqonchali kompyuterda ham `pointer: fine` ni FALSE qaytardi -> desktopda "barmoq
  // bilan suring" chiqib qolardi. Shuning uchun ikkinchi dalil: qurilmada sensor bormi.
  let fine=false, touchable=true;
  try{ fine=matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches; }catch(e){}
  try{ touchable=('ontouchstart' in window)||(navigator.maxTouchPoints||0)>0; }catch(e){}
  return fine || !touchable;      // sensor umuman yo'q -> aniq sichqoncha
})();
const OB_STEP_MS=3000;                     // har bir qadam necha ms turadi
function _obSteps(){                       // ico = natija belgisi, hand = qanday qilish
  const H =_OB_MOUSE?'\uD83D\uDDB1\uFE0F':'\uD83D\uDC46';   // sichqoncha / ko'rsatkich barmoq
  const H2=_OB_MOUSE?'\uD83D\uDDB1\uFE0F':'\uD83E\uDD0F';   // 2-qadam: g'ildirak / chimchilash
  const m=_OB_MOUSE;
  return [
    {ico:'\uD83D\uDD04', k:m?'gs1m':'gs1t', hand:H,  an:'gaDrag'},              // aylantirish
    {ico:'\uD83D\uDD0D', k:m?'gs2m':'gs2t', hand:H2, an:m?'gaWheel':'gaPinch'}, // masshtab
    {ico:'\uD83D\uDC46', k:m?'gs3m':'gs3t', hand:H,  an:'gaTap'},               // detal tanlash
    {ico:'\uD83D\uDEAA', k:m?'gs4m':'gs4t', hand:H,  an:'gaTap2'}               // eshikni ochish
  ];
}
let _obSeq=null, _obStep=0, _obFadeT=null;
function _obPaint(){
  const gh=document.getElementById('ghand'), gt=document.getElementById('gtext');
  const s=_obSteps()[_obStep]; if(!gh||!gt||!s)return;
  const ic=document.getElementById('ghandIco'); if(ic)ic.textContent=s.hand;
  gh.className='on '+s.an;                 // qadam animatsiyasi almashadi
  const gi=document.getElementById('gtIco'), gx=document.getElementById('gtTxt');
  if(gi)gi.textContent=s.ico;
  if(gx)gx.textContent=t(s.k);
  const dots=document.querySelectorAll('#gdots b');
  for(let i=0;i<dots.length;i++)dots[i].classList.toggle('on', i===_obStep);
}
function _obGestureShow(){
  const gh=document.getElementById('ghand'), gt=document.getElementById('gtext');
  if(!gh||!gt)return;
  clearInterval(_obSeq); clearTimeout(_obFadeT);
  _obStep=0; _obPaint(); gt.classList.add('on');
  _obSeq=setInterval(function(){
    if(_obStep>=_obSteps().length-1){ _obGestureHide(); return; }   // oxirgi qadam tugadi
    gh.classList.add('gfade'); gt.classList.add('gfade');           // qadamlar orasida yumshoq o'tish
    _obFadeT=setTimeout(function(){
      _obStep++; _obPaint();
      gh.classList.remove('gfade'); gt.classList.remove('gfade');
    },200);
  }, OB_STEP_MS);
}
function _obGestureHide(){
  clearInterval(_obSeq); _obSeq=null; clearTimeout(_obFadeT);
  const gh=document.getElementById('ghand'), gt=document.getElementById('gtext');
  if(gh)gh.className=''; if(gt)gt.classList.remove('on','gfade');
}
function _obResetIdle(){                 // foydalanuvchi biror harakat qildi
  _obCoachHide(); _obGestureHide();
  clearTimeout(_obIdle);
  _obIdle=setTimeout(_obGestureShow, 10000);   // 10s harakatsizlik -> imo-ishora
}
function startOnboarding(){
  if(_obStarted||SHOT)return; _obStarted=true;
  ['pointerdown','wheel','keydown','touchstart'].forEach(function(ev){
    window.addEventListener(ev, _obResetIdle, {passive:true});
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){ if(!_obCoachHidden)_obCoachShow(); });
  setTimeout(_obCoachShow, 500);   // toolbar joylashgach nomlarni ko'rsatamiz
  _obResetIdle();
}
// xato oynasi bosilsa — qayta yuklash (qotib qolmasin, xavfsizlik)
document.getElementById('err').addEventListener('click',()=>location.reload());

// ---- 3D boshqaruv ----
// ===== ZOOM — KURSOR/PINCH NUQTASIGA (2026-08-29, foydalanuvchi: «yaqinlashtirish
// noqulay, erkinlik cheklangan»). Ilgari zoom doim orbit MARKAZIGA yaqinlashardi —
// kerakli joyni ko'rish uchun avval pan, keyin zoom qilish kerak edi. Endi ekran nuqtasi
// ostidagi nuqta (model yuzasi; bo'sh joy bo'lsa — nishon chuqurligidagi tekislik) JOYIDA
// QOLADI, orbit markazi unga qarab suriladi: T' = H + (T-H)·k. Masofa qadam k — silliq
// (trackpad mayda deltalari ham), chegara [120, 60000].
function zoomAt(sx,sy,k){
  if(!camera||!raycaster)return;
  k=Math.max(0.5,Math.min(2,k));
  const nd=Math.max(120,Math.min(60000,dist*k)); if(Math.abs(nd-dist)<1e-6)return; k=nd/dist;
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(sx/innerWidth*2-1,-(sy/innerHeight)*2+1);
  raycaster.setFromCamera(v,camera);
  let H=null;
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(meshes.filter(m=>m.visible),false);
  if(hits.length)H=hits[0].point.clone();
  else{ const nrm=new THREE.Vector3(); camera.getWorldDirection(nrm); const T=new THREE.Vector3(cx,cy,cz);
        const pl=new THREE.Plane().setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(nrm,T); H=new THREE.Vector3();
        if(!raycaster.ray.intersectPlane(pl,H))H=T; }
  cx=H.x+(cx-H.x)*k; cy=H.y+(cy-H.y)*k; cz=H.z+(cz-H.z)*k;
  // Nishon modeldan uzoqqa qochib ketmasin: uzoqlashtirishda u har safar kursor
  // nuqtasidan k marta siljiydi va bir necha qadamdan keyin model kadr chetida
  // qolib ketardi (xabar #67). Model qobig'i ichida ushlab turamiz.
  if(homeView){
    const _lim=modelR*0.6+300;
    const _dx=cx-homeView.cx,_dy=cy-homeView.cy,_dz=cz-homeView.cz;
    const _dd=Math.hypot(_dx,_dy,_dz);
    if(_dd>_lim){ const _s=_lim/_dd;
      cx=homeView.cx+_dx*_s; cy=homeView.cy+_dy*_s; cz=homeView.cz+_dz*_s; }
  }
  dist=nd; _camFly=null;
}
function addControls(canvas){
  let lx=0,ly=0,moved=0,pinch0=0,dist0=0,panning=false,pcx=0,pcy=0,_pdBtn=-1;
  let rAdjusting=false,rTouchish=false;   // lineyka: nuqtani lupa bilan aniqlashtirish holati
  let rStartX=0,rStartY=0;   // lineyka: BOSGAN nuqta (aylantirishga o'tishni YO'L UZUNLIGI emas,
                              // shu boshlang'ich nuqtadan NECHOG'LI UZOQLASHGANI bo'yicha aniqlaymiz —
                              // aks holda burchakka mo'ljal olishda mayda tebranishlar yig'ilib
                              // ("yo'l uzunligi") o'rnidan qo'zg'almasdan turib ham chegaradan oshib,
                              // model kutilmaganda aylanib/surilib ketardi)
  const RULER_ADJUST_R=22;   // shu radiusdan UZOQLASHSA — bu AYLANTIRISH ekan, nuqta qo'yilmaydi
  const _r=new THREE.Vector3(),_u=new THREE.Vector3();
  // pan: orbit markazini (cx,cy,cz) kamera o'ng/tepa yo'nalishida suradi (masshtabga mos)
  function panBy(dx,dy){
    _r.setFromMatrixColumn(camera.matrixWorld,0);   // kamera o'ng
    _u.setFromMatrixColumn(camera.matrixWorld,1);   // kamera tepa
    const f=dist*0.0016;
    cx+=(-_r.x*dx+_u.x*dy)*f; cy+=(-_r.y*dx+_u.y*dy)*f; cz+=(-_r.z*dx+_u.z*dy)*f;
  }
  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());   // o'ng tugma = pan (menyu chiqmasin)
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{dragging=true;moved=0;lx=e.clientX;ly=e.clientY;velY=0;velX=0;
    _pdBtn=e.button;
    if(e.button===1)e.preventDefault();   // o'rta tugma — brauzer avto-skroll (o'q) chiqmasin
    panning=(e.button===1||e.button===2||e.shiftKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey);
    // LINEYKA: lupa (kattalashtiruvchi doira) ISHLATILMAYDI — o'lchash 2 DETALNI tanlash
    // orqali bo'ladi, lupa esa bosishni qiyinlashtirardi. Detal oddiy bosishда tanlanadi.
  });
  addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
    if(dragging){
      if(_pdBtn===1&&moved<6){ middleAnim(e.clientX,e.clientY); }   // O'RTA tugma KLIK (surilmagan) -> animatsiya
      else if(!panning&&moved<6){ onTap(e.clientX,e.clientY); }
    }
    hideLoupe();
    dragging=false;panning=false;rAdjusting=false;_pdBtn=-1;
  });
  addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!dragging)return;
    const dx=e.clientX-lx,dy=e.clientY-ly;moved+=Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy);
    if(panning){panBy(dx,dy);}
    else{const ddY=-dx*0.006,ddX=dy*0.006;rotY+=ddY;rotX+=ddX;velY=ddY;velX=ddX;}
    lx=e.clientX;ly=e.clientY;
  });
  canvas.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{e.preventDefault();
    let d=e.deltaY; if(e.deltaMode===1)d*=16; else if(e.deltaMode===2)d*=innerHeight;   // qator/sahifa rejimlari
    d=Math.max(-160,Math.min(160,d));
    zoomAt(e.clientX,e.clientY,Math.exp(d*0.0011));       // sichqoncha notch(100) ≈ 11.6%, trackpad — mayda va silliq
  },{passive:false});
  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{if(e.touches.length===2){pinch0=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY);dist0=dist;dragging=false;velY=0;velX=0;
    pcx=(e.touches[0].clientX+e.touches[1].clientX)/2;pcy=(e.touches[0].clientY+e.touches[1].clientY)/2;}},{passive:true});
  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>{if(e.touches.length===2){
    const dd=Math.hypot(e.touches[0].clientX-e.touches[1].clientX,e.touches[0].clientY-e.touches[1].clientY);
    const mx=(e.touches[0].clientX+e.touches[1].clientX)/2,my=(e.touches[0].clientY+e.touches[1].clientY)/2;
    const nd=dist0*pinch0/Math.max(dd,1);                              // 2 barmoq = zoom (pinch MARKAZIGA)
    if(Math.abs(nd-dist)>1e-6)zoomAt(mx,my,nd/dist);
    panBy(mx-pcx,my-pcy);pcx=mx;pcy=my;                                // + 2 barmoq surish = pan
    e.preventDefault();}},{passive:false});
}
// lineyka uchun: berilgan ekran nuqtasida burchakka(vertex) yopishtirilgan yoki yuza nuqtasi topiladi.
// {point, snapped, face, normal} qaytaradi — face+normal = parallel yuzalar orasidagi masofani
// hisoblash uchun (2 element/yuza tanlanganda), snapped = burchakka "magnit" bo'ldi (nuqta-nuqta rejimi).
function rulerPickAt(x,y){
  if(!raycaster||!scene)return null;
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(x/innerWidth*2-1,-(y/innerHeight)*2+1);
  raycaster.setFromCamera(v,camera);
  // yashirin (skrit) detallarga lineyka ham yopishmasin
  const vis=meshes.filter(m=>m.visible);
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(vis,false).filter(h=>h.object.visible);
  if(hits.length){
    const snap=snapToCorner(x,y,hits.slice(0,4).map(h=>h.object),30);
    if(snap)return{point:snap,snapped:true};
    const hit=hits[0];
    const nWorld=hit.face.normal.clone().transformDirection(hit.object.matrixWorld).normalize();
    return{point:hit.point.clone(),snapped:false,face:true,normal:nWorld};
  }
  const snap=snapToCorner(x,y,vis,26);   // bo'sh joyga bossa ham yaqin burchakka yopishadi
  if(snap)return{point:snap,snapped:true};
  return null;
}
function pick(x,y){
  if(!raycaster||!scene)return;
  if(rulerOn){   // lineyka = 2 DETAL orasini o'lchash: kursor ostidagi detalni tanlaymiz
    const v0=new THREE.Vector2(x/innerWidth*2-1,-(y/innerHeight)*2+1);
    raycaster.setFromCamera(v0,camera);
    const vis=meshes.filter(m=>m.visible);
    const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(vis,false).filter(h=>h.object.visible);
    if(hits.length){ const solid=hits.find(h=>{const mm=_matsOf(h.object)[0];return mm&&(mm.opacity>0.5||!!(h.object.userData&&h.object.userData._glass));}); addRulerDetail((solid||hits[0]).object); }
    return;
  }
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(x/innerWidth*2-1,-(y/innerHeight)*2+1);
  raycaster.setFromCamera(v,camera);
  // three.js r128 raycaster ko'rinmas (visible=false) obyektni ham uradi — SKRIT qilingan
  // detallar tanlanmasin uchun yashiringanlarni chiqarib tashlaymiz
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(meshes,false).filter(h=>h.object.visible);
  // panel tanlovi (shaffofdan solid afzal)
  let panelObj=null, panelDist=Infinity;
  if(hits.length){
    const solid=hits.find(h=>{const mm=_matsOf(h.object)[0];return mm&&(mm.opacity>0.5||!!(h.object.userData&&h.object.userData._glass));});
    const chosen=solid||hits[0];
    panelObj=chosen.object; panelDist=chosen.distance;
  }
  // furnitura tanlovi (blok rejimida emas) — panelning oldida bo'lsa furnitura g'olib
  const fh=(!blockMode)?furnHitAt():null;
  if(fh && fh.dist<=panelDist+1){ selectFurn(fh.fi); return; }
  if(panelObj){ blockMode?selectBlock(panelObj):select(panelObj); }
  else { clearFurnSel(); if(!blockMode)select(null); else clearBlock(); }
}
// TAP: bitta bosish -> tanlash; IKKI marta bosish -> shu detalning animatsiyasini o'ynatish
let _lastTapT=0,_lastTapX=0,_lastTapY=0;
function onTap(x,y){
  const now=performance.now();
  const dbl=(now-_lastTapT<350)&&(Math.abs(x-_lastTapX)<26)&&(Math.abs(y-_lastTapY)<26);
  _lastTapT=dbl?0:now; _lastTapX=x; _lastTapY=y;
  if(dbl){ tapAnim(x,y); return; }   // ikki marta bosildi -> animatsiya (birinchi tap allaqachon tanladi)
  pick(x,y);
}
function tapAnim(x,y){
  if(!raycaster||!scene||rulerOn)return;
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(x/innerWidth*2-1,-(y/innerHeight)*2+1);
  raycaster.setFromCamera(v,camera);
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(meshes,false).filter(h=>h.object.visible);
  if(!hits.length)return;
  const solid=hits.find(h=>{const mm=_matsOf(h.object)[0];return mm&&(mm.opacity>0.5||!!(h.object.userData&&h.object.userData._glass));});
  const obj=(solid||hits[0]).object;
  const aid=obj.userData.anim;
  if(aid!=null&&aid>=0&&ANIMS[aid]){
    const root=ANIMS[aid].root, st=animActive.get(root);
    if(st&&st.ph!=='close'){ closeAnim(root); }            // OCHIQ detalga yana 2-klik -> yopish
    else { playAnim(root); toast(t('dblAnim')); }          // aks holda ochish (10s'da o'zi yopiladi)
  }
  else { toast(t('noAnim')); }
}
// SICHQONCHA O'RTA TUGMASI bosilganda: animatsiya ishlaydi (17s ushlab turadi, keyin o'zi
// yopiladi). Ochiq bo'lsa — yana o'rta tugma bossa ERTA yopiladi (foydalanuvchi o'zi yopadi).
// Kursor ostidagi detalning animatsiyasi bo'lsa — o'shani, bo'lmasa BUTUN modelniki.
function middleAnim(x,y){
  if(rulerOn||!raycaster||!scene)return;
  // KURSOR ostidagi detalni topamiz (shaffofni o'tkazib, ustki solid detal)
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(x/innerWidth*2-1,-(y/innerHeight)*2+1);
  raycaster.setFromCamera(v,camera);
  const hits=raycaster.intersectObjects(meshes,false).filter(h=>h.object.visible);
  const solid=hits.find(h=>{const mm=_matsOf(h.object)[0];return mm&&(mm.opacity>0.5||!!(h.object.userData&&h.object.userData._glass));});
  const obj=(solid||hits[0])&&(solid||hits[0]).object;
  let root=-1;
  if(obj){ const aid=obj.userData.anim; if(aid!=null&&aid>=0&&ANIMS[aid])root=ANIMS[aid].root; }
  if(root<0){ toast(t('noAnim')); return; }   // shu detalда animatsiya yo'q
  const cur=animActive.get(root);
  if(cur && cur.ph!=='close'){ closeAnim(root); }        // shu detal ochiq edi -> yopish
  else { playAnim(root); toast(t('dblAnim')); }    // ochish — boshqalarni yopmaydi (ketma-ket), 10s avto-yopilish
}

// ============ SKRIT (yashirish) + QAYTARISH ============
// eslatma: furnitura (krepyoj) alohida mesh emas — InstancedMesh nusxalari, shu sabab ular
// PANEL emas balki blokning 'gid' kaliti bo'yicha (furnInstances ichidan) topib yashiriladi/qaytariladi.
function hideSelected(){
  const arr = blockSel ? blockSel.slice() : (selected?[selected]:[]);
  if(!arr.length){
    if(selectedFurn){ const fi=selectedFurn; clearFurnSel(); hiddenSet.add(fi); select(null); toast(tp('hidN',{n:hiddenSet.size})); return; }
    toast(t('selFirst'));return;
  }
  const rel=furnForMeshes(arr, !!blockSel);   // detalga PLITA bo'yicha / blokka STRUKTURA bo'yicha tegishli furnitura ham yashirinadi
  for(const m of arr){m.visible=false;hiddenSet.add(m);}
  for(const fi of rel)hiddenSet.add(fi);
  select(null);clearBlock();      // select(null) -> refreshSelVis (furnitura/teshik yangilanadi)
  toast(tp('hidN',{n:hiddenSet.size}));
}
function restoreHidden(){
  if(!hiddenSet.size){return;}
  hiddenSet.clear();
  for(const m of meshes)m.visible=true;
  applyFurnVis();applyHoleVis();applyDimVis();   // razmer/2D ham qaytariladi
  updateHideBtn();
  toast(t('shown'));
}
// IZOLYATSIYA: tanlangan detal/blokka tegishli BO'LMAGAN barcha elementlar (panellar VA
// ularga taluqli bo'lmagan furnitura) yashiriladi — faqat tanlangan qism + uning furniturasi qoladi.
function isolateSelected(){
  const arr = blockSel ? blockSel.slice() : (selected?[selected]:[]);
  if(!arr.length){toast(t('selFirst'));return;}
  const keep=new Set(arr);
  for(const m of meshes){
    if(!keep.has(m) && m.visible){ m.visible=false; hiddenSet.add(m); }
  }
  const rel=furnForMeshes(arr, !!blockSel);   // detal: plita ichidagi furnitura; blok: struktura (gid) bo'yicha
  for(const fi of furnInstances){
    if(!rel.has(fi))hiddenSet.add(fi);
  }
  applyFurnVis();applyHoleVis();applyDimVis();   // blokka tegishli bo'lmagan razmer/2D ham yashirinadi
  updateHideBtn();
  if(explodeOn){ const sl=document.getElementById('explodeSlider');   // izolyatsiya -> rejim BLOK->DETAL
                 setExplodeAmount(sl?+sl.value:0); }
  toast(tp('isoN',{n:arr.length}));
}
// yashir/qaytar tugmasi: holatga qarab ikonka + tooltip o'zgaradi
function updateHideBtn(){
  const b=document.getElementById('btnHideTop'); if(!b)return;
  const selPanel=!!(selected||blockSel);
  const canHide=selPanel||!!selectedFurn;
  b.innerHTML=canHide?_ICON_HIDE:_ICON_SHOW;
  b.title=canHide?t('hideSel'):tp('showN',{n:hiddenSet.size});
  b.classList.toggle('on', !canHide && hiddenSet.size>0);   // qaytarish rejimida belgilanadi
  b.style.opacity=(!canHide && hiddenSet.size===0)?0.45:1;   // qilinadigan ish yo'q
  const bi=document.getElementById('btnIso'); if(bi)bi.style.display=selPanel?'flex':'none';   // izolyatsiya faqat panel/blok uchun
  const bx=document.getElementById('btnXray');
  if(bx){ bx.style.display=selPanel?'flex':'none';
          bx.classList.toggle('off',!xrayOn);
          const xl=document.getElementById('xrayLbl'); if(xl)xl.textContent=t(xrayOn?'xrayOn':'xrayOff'); }
  const bf=document.getElementById('btnFrame'); if(bf)bf.style.display=canHide?'flex':'none';
}
// TANLANGANGA YAQINLASHISH — kamerani tanlangan detal/blokning gabaritiga moslab markazlaydi
// (mAll/fitView butun modelni ko'rsatadi, bu esa faqat tanlangan qismni).
function frameSelection(){
  const arr = blockSel ? blockSel : (selected?[selected]:[]);
  const box=new THREE.Box3();
  if(arr.length){ for(const m of arr)box.expandByObject(m); }
  else if(selectedFurn&&_furnHi){ box.setFromObject(_furnHi); }
  else { toast(t('selFirst'));return; }
  const ctr=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length();
  cx=ctr.x;cy=ctr.y;cz=ctr.z;dist=Math.max(size*1.35,80);
}
// SKANERDA TOPILGAN DETALGA PARVOZ (2026-08-28, foydalanuvchi): katta modelda skaner
// topgan detal QAYERDALIGI ko'rinmasdi. Endi kamera avval BUTUN modelga uzoqlashadi,
// keyin detalga silliq yaqinlashadi — foydalanuvchi detal modelning qayerida ekanini ko'radi.
function flyToMesh(mesh){
  if(!mesh)return;
  const box=new THREE.Box3().expandByObject(mesh);
  const ctr=box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const size=box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length();
  const to={cx:ctr.x,cy:ctr.y,cz:ctr.z,dist:Math.max(size*1.6,220)};
  const cur={cx,cy,cz,dist};
  const home=homeView?{cx:homeView.cx,cy:homeView.cy,cz:homeView.cz,dist:homeView.dist}:cur;
  const segs=[];
  // allaqachon butun model ko'rinib turgan bo'lsa — uzoqlashish bosqichi shart emas
  if(dist < home.dist*0.85) segs.push({from:cur,to:home,dur:600});
  segs.push({from:(segs.length?home:cur),to:to,dur:950});
  _camFly={segs:segs,idx:0,t0:performance.now()};
}

// ============ YOYIB KO'RSATISH (exploded view) ============
// Har bir PANELni model markazidan o'zining markaziga qarab yo'nalishda suradi (slayder = masofa).
// Furnitura (InstancedMesh) hozircha o'z joyida qoladi — faqat panellar strukturasini ko'rsatish uchun yetarli.
// BIR VAQTDA BITTA REJIM (foydalanuvchi 2026-09-03: «yoyib ko'rsatish, o'lchash, detallar
// ro'yxati bir-birining ustiga ochilib qolyapti»): biri yoqilganda qolgan ikkitasi o'zi yopiladi.
function _soloMode(keep){
  if(keep!=='explode'&&explodeOn)toggleExplode();
  if(keep!=='ruler'&&rulerOn)toggleRuler();
  if(keep!=='list'){const l=document.getElementById('list'); if(l&&l.style.display==='flex')l.style.display='none';}
}
function toggleExplode(){
  explodeOn=!explodeOn;
  if(explodeOn)_soloMode('explode');
  const b=document.getElementById('mExplode'); if(b)b.classList.toggle('on',explodeOn);
  const bar=document.getElementById('explodeBar'); if(bar)bar.style.display=explodeOn?'flex':'none';
  if(explodeOn){
    // Rejim: model bloklardan iborat bo'lsa — BLOKLAR bo'yicha (sborka chizmasi kabi),
    // blok tanlangan/izolyatsiya qilingan bo'lsa — DETALLAR bo'yicha.
    toast(t(_explModeNow()==='block'?'explTipBlk':'explodeTip'), false, 4500);
  }else{
    const sl=document.getElementById('explodeSlider'); if(sl)sl.value=0;
    setExplodeAmount(0);
  }
}
// har mesh original markazi + baza matritsasini BIR MARTA (yoyilmagan holatda) saqlaymiz
function _ensureExplodeBase(){
  for(const m of meshes){
    const ud=m.userData;
    if(ud._origCenter)continue;
    m.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    ud._origCenter=new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(m).getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
    ud._origPos=m.position.clone();
    ud._explodeBase=(!m.matrixAutoUpdate)?(ud._abase||m.matrix).clone():null;   // animatsiyali (eshik)
  }
}
// ===== YOYISH REJIMI (2026-08-30, foydalanuvchi: sborkachi uchun) =====
//  • BLOK rejimi — model bir nechta blokdan iborat va hech narsa tanlanmagan/izolyatsiya
//    qilinmagan bo'lsa: har BLOK yaxlit (ichi buzilmasdan) o'z yo'nalishida suriladi va
//    ustiga sborka chizmasidagi kabi RAQAM (balon) qo'yiladi + chapda raqam↔nom jadvali.
//  • DETAL rejimi — blok tanlangan yoki faqat bitta blok ko'rinadigan (izolyatsiya) bo'lsa:
//    har detal alohida suriladi va ustiga chizmadagi kabi OBOZNACHENIYA yoziladi.
let explGroup=null, _explTags=[], _explKey='', _explAmp=0, _explLblMode='des';
let _explLegPick=[];      // legenda qatori -> tanlash funksiyasi (indeks bo'yicha)
let _explLegOpen=null;   // legenda ochiqmi — FOYDALANUVCHI tanlovi (teglar qayta qurilganda yo'qolmaydi)   // yorliq: 'des' | 'name'   // _explAmp — joriy yoyish amplitudasi
                                                             // (KO'RINADIGAN qism o'lchamiga mos: butun model yoki izolyatsiya qilingan blok)
function _explModeNow(){
  if(blockSel||selected)return 'part';
  const ks=new Set();
  for(const m of meshes){ if(m.visible!==false)ks.add(blockKeyOf(m.userData)); }
  return ks.size>=2?'block':'part';
}
function _explUnits(mode){          // yoyiladigan birliklar: blok yoki alohida detal
  _ensureExplodeBase();
  const vis=meshes.filter(m=>m.visible!==false&&m.userData._origCenter);
  if(mode==='part')
    return vis.map(m=>{
      const d=(m.userData.des||'').trim(), nm=(m.userData.name||'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
      // yorliqda OBOZNACHENIYA yoki NOM (tugma bilan almashadi); tanlangani bo'sh bo'lsa — ikkinchisi
      const txt=(_explLblMode==='name'?(nm||d):(d||nm));
      const alt=(_explLblMode==='name'?d:nm);
      return {ms:[m],c:m.userData._origCenter.clone(),txt,alt,
              dim:Math.round(m.userData.w||0)+'×'+Math.round(m.userData.h||0),
              key:(m.userData.des||'')+'|'+(m.userData.name||'')};
    });
  const bm=new Map();
  for(const m of vis){
    const k=blockKeyOf(m.userData);
    let b=bm.get(k);
    if(!b){b={ms:[],box:new THREE.Box3(),txt:blockLabelOf(m.userData),key:k};bm.set(k,b);}
    b.ms.push(m); b.box.expandByPoint(m.userData._origCenter);
  }
  const arr=[...bm.values()];
  for(const b of arr)b.c=b.box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  // sborka chizmasidagi kabi tartib bilan raqamlaymiz: chapdan o'ngga, keyin orqadan oldinga
  arr.sort((a,b)=>a.c.x-b.c.x||a.c.z-b.c.z||b.c.y-a.c.y);
  arr.forEach((b,i)=>{b.num=i+1;});
  return arr;
}
// Yorliq. MATNGA MOSLASHUVCHI kenglik — uzun nom («VM-KRISHA VM6») kesilib qolmasin
// (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30). O'lchami EKRANGA nisbatan doimiy: render() da kamera
// masofasiga qarab yangilanadi — izolyatsiya qilingan kichik blokda ham o'qilarli qoladi.
function makeExplTag(text,balloon){
  const txt=String(text), H=128, F=balloon?(txt.length>2?46:58):46;
  const mc=document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d');
  mc.font='700 '+F+'px system-ui,-apple-system,Arial';
  const tw=Math.ceil(mc.measureText(txt).width);
  const cw=balloon?128:Math.max(128,Math.min(1400,tw+56));
  const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cw;cv.height=H;
  const x=cv.getContext('2d');
  x.font='700 '+F+'px system-ui,-apple-system,Arial';
  if(balloon){
    x.beginPath();x.arc(cw/2,H/2,52,0,Math.PI*2);
    x.fillStyle='#ffffff';x.fill();x.lineWidth=6;x.strokeStyle='#15171c';x.stroke();
  }else{
    x.beginPath();const w=cw-10,hh=72,y0=(H-hh)/2;
    x.roundRect?x.roundRect(5,y0,w,hh,16):x.rect(5,y0,w,hh);
    x.fillStyle='#ffffffee';x.fill();x.lineWidth=4;x.strokeStyle='#15171c';x.stroke();
  }
  x.textAlign='center';x.textBaseline='middle';x.fillStyle='#15171c';
  x.fillText(txt,cw/2,H/2+2);
  const tex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);tex.minFilter=THREE.LinearFilter;tex.anisotropy=4;
  const spr=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,transparent:true}));
  spr.renderOrder=1500;
  spr.userData.asp=cw/H;                       // scale render() da qo'yiladi
  return spr;
}
function _explClearTags(){
  if(explGroup){ model.remove(explGroup);
    explGroup.traverse(o=>{ if(o.material){ if(o.material.map)o.material.map.dispose(); o.material.dispose(); }
                            if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose(); });
    explGroup=null; }
  _explTags=[]; _explKey='';
  const lg=document.getElementById('explLegend'), lb2=document.getElementById('explLegB');
  if(lb2)lb2.innerHTML=''; if(lg)lg.style.display='none';
  const em=document.getElementById('explMode'); if(em)em.textContent='';
}
function _explBuildTags(units,mode){
  const key=mode+'|'+_explLblMode+'|'+units.length+'|'+units.map(u=>u.key).join(',');
  if(key===_explKey)return;
  _explClearTags(); _explKey=key;
  explGroup=new THREE.Group(); model.add(explGroup);
  const sz=Math.max(24,(_explAmp||explodeMax||1000)*0.06);   // leader-chiziq siljishi (yorliq o'lchami EMAS)
  const lm=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x15171c,transparent:true,opacity:0.55,depthTest:false});
  const blkNum=(mode==='block'&&_explLblMode!=='name');   // blokda: raqam yoki nom
  for(const u of units){
    if(!blkNum&&!u.txt)continue;
    const spr=makeExplTag(blkNum?u.num:u.txt,blkNum);
    const ln=new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(),new THREE.Vector3()]),lm);
    ln.renderOrder=1499; ln.frustumCulled=false;
    explGroup.add(spr); explGroup.add(ln);
    _explTags.push({u,spr,ln,sz});
  }
  const em=document.getElementById('explMode');
  if(em)em.textContent=t(mode==='block'?'explBlocks':'explParts');
  const lg=document.getElementById('explLegend'), lgb=document.getElementById('explLegB');
  if(lg&&lgb){
    // ⚠️ Qator KALITI atributga YOZILMAYDI — INDEKS ishlatiladi. Sabab: detal nomlarining
    // 253/396 tasida `\r` bor (Bazis'dan shunday keladi); HTML atributida CR → LF ga
    // normallashadi va `dataset.k` bilan solishtirish HECH QACHON mos kelmasdi — detal
    // rejimida ro'yxatdan detal tanlanmasdi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30). Blok rejimi
    // ishlagan, chunki uning kaliti — sof raqamli gid.
    _explLegPick=[];
    if(mode==='block'){
      lgb.innerHTML=units.map((u,i)=>{ _explLegPick.push(()=>{
          const m=meshes.find(x=>blockKeyOf(x.userData)===u.key); if(m)selectBlock(m); });
        return '<div class="lgi" data-i="'+i+'">'
        +'<span class="lgn">'+u.num+'</span><span class="lgt">'+esc(u.txt||'—')+'</span>'
        +'<span class="lgc">'+u.ms.length+'</span></div>'; }).join('');
      lg.style.display='block';
    }else{
      // DETAL rejimi ro'yxati (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30: «bloklardagi kabi ro'yxat chiqsin»).
      // Bir xil detallar (des+nom+o'lcham) bitta qatorga yig'iladi, soni ko'rsatiladi.
      const uniq=new Map();
      for(const u of units){
        const k=u.txt+'|'+(u.alt||'')+'|'+u.dim;
        if(!uniq.has(k))uniq.set(k,{u,n:0});
        uniq.get(k).n++;
      }
      // Faqat TANLANGAN maydon ko'rsatiladi (des bo'lsa nom kerak emas va aksincha) —
      // foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30: «ozgina joy ochiladi».
      lgb.innerHTML=[...uniq.values()].map((o,i)=>{ const ms=o.u.ms[0];
        _explLegPick.push(()=>{ if(ms&&ms.visible!==false)select(ms); });
        return '<div class="lgi" data-i="'+i+'">'
        +'<span class="lgd">'+esc(o.u.txt||'—')+'</span>'
        +'<span class="lgc" style="margin-left:auto">'+esc(o.u.dim)+(o.n>1?(' \u00d7'+o.n):'')+'</span></div>'; }).join('');
      lg.style.display=uniq.size?'block':'none';
    }
    lgb.querySelectorAll('.lgi').forEach(d=>d.onclick=()=>{
      const fn=_explLegPick[+d.dataset.i]; if(!fn)return;
      lgb.querySelectorAll('.lgi.on').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
      d.classList.add('on'); fn();
    });
    // sarlavha: nechta blok/detal + yig'ish. TELEFONDA yig'iq ochiladi — ro'yxat ekranni
    // to'sib qo'ymasin (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30, iPhone skrinshoti).
    const cnt=lgb.querySelectorAll('.lgi').length;
    const lt=document.getElementById('explLegT');
    if(lt)lt.textContent='\uD83D\uDCCB '+cnt;
    if(_explLegOpen===null)_explLegOpen=(innerWidth>=820);   // telefonda yig'iq, kompyuterda ochiq
    lg.classList.toggle('min', !_explLegOpen);
  }
  const lb=document.getElementById('explLblBtn');
  if(lb){ lb.style.display='block';       // BLOK rejimida ham: raqam <-> nom (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30)
          lb.textContent=t(_explLblMode==='name'?'explByName':(mode==='block'?'explByNum':'explByDes'));
          lb.title=t('explLblTip');
  }
}
function _explPlaceTags(frac,ctr){
  const d=new THREE.Vector3();
  for(const it of _explTags){
    const u=it.u;
    d.copy(u.c).sub(ctr); if(d.lengthSq()<1e-6)d.set(0,1,0); else d.normalize();
    const moved=u.c.clone().add(d.clone().multiplyScalar(frac*_explAmp));
    const tag=moved.clone().add(d.clone().multiplyScalar(it.sz*1.6)).add(new THREE.Vector3(0,it.sz*1.1,0));
    it.spr.position.copy(tag);
    it.ln.geometry.setFromPoints([moved,tag]);
    it.ln.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
  }
}
function setExplodeAmount(v){
  if(!homeView)return;   // hali model yuklanmagan
  _ensureExplodeBase();
  const frac=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,v))/100;
  const mode=_explModeNow();
  // MARKAZ: ko'rinadigan (izolyatsiya qilingan) detallar markazi — blok o'ZIGA NISBATAN
  // yoyiladi. Hech narsa izolyatsiya qilinmagan bo'lsa — butun model markazi.
  const box=new THREE.Box3(); let any=false;
  for(const m of meshes){
    if(m.visible!==false && m.userData._origCenter){ box.expandByPoint(m.userData._origCenter); any=true; }
  }
  const ctr=any?box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3())
               :new THREE.Vector3(homeView.cx,homeView.cy,homeView.cz);
  // Amplituda — KO'RINADIGAN qismning kattaligiga bog'liq: butun modelda katta,
  // izolyatsiya qilingan blokda kichik (aks holda 9 ta detal butun sahna bo'ylab uchib ketardi).
  // yoyish amplitudasi 13% kamaytirildi (foydalanuvchi 2026-08-30) — detallar juda uzoqqa uchmasin
  _explAmp=(any?Math.max(explodeMax*0.12, box.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()).length()*0.5):explodeMax)*0.87;
  const units=_explUnits(mode);
  // panel -> uning birligi (blok yoki o'zi) yo'nalishi
  const uOf=new Map(); const dirs=[];
  units.forEach((u,i)=>{
    const d=u.c.clone().sub(ctr);
    if(d.lengthSq()<1e-6)d.set(0,1,0); else d.normalize();
    dirs.push(d.multiplyScalar(frac*_explAmp));
    for(const m of u.ms)uOf.set(m,i);
  });
  const zero=new THREE.Vector3();
  for(const m of meshes){
    const ud=m.userData;
    const dir=uOf.has(m)?dirs[uOf.get(m)]:zero;      // ko'rinmas detal joyida qoladi
    if(m.matrixAutoUpdate){
      m.position.copy(ud._origPos).add(dir);
    }else{
      // ANIMATSIYALI mesh (fasad/eshik): matrixAutoUpdate=false -> position ishlamaydi,
      // baza matritsasini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri siljitamiz (aks holda joyidan qo'zg'almaydi)
      const m4=(ud._explodeBase||m.matrix).clone();
      m4.elements[12]+=dir.x; m4.elements[13]+=dir.y; m4.elements[14]+=dir.z;
      m.matrix.copy(m4); m.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate=true;
    }
  }
  // FURNITURA — o'z BLOKI bilan birga ketadi (gid bo'yicha), gid'siz bo'lsa eng yaqin birlik
  const fp=new THREE.Vector3();
  const byKey=new Map(); units.forEach((u,i)=>byKey.set(u.key,i));
  for(const fi of (furnInstances||[])){
    fp.setFromMatrixPosition(fi.base);
    let i=(mode==='block'&&fi.gid)?byKey.get(String(fi.gid).split('/')[0]):undefined;
    if(i===undefined){ let best=Infinity;
      units.forEach((u,j)=>{const dd=u.c.distanceToSquared(fp); if(dd<best){best=dd;i=j;}}); }
    const dir=(i===undefined)?zero:dirs[i];
    fi.setExplode(frac>0.0001?{x:dir.x,y:dir.y,z:dir.z}:null);
  }
  // YORLIQLAR (blok raqami / detal oboznacheniyasi)
  if(frac>0.0001){ _explBuildTags(units,mode); _explPlaceTags(frac,ctr); }
  else _explClearTags();
}

// ============ BLOK TANLASH ============
// blockKeyOf — TANLASH uchun NOYOB kalit ('gid' — hisoblagichga asoslangan yo'l, faqat
// nomga emas). Ikkita bir xil nomli blok (masalan 2 ta "Полка") oldin nom bo'yicha
// chalkashib ketardi — endi gid orqali har biri o'z holicha ajraladi.
// blockLabelOf — foydalanuvchiga KO'RSATISH uchun o'qiladigan nom (gid emas, group).
function blockKeyOf(p){
  if(p&&p.gid)return p.gid.split('/')[0]||(p.des)||'?';
  return (groupPath(p)[0]) || (p&&p.des) || '?';   // eski (gid'siz) keshlangan modellar uchun zaxira
}
function blockLabelOf(p){ return (groupPath(p)[0]) || (p&&p.des) || '?'; }
// Blok yo'li. Skript orqali yuklangan modelda yo'l ildizida «Model» bo'g'ini turadi —
// uni tashlamasa HAMMA detal bitta «Model» blokiga tushib qolardi (.b3d yo'lida bunday
// bo'g'in yo'q). Server ham yangi yuklashlarda tozalaydi; bu — eski modellar uchun.
const _ASM_ROOTS=['model','модель','модел','root'];
function groupPath(p){
  const parts=((p&&p.group)||'').split(' / ').map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  while(parts.length>1 && _ASM_ROOTS.indexOf(parts[0].toLowerCase())>=0) parts.shift();
  return parts;
}
function clearBlock(){ blockSel=null; if(!selected)select(null); else{updateHideBtn();refreshSelVis();} }
function selectBlock(mesh){
  const key=blockKeyOf(mesh.userData);
  selectMeshSet(meshes.filter(m=>blockKeyOf(m.userData)===key), blockLabelOf(mesh.userData));
}
// Ixtiyoriy detallar TO'PLAMINI blok kabi tanlaydi (3D'dan blok tanlash ham, detallar
// ro'yxatidagi guruh sarlavhasini bosish ham shu orqali ishlaydi).
function selectMeshSet(set, label){
  clearFurnSel();               // blok tanlansa furnitura tanlovi bekor bo'ladi
  clearSelGhost();              // blokda yengil qatlam QO'YILMAYDI — faqat kontur
  if(!set||!set.length)return;
  selected=null; blockSel=set;
  for(const m of meshes){               // blokni DETAL KABI yoritamiz
    const on=set.indexOf(m)>=0;
    const onThin=on && Math.abs(m.userData.thick||16)<=6;   // yupqa blok detali -> depthWrite=true (slot ikkilanishi yo'q)
    const _own=(m.userData._op!==undefined?m.userData._op:1);
    const _owndw=(m.userData._dw!==undefined?m.userData._dw:true);
    for(const mt of _matsOf(m)){                              // rentgen o'chiq -> oddiy tanlash
      mt.opacity=xrayOn?(on?0.85:0.12):_own; mt.emissive=new THREE.Color(on?0xff7a1a:0x000000);
      mt.emissiveIntensity=on?0.5:0; mt.depthWrite=xrayOn?(onThin?true:false):_owndw;
    }
    if(m.userData._eg){ const em=m.userData._eg.material;      // blokda ham: tanlangan kontur OPAQUE (select() dagi izohga qarang)
      const thru=!!on;                                          // blok konturi ham devor orqali
      em.depthTest=!thru;
      em.color.setHex(thru?0xff6a00:0x000000);
      if(on){ em.transparent=true; em.opacity=1; }
      else  { em.transparent=true;  em.opacity=xrayOn?0.3:0.65; }   // blokdan tashqari detallar ham ochroq kontur bilan ko'rinsin (2026-08-08)
      em.needsUpdate=true; m.userData._eg.renderOrder=on?1001:0; }
    if(m.userData._pkDecals){for(const d of m.userData._pkDecals)d.material.opacity=xrayOn?(on?0.9:0.1):0.9;}
  }
  const inf=document.getElementById('info');
  document.getElementById('iDes').textContent='🧱 '+(label||'');
  document.getElementById('iName').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('iL1').innerHTML=esc(tp('blkN',{n:set.length}));
  document.getElementById('iL2').textContent='';
  document.getElementById('btnDrill').style.display='none';
  let aroot=-1;
  for(const m of set){const aid=m.userData.anim; if(aid!=null&&aid>=0&&ANIMS[aid]){aroot=ANIMS[aid].root;break;}}
  const bA=document.getElementById('btnAnimTop');
  animRoot = aroot>=0 ? aroot : -1;
  if(bA) updateAnimBtn();   // '.on' endi "ochiq" degani (mavjudlik emas) — tugma ko'rinishi o'zi hal qiladi
  inf.style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('hint').style.display='none';
  updateHideBtn();   // blok tanlandi -> yashir tugmasi "yashir" holatiga o'tadi
  refreshSelVis();   // blokka taluqli furnitura + teshiklar ko'rsatiladi
}
function toggleBlockMode(){
  blockMode=!blockMode;
  const b=document.getElementById('btnBlockTop');
  if(b)b.classList.toggle('on',blockMode);
  toast(t(blockMode?'blkOn':'blkOff'));
  if(!blockMode){clearBlock();select(selected);}   // detalga qaytamiz
}

// ============ LINEYKA (o'lchash) ============
// Ikki rejim avtomatik aniqlanadi:
//  · burchakka (vertex) yopishgan 2 nuqta -> oddiy 2-nuqta orasidagi masofa
//  · yuza o'rtasidan tanlangan 2 nuqta (elementning parallel yuzalari) -> ular orasidagi
//    PERPENDIKULYAR masofa (normal bo'yicha proyeksiya) — "2 element orasidagi masofa"
// KARKAS ko'rinishi (lineyka rejimida): barcha panellar deyarli shaffof, faqat qirralar —
// razmer chiziqlari va tanlangan (ko'k) detallar model ORQASIDA ham aniq ko'rinadi.
let rulerFrameOn=false;
function toggleRulerFrame(){
  rulerFrameOn=!rulerFrameOn;
  const btn=document.getElementById('rulerFrame'); if(btn)btn.classList.toggle('on',rulerFrameOn);
  for(const m of meshes){
    const marked=rulerDetails.indexOf(m)>=0;
    for(const mt of _matsOf(m)){
      if(rulerFrameOn){
        if(mt.userData._rfo===undefined){mt.userData._rfo=mt.opacity;mt.userData._rfd=mt.depthWrite;}
        mt.opacity=marked?0.55:0.08; mt.depthWrite=false;
      }else{
        if(mt.userData._rfo!==undefined){mt.opacity=mt.userData._rfo;mt.depthWrite=mt.userData._rfd;
          delete mt.userData._rfo; delete mt.userData._rfd;}
      }
    }
    if(m.userData._eg)m.userData._eg.material.opacity=rulerFrameOn?0.85:0.65;
  }
}
function toggleRuler(){
  rulerOn=!rulerOn;
  if(rulerOn)_soloMode('ruler');
  const b=document.getElementById('mRuler');
  if(b)b.classList.toggle('on',rulerOn);
  const sb=document.getElementById('rulerStop'); if(sb)sb.style.display=rulerOn?'flex':'none';
  const mb=document.getElementById('rulerMode'); if(mb)mb.style.display=rulerOn?'flex':'none';
  const fb=document.getElementById('rulerFrame'); if(fb)fb.style.display=rulerOn?'flex':'none';
  rulerModeLbl();
  if(!rulerOn){ if(rulerFrameOn)toggleRulerFrame(); clearRuler();hideLoupe(); }
  else toast(t('rulerHint'));
}
document.getElementById('rulerFrame').onclick=()=>{toggleRulerFrame();};
function addRulerPoint(r){
  // eslatma: bir nechta o'lchov BIR VAQTDA ekranda turishi mumkin — 3-nuqta qo'yilganda
  // faqat KUTILAYOTGAN juftlik (rulerPts) tozalanadi, EKRANDAGI eski o'lchovlar TURIB QOLADI.
  // Hammasini tozalash faqat "To'xtatish" tugmasi (toggleRuler -> clearRuler) orqali bo'ladi.
  if(rulerPts.length>=2)rulerPts=[];
  rulerPts.push(r);
  if(!rulerGroup){rulerGroup=new THREE.Group();scene.add(rulerGroup);}
  const col=r.snapped?0x3fd17a:0x36c5f0;   // yashil = burchakka yopishdi, ko'k = yuza nuqtasi
  const s=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(dimSpan*(r.snapped?0.008:0.006),12,12),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:col,depthTest:false}));
  s.position.copy(r.point);s.renderOrder=999;rulerGroup.add(s);
  if(rulerPts.length===2)drawRuler(rulerPts[0],rulerPts[1]);
}
// ekran-fazoda eng yaqin burchakni (geometriya cho'qqisini) topadi; radPx piksel ichida bo'lsa qaytaradi
function snapToCorner(sx,sy,objs,radPx){
  let best=null, bestD=radPx*radPx;
  const w=new THREE.Vector3(), s=new THREE.Vector3();
  for(const o of objs){
    if(!o||!o.visible)continue;
    const g=o.geometry, pos=g&&g.attributes&&g.attributes.position;
    if(!pos)continue;
    o.updateMatrixWorld();
    const n=pos.count, step=n>3000?Math.ceil(n/3000):1;   // juda zich geometriyada qadamlab o'tamiz (tezlik)
    for(let i=0;i<n;i+=step){
      w.set(pos.getX(i),pos.getY(i),pos.getZ(i)).applyMatrix4(o.matrixWorld);
      s.copy(w).project(camera);
      if(s.z>1)continue;   // kamera orqasida
      const px=(s.x*0.5+0.5)*innerWidth, py=(-s.y*0.5+0.5)*innerHeight;
      const dd=(px-sx)*(px-sx)+(py-sy)*(py-sy);
      if(dd<bestD){bestD=dd;best=w.clone();}
    }
  }
  return best;
}
function drawRuler(ra,rb){
  const a=ra.point,b=rb.point;
  let bb=b, d, faceMode=false;
  // ikkalasi ham (burchakka yopishmagan) YUZA nuqtasi va normallari deyarli parallel bo'lsa ->
  // "2 element orasidagi masofa" = parallel yuzalar orasidagi perpendikulyar masofa
  if(ra.face&&rb.face&&!ra.snapped&&!rb.snapped&&Math.abs(ra.normal.dot(rb.normal))>0.97){
    faceMode=true;
    const proj=b.clone().sub(a).dot(ra.normal);
    d=Math.abs(proj);
    bb=a.clone().add(ra.normal.clone().multiplyScalar(proj));   // b ning a orqali o'tuvchi normal chiziqdagi proyeksiyasi
  } else {
    d=a.distanceTo(b);
  }
  drawDimSeg(a,bb,d);
}
// ikki WORLD nuqta orasidagi o'lchov chizig'i (strelka + raqam) — nuqta-nuqta ham, 2-detal
// yuzalari orasi ham shuni ishlatadi
function drawDimSeg(a,bb,d){
  if(!rulerGroup){rulerGroup=new THREE.Group();scene.add(rulerGroup);}
  // CAD-uslub razmer: vinoska (chiqarish) chiziqlari MODEL O'LCHAMIga mos uzun bo'ladi va
  // model markazidan TASHQARIga chiqadi — avval ofset masofaga bog'liq (12..60mm) bo'lib,
  // katta mebelда deyarli ko'rinmasdi ("faqat son qo'yilyapti" shikoyati).
  const lineMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x1668e8,depthTest:false});
  const dir=(d>1e-6)?bb.clone().sub(a).normalize():new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);
  const up=Math.abs(dir.y)<0.9?new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0):new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0);
  const perp=new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(dir,up).normalize();
  const mid=a.clone().add(bb).multiplyScalar(0.5);
  const ctr=new THREE.Vector3(homeView.cx,homeView.cy,homeView.cz);
  if(perp.dot(mid.clone().sub(ctr))<0)perp.negate();   // model ichiga emas, tashqariga
  const off=Math.max(50,Math.min((modelR||2000)*0.045,260));
  const a2=a.clone().add(perp.clone().multiplyScalar(off));
  const b2=bb.clone().add(perp.clone().multiplyScalar(off));
  const ovr=perp.clone().multiplyScalar(off*0.12);     // vinoska razmer chizig'idan sal o'tadi
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([a,a2.clone().add(ovr), bb,b2.clone().add(ovr), a2,b2]);
  const ln=new THREE.LineSegments(g,lineMat);
  ln.renderOrder=999;rulerGroup.add(ln);
  const HL=Math.max(8,Math.min(Math.max(d,off)*0.12,22)), HW=Math.max(3,HL*0.35);
  const di=dir.clone().multiplyScalar(Math.min(HL,d>1e-6?d*0.45:HL)), pe=perp.clone().multiplyScalar(HW);
  const ar=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([
    a2,a2.clone().add(di).add(pe), a2,a2.clone().add(di).sub(pe),
    b2,b2.clone().sub(di).add(pe), b2,b2.clone().sub(di).sub(pe)]);
  const arLn=new THREE.LineSegments(ar,lineMat); arLn.renderOrder=999; rulerGroup.add(arLn);
  const dimSize=Math.max(22,Math.min((modelR||2000)*0.028,70));
  const lbl=makeDimLabel(Math.round(d)+' mm',dimSize,false);
  lbl.position.copy(a2.clone().add(b2).multiplyScalar(0.5).add(perp.clone().multiplyScalar(dimSize*0.6)));
  lbl.renderOrder=1000;rulerGroup.add(lbl);
}
// ---- 2-DETAL O'LCHASH: 2 detal tanlanadi -> yuzalari (eng yaqin) orasidagi masofa ----
function _worldBox(m){ const g=m.geometry; if(!g.boundingBox)g.computeBoundingBox(); m.updateMatrixWorld(); return g.boundingBox.clone().applyMatrix4(m.matrixWorld); }
// Lineyka tanlovi: faqat emissive YETARLI EMAS (och yog'ochda qo'shiluvchi ko'k oqarib
// bilinmay qolardi) — yuza RANGINI ham ko'kka bo'yaymiz (tekstura ustidan tint) va asl
// rangni saqlab, bekorда qaytaramiz. Natija: tanlangan detal ANIQ to'q ko'k ko'rinadi.
function _rulerMark(m,on){ for(const mt of _matsOf(m)){
  if(on){ if(mt.userData._rc===undefined)mt.userData._rc=mt.color.getHex();
    mt.color.set(0x2b62c4); mt.emissive=new THREE.Color(0x1a5fff); mt.emissiveIntensity=0.75; }
  else { if(mt.userData._rc!==undefined){ mt.color.set(mt.userData._rc); delete mt.userData._rc; }
    mt.emissive=new THREE.Color(0x000000); mt.emissiveIntensity=0; }
  if(rulerFrameOn)mt.opacity=on?0.55:0.08;   // karkas rejimida tanlangan detal ko'rinib turadi
} }
function clearRulerHi(){ for(const m of rulerDetails)_rulerMark(m,false); rulerDetails=[]; }
function addRulerDetail(mesh){
  if(!mesh)return;
  if(rulerDetails.length>=2)clearRulerHi();        // yangi juftlik boshlanadi (eski o'lchov ekranda qoladi)
  if(rulerDetails.indexOf(mesh)>=0)return;          // bitta detalni ikki marta emas
  rulerDetails.push(mesh); _rulerMark(mesh,true);
  if(rulerDetails.length===2)measureDetails(rulerDetails[0],rulerDetails[1]);
}
// ICHKI (standart): ikki detalning bir-biriga QARAGAN (yaqin) yuzalari orasi — «ichkarisi».
// TASHQI: eng UZOQ (tashqi) yuzalari orasi — ikki detalni qamrab olgan umumiy o'lcham.
function rulerModeLbl(){
  const l=document.getElementById('rulerModeLbl');
  if(l)l.textContent=t(rulerOuter?'rulerOut':'rulerIn');
  const b=document.getElementById('rulerMode');
  if(b)b.classList.toggle('out',rulerOuter);
}
function toggleRulerMode(){
  rulerOuter=!rulerOuter;
  rulerModeLbl();
  toast(t(rulerOuter?'rulerOutMsg':'rulerInMsg'));
  // ikki detal allaqachon tanlangan bo'lsa — o'lchovni darrov qayta chizamiz
  if(rulerDetails.length===2){
    const a=rulerDetails[0], b=rulerDetails[1];
    if(rulerGroup){scene.remove(rulerGroup);rulerGroup.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();});rulerGroup=null;}
    for(const m of rulerDetails)_rulerMark(m,true);
    measureDetails(a,b);
  }
}
function measureDetails(a,b){
  const ba=_worldBox(a), bb=_worldBox(b);
  const pa=new THREE.Vector3(), pb=new THREE.Vector3();
  for(const ax of ['x','y','z']){
    const amin=ba.min[ax],amax=ba.max[ax],bmin=bb.min[ax],bmax=bb.max[ax];
    if(amax<bmin){                                       // A oldinda
      if(rulerOuter){ pa[ax]=amin; pb[ax]=bmax; } else { pa[ax]=amax; pb[ax]=bmin; }
    }else if(bmax<amin){                                 // B oldinda
      if(rulerOuter){ pa[ax]=amax; pb[ax]=bmin; } else { pa[ax]=amin; pb[ax]=bmax; }
    }else{                                               // shu o'q bo'yicha ust-usta
      // IKKALA rejimda ham O'RTA nuqta: o'lchov faqat AJRALISH o'qi bo'ylab bo'ladi va
      // razmer ikki yuzaning O'RTASIga nisbatan yasaladi. (Avval tashqi rejimda bu yerda
      // union chegaralari olinib, natija DIOGANAL bo'lib chiqardi — noto'g'ri edi.)
      const mid=(Math.max(amin,bmin)+Math.min(amax,bmax))/2; pa[ax]=mid; pb[ax]=mid;
    }
  }
  const d=pa.distanceTo(pb);
  drawDimSeg(pa,pb,d);
  toast(Math.round(d)+' mm  ('+t(rulerOuter?'rulerOut':'rulerIn')+')');
}
function clearRuler(){
  if(rulerGroup){scene.remove(rulerGroup);rulerGroup.traverse(o=>{if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();if(o.material)o.material.dispose&&o.material.dispose();});}
  rulerGroup=null;rulerPts=[];
  clearRulerHi();   // tanlangan detallar belgisini olib tashlash
}
// ---- LUPA (kattalashtiruvchi doira) — nuqtani aniqroq tanlash uchun ----
function showLoupe(x,y,touchish){ loupeVisible=true; updateLoupePos(x,y,touchish); document.getElementById('loupe').style.display='block'; }
function hideLoupe(){ loupeVisible=false; document.getElementById('loupe').style.display='none'; }
function updateLoupePos(x,y,touchish){
  const off=touchish?100:46;   // barmoq lupani bekitib qo'ymasligi uchun tepaga suramiz
  loupeX=x; loupeY=y-off;
  const el=document.getElementById('loupe');
  const half=el.clientWidth/2||75;
  el.style.left=(x-half)+'px'; el.style.top=(y-off-half)+'px';
}
function drawLoupe(){
  if(!loupeVisible||!renderer)return;
  const el=document.getElementById('loupe');
  const ctx=el._ctx||(el._ctx=el.getContext('2d'));
  const SIZE=el.width, ZOOM=3, src=SIZE/ZOOM;
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,SIZE,SIZE);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(SIZE/2,SIZE/2,SIZE/2,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.clip();
  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  // renderer.domElement'ning HAQIQIY piksel bufferi CSS piksellardan devicePixelRatio marta katta
  // (retina ekranlarda) — kesib olishdan oldin shu koeffitsientga ko'paytirish shart, aks holda lupa
  // noto'g'ri joydan kesib oladi.
  const pr=renderer.getPixelRatio?renderer.getPixelRatio():1;
  try{ctx.drawImage(renderer.domElement,(loupeX-src/2)*pr,(loupeY-src/2)*pr,src*pr,src*pr,0,0,SIZE,SIZE);}catch(e){}
  ctx.restore();
  const r=rulerPickAt(loupeX,loupeY);
  if(r&&r.snapped&&camera){   // burchakka yopishgan nuqtani ANIQ joyida yashil halqa bilan ko'rsatamiz
    const sv=r.point.clone().project(camera);
    const sx=(sv.x*0.5+0.5)*innerWidth, sy=(-sv.y*0.5+0.5)*innerHeight;
    const zx=SIZE/2+(sx-loupeX)*ZOOM, zy=SIZE/2+(sy-loupeY)*ZOOM;
    ctx.strokeStyle='#3fd17a';ctx.lineWidth=2.5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(zx,zy,9,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.strokeStyle='#ff8a2a';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(SIZE/2-11,SIZE/2);ctx.lineTo(SIZE/2+11,SIZE/2);
  ctx.moveTo(SIZE/2,SIZE/2-11);ctx.lineTo(SIZE/2,SIZE/2+11);ctx.stroke();
}

// ============ VIEWCUBE (3ds Max kabi orientatsiya) ============
function vcFaceTex(txt,main){
  // 256px yuza + yumshoq gradient + nozik ramka — kichik o'lchamda ham keskin, "arzon" emas
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=256;const g=c.getContext('2d');
  const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,256);
  gr.addColorStop(0,main?'#fbfdff':'#eef2f7');gr.addColorStop(1,main?'#e7edf5':'#dde4ec');
  g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,256,256);
  g.strokeStyle='#c2ccd8';g.lineWidth=4;g.strokeRect(2,2,252,252);
  const FF='600 44px -apple-system,system-ui,"Segoe UI",sans-serif';
  g.font=FF;g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';
  const w=g.measureText(txt).width;               // uzun so'z (masalan ЗАДНЯЯ) sig'sin
  if(w>210)g.font=FF.replace('44px',Math.floor(44*210/w)+'px');
  g.fillStyle='#46525f';g.fillText(txt,128,131);
  const tx=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);tx.anisotropy=4;
  return tx;
}
function initViewCube(){
  const cv=document.getElementById('vcube');if(!cv||vcRenderer)return;
  vcRenderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas:cv,antialias:true,alpha:true});
  // buffer 94px * dpr3 — CSS 68/88px ga siqiladi = supersampling (mobилда ham keskin)
  vcRenderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio,3));vcRenderer.setSize(94,94,false);
  vcScene=new THREE.Scene();
  vcCamera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(36,1,0.1,100);
  // Lambert + yorug'lik: har yuza turli ton oladi — hajm/chuqurlik hissi ("yassi arzon" emas)
  vcScene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff,0x8894a2,1.0));
  const dl=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.55);dl.position.set(2,3,4);vcScene.add(dl);
  // material tartibi: +x,-x,+y,-y,+z,-z
  const L=[t('vO'),t('vCh'),t('vT'),t('vP'),t('vOl'),t('vOr')];
  const mats=L.map((s,i)=>new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({map:vcFaceTex(s,i===2||i===4)}));
  vcCube=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.32,1.32,1.32),mats);
  vcCube.add(new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(vcCube.geometry),
    new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x93a2b2,transparent:true,opacity:.55})));
  vcScene.add(vcCube);
  vcRay=new THREE.Raycaster();
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();vcClick(e);});
}
function renderViewCube(){
  if(!vcRenderer||!camera)return;
  const dir=new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(rotX)*Math.sin(rotY),Math.sin(rotX),Math.cos(rotX)*Math.cos(rotY));
  vcCamera.position.copy(dir.multiplyScalar(3.4));vcCamera.up.set(0,1,0);vcCamera.lookAt(0,0,0);
  vcRenderer.render(vcScene,vcCamera);
}
function vcClick(e){
  const cv=document.getElementById('vcube'),r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  const v=new THREE.Vector2(((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width)*2-1,-((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height)*2+1);
  vcRay.setFromCamera(v,vcCamera);
  const hit=vcRay.intersectObject(vcCube,false)[0];if(!hit)return;
  const n=hit.face.normal;
  rotX=Math.asin(Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,n.y)));
  rotY=Math.atan2(n.x,n.z);velX=velY=0;
}
</script>
<script>
if('serviceWorker' in navigator){
  navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(()=>{});
  // yangi versiya chiqsa (yangi SW nazoratni olganda) sahifani avtomatik yangilash
  let _swReloaded=false;
  navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange',()=>{
    if(_swReloaded)return;
    // BIRINCHI TASHRIF: yangi SW nazoratni olishi odatda splash turgan paytga to'g'ri
    // keladi. O'shanda qayta yuklasak, brend ekrani IKKI marta ko'rsatilib, model
    // ~2 barobar kech ochilardi. Sahifalar baribir "avval tarmoq" bilan olinadi
    // (sw.js), ya'ni yangi kod uchun qayta yuklash SHART emas — yangi SW keyingi
    // ochilishda o'zi kuchga kiradi.
    if(document.getElementById('splash'))return;      // brend ekrani ochiq
    if(window.__modelOpen)return;                     // model ochiq — ish buzilmasin
    _swReloaded=true;location.reload();
  });
}
</script>
